|| atha tṛtīyaṃ maṇḍalam ||
vaiśvāmitre tṛtīye¹ pañcānuvākāḥ |
tatra prathame'nuvāke dvādaśa sūktāni |
tatra 'somasya mā' iti trayoviṃśatyṛcaṃ prathamaṃ sūktam |
tathā cānukrāntaṃ - 'somasya tryadhikā' iti |
asya maṇḍaladraṣṭā² viśvāmitra ṛṣiḥ |
anuktatvāt triṣṭup chandaḥ |
'maṇḍalādiṣvāgneyam' iti paribhāṣayāgnirdevatā |
prātaranuvākāśvinaśastrayostaibhye chandasi viniyogaḥ |
tathā ca sūtritaṃ³ - 'somasya mā tavasaṃ patyabhirūpasa iti trīṇi' ( āśva. śrau. 4. 13 ) iti ||
somasya mā tavasaṃ vakṣyagne vāhvaṃ cakarya vidathe yajadhyai |
devā~ acchā dīdyadyuñje adriṃ śamāye agne tanvaṃ juṣasva ||
1 ||
somasya | mā | tavasam | vakṣi | agne | vāhvam | cakarya | vidathe | yajadhyai | devān | acchā | dīdyat | yuñje | adrim | śam'āye | agne | tanvam | juṣasva || 1 ||
he agne tvaṃ vidathe yajñe yajadhyai yaṣṭuṃ somasya vahniṃ voḍhāraṃ yaṃ māṃ cakarya kṛtavānasi taṃ māṃ tavasaṃ tavasvinaṃ balinaṃ vakṣi kāmayasva |
kiṃca he agne devān acchā abhi dīdyat dīpyamāno'ham adriṃ prāvaṇaṃ yuñje abhipavaṇāya yunajmi śamāye śyāmi4 ca |
tathā ca mantrāntaram - 'ṛtena devaḥ savitā śamāyate' ( ṛ. saṃ. 4. 86. 5 ) iti |
yadvā śamāye staumi |
'śaśamāno jarati' iti stutikarmasu pāṭhāt |
tathā ca mantrāntaraṃ - 'so anna iṃje śaśame ca marttaḥ' ( ṛ. saṃ. 6. 1. 9 ) iti |
kiṃca he agne tanvaṃ śarīraṃ juṣasva rakṣāyai sevasva |
yadvā karmāṇi tanvantaṃ māṃ sevasvetyarthaḥ ||
prāñcaṃ yajñaṃ cakruma vardhatāṃ gīḥ samidbhiragniṃ namasā duvasyan |
divaḥ śaśāsurvidathā kavīnāṃ gṛtsāya cittavase gātumīṣuḥ ||
2 ||
prāñcam | yajñam | cakruma | vardhatām | gīḥ | samidbhaḥ | agnim | namasā | duvasyan | divaḥ | śaśāsuḥ | vidathā | kavīnām | gṛtsāya | cit | tavase | gātum | īṣuḥ || 2 ||
he agne vayaṃ yajñaṃ prāñcaṃ prakarṣeṇa gacchantaṃ cakruma akārṣma |
gīḥ madīyā stutiḥ vardhatām edhatām |
agniṃ samidbhiḥ namasā haviṣā ca duvasyan asmadīyāḥ paricareyuḥ |
kiṃca divaḥ dhulokādāgatya devāḥ kavīnāṃ stotṝṇāṃ vidathā jñānāni stotrāṇi vā śaśāsuḥ anvaśāsan |
api ca gṛtsāya |
gṛṇāteridaṃ rūpam |
stotavyāyāgnaye tavase vṛddhāya gātuṃ stotum īṣuḥ |
cit stotāra icchanti ca |
ciditi cāyeṃ ||
1. jha-ma-tṛtīye maṇḍale | 2. ca-ma-maṇḍalasya draṣṭā | 3. ka-kha-jha-ṇa-tha-na-ma-va-sūtram | 4. ka-ca-jha-ṇa-tha-va-śamyāmi ; kha-ṇa-1-śaśyāmi | ṛ. 2-23
mayo dadhe medhiraḥ pūtadakṣo divaḥ subandhurjanuṣā pṛthivyāḥ | arivandan darśatamapsu antardevāso agnimāpasi svasṛṇām || 3 || mayeḥ | dadhe | medhiraḥ | pūtadakṣaḥ | divaḥ | subandhuḥ | januṣā | pṛthivyāḥ | arivandan | oṃ iti | darśatama | apsu | antaḥ | devāsaḥ | agnim | apasi | svasṛṇām ||3||
ᵛmedhiraḥ medhāvān prājñaḥ ᵛpūtadakṣaḥ śuddhabalaḥ ᵛjanuṣā janmamātreṇa ᵛsubandhuḥ śobhanabandhuḥ |
svata eva bandhuriti yāvat |
yo'gniḥ ᵛdivaḥ dyulokasya ᵛpṛthivyāḥ bhūmeśca ᵛmayaḥ sukhaṃ ᵛdadhe vidadhāti |
devānāṃ manuṣyāṇāṃ ca sukhaṃ karotīti yāvat |
taṃ ᵛdarśataṃ darśanīyam ᵛagniṃ ᵛdevāsaḥ devāḥ ᵛsvasṛṇāṃ saraṇaśīlānāṃ nadīnām ᵛapsvantaḥ gūḍhaṃ sthitam ᵛapasi yajñavahanarūpe' karmaṇi nimitte |
śodhayan śociḥ dīptiṃ vastrasthānīyaṃ pari paritaḥ vasānaḥ ācchādayan apām apasyatāṃ karmavatām āyuḥ annaṃ bṛhatīḥ prabhūtāḥ anūnāḥ sampūrṇāḥ śriyaḥ saṃpadaśca mimīte karoti ||
13 ||
vrājā sīmanadatīrarbadhā divo yadvīravasanā anagnāḥ |
sanā atra yuvatayaḥ sayonīrekaṃ garbhaṃ dadhire sapta vāṇīḥ ||
6 ||
vrājaṃ | sīm | anadatīḥ | adabdhāḥ | divaḥ | yadvīḥ | avasānāḥ | anagnāḥ | sanāḥ | atra | yuvatayaḥ | sayonīḥ | ekam | garbham | dadhire | sapta | vāṇīḥ || 6 ||
anadatīḥ abhakṣayantīḥ adabdhāḥ ahiṃsitā mātṛbhūtā apo'gniḥ sau sarvataḥ vavrāja vrajati |
abhirapāṃ śoṣakaḥ taṃ cāpaḥ śamayanti tadubhayamiha nāstītyuktaṃ bhavati |
kīdṛśyastāḥ |
divo yadvīḥ antarikṣasyāpatyabhūtāḥ avasānāḥ vastramācchādayantyaḥ |
vatrasthānīyasya jalasya vidyamānatvāt |
ata eva anagnāḥ |
kiṃca tā api sanāḥ sanātanyaḥ yuvatayaḥ nityataruṇyaḥ |
nityavṛddhā ityarthaḥ |
sayonīḥ samānamantarikṣaṃ sthānaṃ yāmāṃ tāḥ sapta vāṇīḥ nadyaḥ ekam agniṃ garbha garbhasthaṃ dadhire dadhate ||
stīrṇā asya saṃhatau viśvarūpā ghṛtasya yonau sravathe madhūnām |
asthuratra dhenavaḥ pinvamānā mahī dasmasya mātaraṃ samīcī ||
7 ||
stīrṇāḥ | asya | sam'hataḥ | viśva'rūpāḥ | ghṛtasya | yonau | sravathe | madhūnām | asthuḥ | atra | dhenavaḥ | pinvamānāḥ | mahī iti | dasmasya | mātaraṃ | samīcī iti sam'īcī ||7||
ghṛtasya udakasya yonau sthāne'ntarikṣe saṃhataḥ puñjībhūtāḥ |
hagateḥ kvip |
viśvarūpāḥ nānārūpāḥ¹ stīrṇāḥ sarvatra prasṛtāḥ asya agneḥ raśmayaḥ madhūnām udakānāṃ sravathe sravaṇe sati asthuḥ tiṣṭhanti |
pinvamānāḥ pūryamāṇāḥ atra agnau āpaḥ dhenavaḥ sarveṣāṃ prīṇayitavyo'bhavan |
samīcī samañjanyau² śobhamāne mahī mahatyau dyāvāpṛthivyau dasmasya darśanīyasya agneḥ³ mātarā mātarau bhavataḥ ||
babhrāṇaḥ sūno sahaso vyadyaurdhānaḥ śukrā rabhasā vapūṃṣi śrotanti dhārā madhuno ghṛtasya vṛṣā yatra vāvṛdhe kāvyena ||
8 ||
babhrāṇaḥ | sūno iti | sahasaḥ | vi | adyaut | dadhānaḥ | śukrā | rabhasā | vapūṃṣi | śrotanti | dhārāḥ | madhunaḥ | ghṛtasya | vṛṣā | yatra | vavṛdhe | kāvyena || 8 ||
1. ma-va-nānāvidharūpāḥ |
2. kha-ga-ga-ta-tha-samañcatyau |
3. jha-ta-tha-na-ma-va-yasyāmeḥ |
he ᵥsahasaḥ ᵥsūno sahasasputra ᵥbabhrāṇaḥ ᵥsarvedhāyamāṇastvaṃ ᵥśukrā śukrāṇi bhāsvarāṇi ᵥrabhasā vegavanti ᵥvapūṃṣi arcīṣi ᵥdadhānaḥ bhārayastvaṃ ᵥvyadyaut dyotate |
ᵥvṛṣā agniḥ ᵥyatra yasmin‌yajamāne ᵥkāvyena stotreṇa ᵥvavṛdhe vṛddhiṃ prāpnoti tadānīṃ ᵥmadhunaḥ atyantaṃ madhurasya ᵥghṛtasya udakasya ᵥdhārāḥ ᵥśrotranti sravante |
yadā yajamānastotreṇāmisṛṣṭo bhavati tadānīṃ vṛṣṭirbhavatītyarthaḥ ||
pituścid‌dharjanuṣā viveda vyaṃsya dhārā asṛjadvi dhenāḥ |
guhā carantaṃ sakhibhiḥ śivebhirdivo yahvībhirna guhā babhūva ||
9 ||
pituḥ | cit | ūdhaḥ | ᵥjanuṣā | viveda | vi | asya | dhārāḥ | asṛjat | vi | dhenāḥ | guhā | carantam | sakhibhiḥ | śivebhiḥ | divaḥ | yahvībhiḥ | na | guhā | babhūva || 9 ||
ᵥpituḥ antarikṣasya ᵥūdhaḥ ūdhaḥsthānīyaṃ jalapradeśamayamagniḥ ᵥjanuṣā svayameva ᵥviveda jānāti |
kiṃca ayamagniḥ ᵥasya ūdhasaḥ saṃbandhinyaḥ ᵥdhārāḥ payodhārāḥ ᵥvi ᵥasṛjat |
ᵥdhenāḥ mādhyamika vācaśca visṛjati |
ᵥśivebhiḥ śivaṃkareḥ ᵥsakhibhiḥ ātmanaḥ sakhibhūtairvāyubhiḥ ᵥdivaḥ ᵥyahvībhiḥ antarikṣasyāpatyabhūtābhiradbhiḥ saha ᵥguhā guhāyāṃ ᵥcarantaṃ vartamānamenarmāgniṃ ᵥguhā ᵥcit guhāyāṃ sthitaḥ ko'pi ᵥna babhūva prāptuṃ samartho nābhavat ||
pituśca garbhaṃ janituśca babhre pūrvīreko adhayatpīpyānāḥ |
vṛṣṇe sapatnī śucaye sabandhū ubhe asmai manuṣye ni pāhi ||
10 ||
pituḥ | ca | garbham | janituḥ | ca | babhre | pūrvīḥ | ekaḥ | adhayat | pīpyānāḥ | vṛṣṇe | sapatnī iti sa'patnī | śucaye | sabandhū iti sa'bandhū | ubhe iti | asmai | manuṣye iti | ni | pāhi || 10 ||
ayamagniḥ ᵥpituḥ ᵥca antarikṣasya ᵥgarbhaṃ vṛṣṭidvārā garbhabhūtaṃ ᵥjanituḥ sarvasya lokasya jana- yituḥ brahmaṇaḥ 2 ᵥca lokamopadhyādikaṃ ᵥbabhre bibharti |
ᵥekaḥ evāgniḥ 3 ᵥpūrvīḥ bahvīḥ ᵥpīpyānāḥ pyāyamānā vṛddhiṃ prāptā opadhīḥ ᵥadhayat dhayati bhakṣayati |
ᵥsapatnī samāna ekaḥ sūryaḥ patiryoste ᵥmanuṣye manuṣyebhyo hite ᵥubhe dyāvāpṛthivyau ᵥvṛṣṇe varṣiṃtre ᵥasmai ᵥśucaye agnaye ᵥsabandhū bhavataḥ |
anayorabhiriva bandhurityarthaḥ |

ityarthaḥ |
ᵥdvitā dvidhā gārhapatyāhavanīyarūpābhyāṃ yuktaṃ ᵥhotāraṃ devānāmāhvātāramagniṃ ᵥmanuṣaḥ manuṣyāḥ ᵥvāvataḥ ᵥca ṛtvijaśca ᵥdhiyā karmaṇā ᵥkuliśaḥ vāsyādiḥ ᵥrathaṃ ᵥna rathamiva ᵥsamaṇvati saṃskurvanti ||
sa raucayajjanuṣā rodasī ubhe sa mātrorabhavatputra īḍyaḥ |
havyavāḻbhirajarannohito dūlabho viśāmatithirvibhāvasuḥ ||
2 ||
saḥ | rocayat | januṣā | rodasī iti | ubhe iti | saḥ | mātroḥ | abhavat | putraḥ | īḍyaḥ | havya'vāṭ | agniḥ | ajaraḥ | carnaḥ'hitaḥ | duḥ'dabhaḥ | viśām | atithiḥ | vibhāvasuḥ ||2||
ᵥsaḥ agniḥ ᵥjanuṣā janmanā ᵥubhe ᵥrodasī dyāvāpṛthivyau ᵥrocayat arocayat prakāśayati |
ᵥsa ᵥmātroḥ dyāvāpṛthivyoḥ ᵥīḍyaḥ stutyaḥ ᵥputraḥ ᵥabhavat |
ᵥhavyavāṭ havyānāṃ voḍhā ᵥagniḥ aṅgādiguṇayuktaḥ ᵥajaraḥ jarārahitaḥ |
ᵥcano'dhaṃ hitaṃ yajamāne nihitaṃ yenāsau ᵥcanohitaḥ ᵥdūlabhaḥ hiṃsitumaśakyaḥ |
ᵥso'yaṃ ᵥvibhāvasuḥ prabhādhanaḥ ᵥviśāṃ manuṣyāṇām ᵥatithiḥ iva pūjyo bhavati ||
kratvā dakṣasya taruṣā vidharmaṇi devāsoṃ agniṃ janayanta cittibhiḥ |
rurucānaṃ bhānunā jyotiṣā mahāmatyaṃ na vājaṃ saniṣyannupa bruve ||
3 ||
kratvā | dakṣasya | taruṣā | vi'dharmaṇi | devāsaḥ | agnim | janayanta | citti'bhiḥ | rurucānam | bhānunā | jyotiṣā | mahām | atyam | na | vājam | saniṣyan | upa | bruve ||3||
ᵥcittibhiḥ jñānairupalakṣitāḥ ᵥdevāsaḥ devāḥ ᵥtaruṣā vyasanebhyastārakasya ᵥdakṣasya balasya ᵥkratvā karmaṇā ᵥvidharmaṇi vidhārake yajñe ᵥagniṃ janayanta ajanayan |
ᵥbhānunā bhāsamānena ᵥjyotiṣā ᵥrurucānaṃ rocamānaṃ ᵥmahāṃ mahāntamagnim ᵥatyaṃ ᵥna bhārasahamaśvamiva ᵥvājam annaṃ ᵥsaniṣyan lipsamāno'ham ᵥupa ᵥbruve staumi ||
ā mandrasya saniṣyanto vareṇyaṃ vṛṇīmahe ahyaṃ vājamṛmiyam |
rātri bhṛgūṇāmuśijaṃ kavikratumagniṃ rājantaṃ divyena śociṣā ||
4 ||
ā | mandrasya | saniṣyantaḥ | vareṇyam | vṛṇīmahe | ahyam | vājam | ṛmiyam | rātrim | bhṛgūṇām | uśijam | kavi'kratum | agnim | rājantam | divyena | śociṣā ||4||

1. kha-ga-ca-kṣa-ṇa-ṇa-na-va-jarādirahitaḥ |

sa jinvate jaṭhareṣu prajāñjinvānvāṃ citreṣu nānadan miṃha: | vaiśvānara: pṛthupājā amartyo vasu ratnā daryamāno tri dāśuṣe || 11 || sa: | jinvate | jaṭhareṣu | prajāñjinvān | vṛpā | citreṣu | nānadat | na | siṃha: | vaiśvānara: | pṛthu'pājā: | amartya: | vasu | ratnā | daryamāna: | vi | dāśuṣe || 11 ||

'sumanmabhi: stotṛbhi: 'bhandamāna: stūyamāna: 'vaiśvānara: jātamātra eva 'pratnathā pratna iva cirantana iva 'nākam |

'diva: antarikṣasya 'pṛṣṭham uparinapradeśaṃ svarohadbhi: 'āruhat ārohati |
'sa: vaiśvānara: 'pūrvavat pūrvebhya: ṛṣibhya iva 'jantave stotre yajamānāya 'dhanaṃ paścādilakṣaṇaṃ 'janayan saṃpādayan 'jāgṛvi: sadā prabuddha: 'samānaṃ sarveṣāṃ devānāṃ sādhāraṇam 'ajam ākāśamārgaṃ 'paryaiti sūryarūpeṇa gacchati ||
ṛtāvānaṃ yajñiyaṃ vipramukhyaṃ māṃ dudhe māturiśvā divi kṣayam | taṃ citrayāṃ hariṃkeśamīmahe sudītimimaṃ suvitāya narvyase || 13 || ṛta'vānam | yajñiyam | vipram | ukthyam | ā | yam | dudhe | māturiśvā | divi | kṣayam | tam | citra'yāmam | hari'keśam | īmahe | su'dītim | agnim | suvitāya | narvyase || 13 ||

^ṛtāvānaṃ balavantaṃ ^yajñiyaṃ yajñāṃ ^vipraṃ medhāvinam ^ukthyaṃ stutyaṃ ^divi dyuloke ^kṣayaṃ nivasantaṃ ^yam agniṃ ^mātariśvā vāyurdyulokādāhṛtya pṛthivyām ^ā ^dadhe āhitavān |
^citrayaṃ nānāvidhagamanaṃ ^harikeśaṃ piṅgalācipaṃ ^sudītaṃ śobhanadīptiṃ ^tam ^agniṃ ^nadhyase navatarāya ^suvitāya suṣṭhu prāptavyāya dhanāya tadartham ^īmahe yācāmahe ||
śuciṃ na yārmanviṣpiṃ svarhaśaṃ ketuṃ divo raucanasthāṃmuparbudham | agniṃ mūrdhānaṃ divo apratiṣkṛtaṃ tamīmahe narmasā vājinaṃ bṛhat || 14 || śucim | na | yārman | iṣpim | svaḥ'dṛśam | ketum | divaḥ | rocana'sthām | upaḥ'budham | agnim | mūrdhānam | divaḥ | aprati'skṛtam | tam | īmahe | narmasā | vājinam | bṛhat || 14||
^na iti saṃpratyarthe |
^śuciṃ dīptaṃ ^yāmana yāmani yajñe ^iṣpim eṣaṇīyaṃ ^svarhaśaṃ sarvadṛśaṃ sarvapadārthavijñānayuktaṃ ^divaḥ dyulokasya ^ketuṃ prajñāpakaṃ ^rocanasthāṃ rocanastham |

mandraṃ hotāraṃ śucimdvayāvinaṃ dmūnasamukthyaṃ viśvarṣaṇim | rathaṃ na citraṃ vapuṣāya darśataṃ manurhitaṃ sadamidrāya īmahe || 15 || mandram | hotāram | śucim | advayāvinam | dmūnasam | ukthyam | viśva'rṣaṇim | ratham | na | citram | vapuṣāya | darśatama | manuḥ'hitam | sadam | it | rāyaḥ | īmahe ||15||
^mandraṃ stutyaṃ ^hotāraṃ devānāmāhvātāraṃ ^śuciṃ sarvadā śuddham ^advayāvinaṃ dvayahīnam^2 akuṭilaṃ ^dmūnasaṃ dānamanasam ^ukthyaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ ^viśvarṣaṇiṃ viśvasya draṣṭāraṃ ^citraṃ nānāvidhavarṇaṃ ^rathaṃ ^na rathamiva ^vapuṣāya rūpārthaṃ ^darśataṃ darśanīyaṃ ^sadamit sadaiva ^manurhitaṃ sarvebhyo manuṣyebhyo hitaṃ tamagni ^rāyaḥ dhanāni ^īmahe yācāmahe |

vaiśvānarāya pṛthupārjase vipo ratnā vidhanta dhuruṇeṣu gātave | agniṃ devā~ amṛtau duvasyathā dharmāṇi sanatā na dūduṣat || 1 || vaiśvānarāya | pṛthupārjase | vipaḥ | ratnā | vidhanta | dhuruṇeṣu | gātave | agniḥ | hi | devān | amṛtaḥ | duvasyati | artha | dharmāṇi | sanatā | na | dūduṣat || 1 || pavipaḥ medhāvinaḥ stotāraḥ papṛthupārjase bahuprajñāya vahnāya vā | pājaḥśabdasya balānnārthāḥ pāṭhāt | pavaiśvānarāya agnaye padhuruṇeṣu dharmeṣu yajñeṣu paratnā ramaṇīyāni stotrāṇi pagātave sanmārga- lābhāya pavidhanta paricaranti kurvanti | paamṛtaḥ maraṇarahitaḥ paagniḥ padevān paduvasyati haviṣāṃ pradānena paricarati | hiḥ prasiddhā | paatha ataḥ kāraṇāt ko'pi pasanatā sanātanāni padharmāṇi yajñān pa na padūduṣat na dūṣayati ||
antardūtau rodasī dasma īyate hotā niṣatto manuṣaḥ purohitaḥ | kṣayaṃ bṛhantaṃ pari bhūṣati yubhirdevairbhiragiririṣito dhiyāvasuḥ || 2 || antaḥ | dūtaḥ | rodasī iti | dasmaḥ | īyate | hotā | ni'sattaḥ | manuṣaḥ | puraḥ'hitaḥ | kṣayam | bṛhantam | pari | bhūṣati | yubhiḥ | devaiḥ | agniḥ | iṣitaḥ | dhiyā'vasuḥ ||2|| padasmaḥ darśanīyaḥ pahotā agniḥ padūtaḥ devānāṃ dūtaḥ san parodasī rodasyorvāpṛthivyoḥ paantaḥ madhye paīyate gacchati | pamanuṣaḥ manojātasya manuṣyasya papurohitaḥ āhavanīyādirūpeṇa purataḥ sthāpitaḥ paniṣattaḥ niṣaṇṇaḥ san paagniḥ padyubhiḥ dīptibhiḥ pabṛhantaṃ mahāntaṃ pakṣayaṃ yajñagṛhaṃ pari pabhūṣati alaṃkurute | padevebhiḥ devaiḥ paiṣitaḥ preritaḥ padhiyāvasuḥ prajñā' vyākhyātaḥ | ete dve agniviśeṣaṇe ||

ᐁuśāna: kāmayamāna: ᐁagni: ᐁnṛtavannaṃ dīsīmantaṃ ᐁpṛthupragāṇaṃ pṛthugatim ᐁuśantaṃ kāmayamānaṃ ᐁyoniṃ sthānamāyatanam ᐁā ᐁasthān ātiṣṭhati |

‘śitippṛṣṭhasya’ nīlapṛṣṭhasya ‘dhāseḥ’ sarvasya dhārayituragneḥ ‘ye raśmayaḥ’ ‘pra’ ‘āḥ’ prakarṣeṇocchranti te raśmayaḥ ‘mātarā mātarau dyāvāpṛthivyau ‘ā’ ‘viviśuḥ’ sarvataḥ praviṣṭāḥ |
‘sapta’ sarpaṇasvabhāvāḥ ‘vāṇīḥ’ vanīyā nadīścāviviśuḥ |
‘pariṣkṛtā’ parito vartamāne ‘pitarā pitarau dyāvāpṛthivyau tenāgninā ‘saṃ ‘carate samyak vyavaharataḥ |
tathā ‘prayakṣe’ prakarṣeṇa ‘yaṣṭuṃ ‘dīrghamāyuḥ agneścaraṃ jīvanaṃ prabhūtamanaṃ vā ‘pra ‘srāte prasārayataḥ |
saṃpādayata ityarthaḥ |
yadvā dyāvāpṛthivyau pratyakṣe prakarṣeṇa satataṃ yaṣṭuṃ yajamānasya dīrghamāyuḥ saṃpādayataḥ ||

1 draviṇaḥ |
dravati satataṃ gacchatīti draviṇaḥ śabdena' agnirabhidhīyate |
draviṇaḥ satatagamanasvabhāva he agne 2 pukṣaprayajaḥ |

7śukrā śukrāṇi dīptāni raśanākhyāni 7vāsānti 7vasānāḥ ācchādayantaḥ 7svaravaḥ svarumantaḥ 7śreṇiśaḥ paṅktiśaḥ 7yatānāḥ pravartamānāḥ |

7haṃsāiva iti |

1. kha-ga-gha-ca-na-pa-bha-saṃmatāḥ | 2. ca-tatsaṃtaniyogena | 3. ga-ca-bha-la-kṣamā | 4. na-la- ‘raśanākhyāni’ nāsti | 5. la-āhavanīyasya pūrvabhāge |

ṛgveda:
( niru. 6. 7 ) iti yāska: |

śṛṅgāṇi'iva | it | śṛṅgāṇām | sam | dadṛśe | capāla'vantaḥ | svarava: | pṛthivyām | vāghat'bhi: | vā | vi'have | śropamāṇā: | asmān | avantu | pṛtanā'jyeṣu || 10 ||
‘svarava: svaravantaḥ capālavantaḥ pratimuktakaṭakā: |
’ evaṃvidhā yūpā: ‘pṛthivyāṃ śṛṅgāṇāṃ mahipādīnāṃ śṛṅgāṇīveva vipāṇāānīva saṃ dadṛśe samyak dṛśyante eva |
‘vā api ca vihave |
vividhaṃ hūyante havīṃṣyatreti vihavo yajña: |
tasmin yajñe ‘vāghadbhi: ṛtvigbhi: |

ṛtvijo yajamānaḥ prerayati | he ṛtvijaḥ ‘pāvakaśociṣaṃ śodhakadīptiṃ śīraṃ kāṣṭheṣvoṣadhīṣu cānuśāyinaṃ svadhvaraṃ śobhanayajñopetamimamagnim ā juhota āhutibhiḥ ā samantātparyata | api ca āśuṃ karmaṇi vyāptaṃ dūtam āhvānakāritvena devānāṃ dūtam ajiraṃ kṣipraṃ gantāraṃ pratnaṃ purātanam īḍyaṃ stotavyaṃ devaṃ dyotamānamimamagnim śruṣṭī saparyata | ‘śruṣṭīti kṣipranāma’ iti yāskaḥ | kṣipraṃ pūjayata || juhota | ‘hu dānādanayoḥ’ | asmādātorloṭ | ‘juhotyādibhyaḥ pluḥ’ iti śapaḥ pluḥ | ‘śluḥ’ iti dvivacanam | ‘loṭo laṅvat’ iti laṅabhāve ‘tasthasthamipām’ iti thasya tādeśaḥ | ‘chandasi’ ityanuvṛttau tasya ‘tasanasanathanāśca’ iti tabādeśaḥ | viśvādruṇaḥ | nighātaḥ | svadhvaram | bahuvrīhau ‘nansubhyām’ ityuttarapadāntodāttatvam | śīram | ‘śīṅ svapre’ | asmāddhātoḥ ‘sphāyitañcivañca’ ityādinā ravapratyayaḥ | kivādruṇābhāvaḥ | prāyayasvaraḥ | pāvakaśociṣamityatra pavanaṃ pāvaḥ śuddhiḥ | taṃ kāyatīti pāvakaḥ | ‘āto'nupasarge kaḥ’ | pratyayasvaraḥ | ‘bahuvrīhau prakṛtyā pūrvapadam’ iti pūrvapadaprakṛtisvaratvam | āśum | ‘aśu vyāptau’ asmāddhātoḥ ‘kṛvāpājimi’ ityādinā uṇ | upadhāvṛddhiḥ | pratyayasvaraḥ | ajiram | ‘aja gatikṣepaṇayoḥ’ ityasmāddhātoḥ ‘ajiraśiśaraśiśithala’ iti kiracpratyayāntatvena nipātanādantodāttatvam | pratnam | ‘pragasya chandasi galopaśca’ (kā. 4. 3. 23. 2) iti galopaḥ | cakārān tanpratyayaḥ | pratyayasvaraḥ | īḍyam | ‘īḍa stutau’ | asmānṇyat | ‘īḍavanda’ ityādināyudāttatvam | saparyata | saparaśabdāt ‘kaṇḍvādibhyo yak’ pūjāyāṃ vartate | loṇmadhyamapuruṣabahuvacanam | śap | ‘ato guṇe’ iti pūrvarūpatvam | nighātaḥ ||
trīṇi śatā trī sahasrāṇyagniṃ triṃśacca devā nava cāsaparyan |
aukṣanghṛtairastvaṇabarhirasmā ādiddhotāraṃ nyasādayanta ||

1. kha-ga-ca-ja-ṇa-na-bha-ajapratyayaḥ |
2. kha-ga-ca-ja-ṇa-na-bha-‘asmāṇṇyat’ nāsti |

na: pāvaka asmatsambandhina: pāpasya śoṣaka he agne sa: pūrvamantre pratipāditastvaṃ dyumat kāntiyuktaṃ suvīryaṃ śobhanasāmarthyapetaṃ dānabhogāhaṃ dhanam asme asmabhyaṃ dīdihi | yadvā dyumattajo- yuktaṃ suvīryaṃ śobhanavīryapetaṃ ca yathā bhavati tathāsme asmadarthaṃ tvaṃ dīdihi dīpyasva | kiṃca stotṛbhya: | paṣṭhyarthe caturthī | tvadviṣayastutikāriṇāṃ hotrādīnāṃ svastaye avināśanāya kṣemāya anta: antikatama: teṣāmatiśayena samīpavartī bhava || pāvaka | 'pūṣ pavane' | asmāddhātorvulu | ṇittvāddhṛddhi: | vorakādeśa: | āmantritatvādāṣṭamikamanudāttatvam | dīdihi | divu krīḍādau | asmādyaṅ | 'tujādīnāṃ0' ityabhyāsadīrghatvam | 'saṃjñāpūrvako vidhiranitya: ' ityabhyāsasya guṇābhāva: | 'bahulaṃ chandasi' iti yaṅo luk | loṭi mip | 'carkarītaṃ parasmaipadamādivacca' iti vacanācchapo luk | sipo hirādeśa: | 'lopo vyorvali' iti valopa: | herapittvāt aguṇa: | nighāta: | dyumat | 'diva ut' ityukāra: | 'hasvatuḍbhyām0' iti matupa udāttatvam | suvīryam | 'vīravīryau ca' ityuttarapadādyudāttatvam | anta: | anto'syāstītyantika: | 'ata iniṭhanau' iti ṭhan | atiśayenāntika iti 'atiśāyane tamap' iti tamap | 'tame tādeśa' iti tādilopa: | nittvādā- dyudātta: | svastaye | prātipadikasvara: ||

ṛgvedaḥ
taṃ tvā viprā vipanvavo jāgṛvāṃsaḥ samindhate |
havyavāhaṃmartyaṃ sahovṛdham ||

tam | tvā | viprāḥ | vipanvavaḥ | jāgṛ'vāṃsaḥ | sam | indhate | havya'vāham | amartyam | sahaḥ'vṛdham || 9 ||
he agne 'viprāḥ stutīnāṃ prerakāḥ 'jāgṛvāṃsaḥ agnihotrādikarmaṇyapramattatayā prabuddhāḥ 'vipanvavaḥ | vipanvaśabdo medhāvinamācaṣṭe | 'vipaśrit vipanvaḥ' ( ni. 3. 15. 17 ) iti tannāmasu pāṭhāt | vipanvavo medhāvinaḥ stotāraḥ 'havyavāhaṃ havyānāṃ voḍhāram 'amartyaṃ maraṇa- dharmarahitaṃ 'sahovṛdhaṃ sahasā mathanarūpeṇa balena vardhamānaṃ tādṛśaṃ 'tvā tvāṃ 'samindhate somājyādyāhutibhiḥ samyagdīpayanti || viprāḥ | 'ḍuvap bījasantāne' | asmāddhātoḥ 'ṛjendrāpravajravipra' iti ranpratyaye itvam | nivādādyudāttatvam | vipanvavaḥ | vividhaṃ panaṃ panane stutiṃ yātīti vipanvyuḥ | 'mṛgavādayaśca' iti kuḥ | akāralopaścāndasaḥ | pratyayasvaraḥ | jāgṛvāṃsaḥ | 'jāgṛ nidrākṣaye' | asmālliṭ | tasya kasuḥ | 'ugidacām' iti num | dvivacanābhāvaśchāndasaḥ | 'vasvekājādasām' iti niyamāt' iḍabhāvaḥ | 'jāgroḍaviciṇṇalahisu' ( pā. sū. 7. 3. 85 ) iti guṇo na bhavati tatra viśiṣṭasya ka sorapi gṛhītatvāt | pratyayasvaraḥ | indhate | 'jihandhī dīptau' | antarbhāvitaṇyartho'- yam | nighātaḥ | havyavāham | 'vaha prāpaṇe' | 'vahaśca' iti ṇviḥ | upadhāvṛddhiḥ | kṛduttarapada- prakṛtisvaratvam | sahovṛdham | 'vṛṣu vardhane' | 'kvip ca' iti kvip || || 8 ||
'agnihotrā' iti navacamekādaśaṃ sūktaṃ viśvāmitraṃ gāyatramāgneyam |
'agnihotrā gāyatram' ityanukrāntatvāt |
prātaranuvākāśvinaśastrayogārthatre chandasīdaṃ sūktam |
sūtritaṃ hi- 'agnihotrāt ileti khataḥ' ( āśva. śrau. 4. 13 ) iti |
ādhāne prathamāyāḥ pavamāneṣṭau sviṣṭakṛto yājyā 'agnihotrā' ityeṣā 'sa havyavāḻamartyaḥ' iti puronuvākyā |
dvitīyapavamāneṣṭau sviṣṭakṛto'nuvākyā 'sāhvān' iti |
sūtritaṃ ca- 'sāhvānviśvā abhiyujo'bhimīḻe purohitamiti saṃyājye' ( āśva. śrau. 2. 1 ) iti |
ābhilavikeṣudhyepu tṛtīyasavane maitrāvaruṇaśastre 'abhi prayāṃsi' iti vaikalpiko'nurūpastracaḥ |
atra sūtraṃ- 'pra vo vājā abhiyavo'bhi prayāṃsi vāhasā' ( āśva. śrau. 7. 8 ) iti ||
agnihortā purohito'dhvarasya |
sa veda yajñamānuṣak ||

agniḥ | hotā | puraḥ'hitaḥ | adhvarasya | vi'carṣaṇiḥ | saḥ | veda | yajñam | ānuṣak || 1 ||
viśvāmitraḥ stauti | 'hotā devānāmāhvātā 'purohitaḥ purata evābhimatāphalasaṃpādakatvena hitakārī | yadvā puraḥ pūrvabhāge āhavanīyarūpeṇa hito nihita iti purohitaḥ | 'adhvarasya yajñasya 'vicarṣaṇiḥ | viśeṣeṇa draṣṭā | 'vicarṣaṇiḥ viśvacarṣaṇiḥ' iti tannāmasu pāṭhāt | 'saḥ tathāvidhaḥ 'agniḥ 'ānuṣak ānupūrvyeṇa sakṛt 'yajñam agniṣṭomādikaṃ 'veda jānāti || veda | 'vida jñāne' | 'vido laṭo vā' iti tipo ṇalādeśaḥ | laghūpadhaguṇaḥ | nighātaḥ | ānuṣak | āsamantāt anupatatītyā- nuṣak | 'ganja saṃge' | dhātuṣakārasya sakāraḥ | 'kvip ca' iti kvip | 'aniditām' iti upadhālopaḥ | 'upasargātsunoti' iti pādyam ||
1. sa--bījantantusantāme |
2. la--'niyamāt' nāsti |
3. sa--pāvamāneṣṭau |

a॒gniḥ dhi॒yā sa̭mṛ॒ṇvati ||
2 ||
saḥ | ha॒vya॒vāṭ | ma॒rtyaḥ | u॒śik | dūtaḥ | ca॒naḥ'hi̭taḥ | a॒gniḥ | dhi॒yā | sam | ṛ॒ṇvati || 2 ||
ᵛhavyavāṭ havipaḥ¹ voḍhā ᵛamartyaḥ maraṇadharmarahitaḥ | ᵛuśig haviḥ kāmayamānaḥ ᵛdūtaḥ devānāṃ dūtaḥ canase² havilakṣaṇāyannāya hito nihitaḥ | yadvā canohitaḥ | cano'nnaṃ havilakṣaṇaṃ hita- masyeti canohitaḥ | ᵛsaḥ tathāvidhaḥ ᵛagniḥ ᵛdhiyā prajñayā ᵛsamṛṇvati saṃgacchate | karmaviṣaya- prajñā'nvito'bhirasamānapi tādṛkprajñā'nvitaṃkarotivyāśāste || havyavāṭ | 'vaha prāpaṇe' | 'vahaśca' iti ṇtriḥ | ṭhatvajaśatvacatvāni | kuduttarapadaprakṛtisvaratvam | uśik | 'vaśa kāntau' | 'hajiḥ' ityanuvṛttau 'vaśeḥ³ kicca' iti ijpratyayaḥ | tasya kitvāt 'prahivyā'° iti saṃprasāraṇam | pratyayasvaraḥ | canohitaḥ | canaḥ 'cāyaterase hasvaśca' ityasunnantaḥ | cakārātkuḍāgamaḥ | tasmintyalopaḥ | nighātā'yudāttaḥ | 'punaścanaso'chandasi gatisaṃjñā vaktavyā' (pā. sū. 1. 4. 60. 2) iti gati- saṃjñāyāṃ 'gatiranantaraḥ' iti prakṛtisvaratvam | bahuvrīhau tu pūrvapadaprakṛtisvaratvam | dhiyā | 'sāvekācaḥ'° iti vibhakterudāttatvam | ṛṇvati | 'ṛṇu gatau' | 'leṭi tanādivāttupratyayaḥ | 'leṭo'ḍāṭau' hyaḍāgamaḥ ||
a॒gniḥ dhi॒yā sa ca̭tati ke॒turya॒jñasya̭ pū॒rvyaḥ |
artha̭ ha॒sya ta॒raṇi ||
3 ||
a॒gniḥ | dhi॒yā | saḥ | cḙtati | ke॒tuḥ | ya॒jñasya̭ | pū॒rvyaḥ | artha̭m | hi | a॒sya | ta॒raṇi || 3 ||
ᵛyajñasya agnihomādeḥ ᵛketuḥ jñāpakaḥ ᵛpūrvaḥ pūrve bhavaściraṃtanaḥ ᵛsaḥ tathāvidhaḥ ᵛagniḥ ᵛdhiyā prajñayā ᵛcetati sarvaṃ jānīte | asya etādṛśasyāgneḥ ᵛatha gamanasvabhāvaṃ tejaḥ ᵛasya ākāśe dṛśyamānasya tamasaḥ ᵛtaraṇi ᵛhi⁶ tārakaṃ⁷ khalu | nivartakamiti yāvat || cetati | 'citī saṃjñāne' | nighātaḥ | artham | 'ṛ gatau' | 'uṣikugiātibhyasthan' iti than | guṇaḥ | nintvādāyudāttaḥ | taraṇi | 'tṛ plavanataraṇayoḥ' | 'atisṛbhṛdhṛmi' ityadinā anipratyayaḥ | guṇaḥ | pratyayasvaraḥ ||
a॒gniḥ su॒nuṃ sarna̭śrutaṃ sa॒haso̭ jā॒tavḙdasam |
va॒hniṃ de॒vā a̭kṛṇvata ||
4 ||

ᵛsahasaḥ ᵛsunuṃ balasya putraṃ mathanarūpeṇa balena niṣpādyamānatvāt ᵛsanaśrutaṃ sanātanatvena prasiddhaṃ ᵛjātavedasaṃ jātaprajñaṃ tamimam ᵛagniṃ ᵛdevāḥ havirbhujo devāḥ ᵛvahniṃ haviṣāṃ voḍhāram ᵛakṛṇvata

'athaitasyāḥ' iti khaṇḍe sūtritam—'ā hoteti daśānāṃ tṛtīyāṣṭame ūrdhvediti traiṣṭubham' (ābha. śrau. 4. 13) iti |

'sahasvaḥ balavān he 'agne 'mitraśca 'varuṇaḥ ca mitrāvaruṇau 'viśve 'marutaḥ sarve devāśca 'tubhyaṃ 'tvadarthaṃ 'suṣṭhu stotram 'archan vahanti |
yadvā sarve devāstubhyaṃ tvāṃ suṣṭhu sukhenarcan pūjayanti |
'yat yasmāt kāraṇāt 'sahasraputra he agne 'sūryeḥ |
suṣṭhu arthaḥ svāmī |

yadvā sūryaḥ śobhanavīryaḥ |
'abhi 'kṣitīḥ |

vi pārjasā pṛthunā śośucāno bādhasva dviṣo rakṣaso amīvāḥ |

1 ||
vi | pārjasā | pṛthunā | śośucānaḥ | bādhasva | dviṣaḥ | rakṣasaḥ | amīvāḥ | suśarmaṇaḥ | bṛhataḥ | śarmaṇi | syāma | agre | aham | suhavasya | praṇītau || 1 ||
ukthīlaḥ stauti| he agne pṛthunā vistīrṇena pārjasā tejasā śośucānaḥ atyarthaṃ dīpyamānastvaṃ dvipaḥ karmānuṣṭhānavighnakāriṇaḥ śatrūn vi bādhasva viśeṣeṇa nāśaya| tathā amīvāḥ rogarāhityena māmadhyopetāni rakṣasaḥ | ubhayatra suvyatyayaḥ | tāni rakṣāṃsi bādhasva | karmavighnakārīṇi tānyapa- nudasva | yadvā amīvāḥ ābhimukhyenāgamanaparāgamanaparāḥ piśācikāśca bādhasva | suśarmaṇaḥ svargādi- lakṣaṇaśobhanasukhahetoḥ bṛhataḥ mahataḥ śatruvijayena prāptamahinaḥ suhavasya suṣṭuhvānopetasya agne śarmaṇi asmākaṃ sukhahetau praṇītau uttaravedādisthāneṣu praṇayane | prakarṣeṇa nayanaṃ tatra tatrānīya prakṣepaḥ | evaṃbhūte tava praṇayane ahaṃ syāma abhipraṇayanapūrvaka ṛtvanuṣṭhāne bhaveyam | pārjasā | pā rakṣaṇe | asun | ityanuvṛttau | pāterbale jud ca | ityasun | juḍāgamaḥ | nittvādādyudāttaḥ | pṛthunā | pratha prasthāne | ut | ityanuvṛttau | prathnidhradibhrajāṃ saṃprasāraṇam | ityupapratyayaḥ | tatsaṃniyogena saṃprasāraṇam | pratyayasvaraḥ | śośucānaḥ | śuca dīptau | ityasya yaṅluki | guṇo yaṅlukoḥ | ityabhyāsasya guṇaḥ | vyatyayena śānac | carkarītasya adādidatvāt śapo luk | civādantodāttatve prāpte abhya- mānāmādiḥ | ityādyudāttatvam | bādhasva | bādha loḍane | ityasya loṭi rūpam | pādādiṣvān nighātābhāvaḥ | suśarmaṇaḥ | śṛ hiṃsāyām | duḥkhaṃ śraṇātītyoṇādiko manin | sormanasī | iti uttarapadādyudāttatvam | syāma | asa bhuvi | ityasya liṅi | yāsuṭ parasmaipadeṣu | ityādinā yāsuṭ | liḍaḥ salopaḥ | iti salopaḥ | yāsuṭā iccatvāt kṣasoralopaḥ | nighātaḥ ||
tvaṃ nau asyā upamo vyuṣṭau tvaṃ mūra udite bodhi gopāḥ |
janmreva nityaṃ tanayaṃ jupasva stomaṃ me agne tanvāṃ sujāta ||

imaṃ naro marutaḥ saśvatā vṛdhaṃ yasminnāyaḥ śevṛdhāsaḥ |

sahasaputra balasya putra he agre tvaṃ naḥ asmān amataye śatrubhūtāya dāridryāya mā riradhaḥ siddhidān vipayabhūtān mā kuru | tathā avīratāyai aputratāyai | vīraśabdena tadvān lakṣyate | vīrasya bhāvo vīratā | na vīratā avīratā | tasyai | +asmān aputratāyā viṣayabhūtān mā kuru | tathā tvamasmān+ agotāyai gavādipaśusaṃpatsyābhāvasya viṣayabhūtān mā kuru | kiṃca asmān nide nindāyā viṣayabhūtān mā kuru mā vidhehi | tathā tvaṃ dveṣyāṃsi karmāparādhanimittān dveṣyāṃśca apa ā kṛdhi apākuru nivāraya || avīratāyai | vīrā asya santīti vīraḥ | arśāditvādyac | tasya bhāvo vīratā | tasya bhāvastvatalau iti tal | na vīratā avīratā | nañaḥ svare prāpte parādibhchandasi bahuḻam iti nañuttarasya ārdarudāttatā | riradhaḥ | rādha sādha maṃsiddhau | asmāṇyantācchāndase luṅi ṇiśridrusrubhyaḥ iti caleścaṅādeśaḥ | jeraniṭi iti ṇilopaḥ | ṇau caṅyupadhāyāḥ hrasvapadhāsvaḥ | caṅi iti dvivacanam | sanvallaghuni caṅapare iti sanvadbhāvaḥ | sammataḥ ityabhyāsasyetvam | tasya dīrgho laghoḥ iti dīrghaḥ | na māṅayoge ityaḍabhāvaḥ | nighātaḥ | agotāyai | gośabdena gomān lakṣyate | na gauragoḥ | agorbhāvo'gotā | tasya bhāvaḥ iti tal | lisvaraḥ | sahasaputra | subāmamantrita parāṅṅambare iti parāṅṅadbhāvāt sahasaputra ityetayoḥ sarvānudāttatvam | nide | ṇiti kutsāyām | asmātsapadādvidvābhāve kip | aniśyamāga śāsanam iti numabhāvaḥ | māvekācaḥ iti vibhakterudāttatā ||

he jātavedaḥ jātaprajña tava trīṇyāyūṃṣi trividhāni ājyauṣadhisomātmakānyanyāhāratayā kalpitāni he agne tisraḥ upasaḥ ekāhāhīnasaṃgrahatā upasaḥ upo'bhimāninyastisro devatāḥ te tava ājānīḥ janayibhyo mātaraḥ |

1. ta- ‘ tvaṃ ’ nāsti | 2. kha-ga-ca-ja-ṇa-pha- ‘ yathā ’ nāsti | 3. ga-ca-tanna- ‘ tvaṃ yathā ca ’ nāsti | 4. ga-ca-na- ‘ divaḥ...devān ’ nāsti | 5. kha-sa-yajñānuṣṭhānaṃ saṃpūrtyā | 6. kha-ga-ca-ja-ṇa-pa-pha-nātaprajña agne | 7. ta- ‘ upasaḥ ’ nāsti | 8. kha-ga-ca-ja-ṇa- ‘ yadvā...tisraḥ ’ nāsti | ṛ. 3-23
svasāraḥ 1 | tathā ca svasaḥ prakṛtya mantraḥ—' prajābhekā rakṣayūrjamekā rāṣṭramekā rakṣati ' iti | 7tābhiḥ ugho'bhimāninībhirdevatābhiḥ sahitasvam 7avaḥ asmābhirdīyamānaṃ havirlakṣaṇamannaṃ 7devānāṃ 7yakṣa yaṣṭavyadevānāṃ prayaccha | 7atha anantaraṃ 7vidvān jānastvaṃ 2 yajamānāya 7śaṃ 7bhava sukhaheturbhava | 7yoḥ ca sukhasya miśrayitā ca 3 bhava || tava | ' yuṣmadassadordesi ' ityādyudāttaḥ 4 | tistraḥ | triṣabdasya sthyarthe ' tricaturoḥ striyām 0 ' iti tisṛ ityādeśaḥ | ' aci ra ṛtaḥ ' iti rephādeśaḥ | ' tisṛbhyo jasaḥ ' iti vibhakterudāttatvam | ājānīḥ | ' jana janane ' | ' janighasibhyāmiṇ ' iti kartari iṇ | ṇittvādupadhāvṛddhiḥ | ' vā chandasi ' iti savarṇadīrghaḥ 5 | kṛduttarapadaprakṛtisvaraḥ 6 | avaḥ | ' ava rakṣaṇe ' | ' asun ' ityasun | nisvaraḥ | yakṣa | yajedaṃnārthasya leṭi rūpam | yajamānāya | ' pūṅyajoḥ śānan ' ( pā. sū. 3. 2. 128 ) iti yajeḥ śānan | nisvaraḥ | yoḥ | ' yu miśraṇe ' ityasmāt ' anyebhyo'pi dṛśyante ' iti vic | ārdhadhātukalakṣaṇo guṇaḥ | kṛduttarapadaprakṛtisvaraḥ ||

he agne tvat tvatto'pi pūrvaḥ prathamabhāvī yajīyān atiśayena yaṣṭā yaḥ yaḥ kaścit hotā homanipādakaḥ dvitā dvaidhaṃ madhye cottame ca sthāne svadhayā somākhyannena saha sattā niṣaṇṇaḥ san śaṃbhuḥ yaṣṭṝṇāṃ sukhabhūḥ abhūt |
dvitīyapādamevaṃ yāsko vyācakhyau—‘dvitā ca sattā svadhayā ca śaṃbhuḥ |
dvaidha sattā madhye ca sthāna uttame ca |
śaṃbhuḥ sukhabhūḥ ’ ( niru. 5. 3 ) iti |

1. te-homanipādakaḥ agniḥ | 2. ta-karmānulakṣya | 3. ta-la-ityasya | 4. kha-ga-ca-ja-ṇa- pha-bha-svarge loke; talla-ślokam | 5. la-kaḥ | 6. ta-pūrvadasvaraḥ | 7. ta-viniyogaḥ |
he ᵛagne tvam ᵛupetāṃ asmābhiḥ kriyamāṇasya ᵛpravagyākhyakarmaṇaḥ 1 ābhimukhyenāgamane ᵛsumanāḥ tatkarmasiddhayarthamanukūlamanasko bhūtvā ᵛnaḥ ᵛsādhuḥ ᵛbhava asmatsambandhikarmasādhako bhava |

he agne icchamānaḥ dhanaṃ kāmayamāno'haṃ yajamānaḥ idhmena samindhanakārisamisamūhena ghṛtena ājyena saha havyaṃ havanayogyaṃ haviḥ puroḍāśādikaṃ juhomi | kimartham | tarase vegāya tava satatagamanasiddhayarthaṃ balāya sāmarthyāya | puroḍāśādihavirbhāravahanasiddhayarthaṃ svayi haviḥ prakṣipāmi | kiṃca brahmaṇā stotreṇa vandamānaḥ tvāṃ stuvan ahaṃ yāvadīśe yāvaddhanaṃ voḍhuṃ śaknomi tāvadehi | tvaṃ ca imām asmābhiḥ kriyamāṇāṃ dhiyaṃ svadviṣayāṃ stutiṃ śataseyāya aparimitadhanaparyavasānāya devīṃ dīptām atiprabhūtāṃ kuru || idhmena | 'jihandhī dīstau' | idhyate'nenāgbhiritīdhmaḥ kāṣṭhaviśeṣaḥ | 'iṣi- yudhīndhidasibhyaścasṛbhyo mak' iti karaṇe mak | kittvādanāsikalopaḥ | prāyayasvaraḥ | icchamānaḥ | 'iṣu icchāyām' | vyatyayena śānac | ipugamimāṃ chaḥ' iti chakāraḥ | juhomi | 'hu dānādanayoḥ' ityasya laṭi rūpam | pādādisvādanighātaḥ | havyam | havanam arhatīti 'chandasi ca' ini yapratyayaḥ | bhasaṃjñāyāṃ 'yasya' ini lopaḥ | pratyayasvaraḥ | yāvat | yacchabdāt 'yattadetebhyaḥ parimāṇe vatup' iti vatup | 'ā sarvanāmnaḥ' ityākāraḥ | ādyudāttaḥ | īśe | 'īśa aiśvarye' ityasya laḍuttamaika- vacane iṭṭheṭtvam | adāditvācchapo luk | śataseyāya | 'po antakarmaṇi' | 'ādeca upadeśe'śiti' ityātvam | 'aco yat' iti bhāve yat | 'īyati' itītvam | ārdhadhātukalakṣaṇo guṇaḥ | kṛduttarapadaprakṛtisvaraḥ ||

ekasminbahuvacanaṃ pūjārtham | tathāvidheṣvamāsu ^1revat dhanayuktaṃ ^2bṛhat prabhūtaṃ ^3vayaḥ | vayaḥśabdaḥ annavācī | ‘arkaḥ vayaḥ kṣatra’ ityannanāmatu paṭhitatvāt | tādṛśaṃ prabhūtamanaṃ ^4dhehi vidhehi^2 | kiṃca ^5śaṃ rogāṇāṃ upaśamanaṃ ^6cāsmāsu ^7dhehi | ^8yoḥ bhayānāṃ yāvanam^9 amiśraṇaṃ ca dhehi^6 | ‘śamanaṃ ca rogāṇāṃ yāvanaṃ ca bhayānām (niru. 4. 21) iti yāskaḥ | ^10kṛttvaḥ asmābhiḥ kriyamāṇasya prabhṛtibhiḥ^7 vayaḥ sidghāmaḥ || śaśamāneṣu | ‘śaśa plutagatau’ ityayaṃ dhāturatra stutyarthaḥ | ‘tācchīlyavayovacanaśaktiṣu’ iti cānśa | ‘citaḥ^8’ ityantodāttatvam | ‘dhehi | ‘ḍudhāñ dhāraṇa- poṣaṇayoḥ’ ityasya loṭi rūpam | revat | rayirasyāstīti matup | ‘hasvanḍbhyāṃ matup’ iti matupa udāttatvam | ‘chandasīraḥ’ iti tasya vatvam | ‘rayematau bahulaṃ chandasi’ iti saṃprasāraṇam | parapūrvatvam | guṇaḥ | viśvāmitreṣu | ‘mitre caṇau’ (pā. sū. 6.3.130) iti pūrvapadasya dīrghatvam | ‘bahuvrīhau viśvaṃ saṃjñāyām’ iti pūrvapadāntodāttatvam | marmejjam | ‘mṛjjū śuddhau’ ityasya vartamāne liṭi^10 uttamabahuvacane maso mādeśaḥ | ‘anityamāgamāśasanam’ itīdabhāvaḥ | rephaśchandasaḥ | pādā- dirvādanaghātaḥ | pratyayasvaraḥ | bhūri | ‘bhū sattāyām’ | ‘adiśavibhūśubhibhyaḥ kin’ iti kin | kitvādguṇābhāvaḥ | nisvaraḥ | kṛttvaḥ | ‘ḍukṛñ karaṇe’ | saṃpadāditvādbhāve kvip | ‘tadasyāsti’ iti matup | ‘jhayaḥ’ iti tasya vatvam | ‘tasau mathvarthe’ iti bhasaṃjñāyāṃ takārasya jabhvābhāvaḥ | matuvaso ruḥ^0 iti takārasya rutvam ||

( ādha śrau . 5.14 ) iti ||
agnimupa̭sa॒maśvi̭nā॒ dadhi̭krā॒ṃ vyṷṣṭi॒ṣu ha̭vate॒ vahni̭rukthaiḥ | su॒jyoti̭ṣo॒ na॒ḥ śṛṇva̭ntu॒ devā̭ḥ su॒joṣā̭so a॒dhvara̭ṃ vā॒vaśa̭ānāḥ || 1 || agnim | upasam | aśvinā | dadhikrām | vi'uṣṭiṣu | havate | vahniḥ | ukthaiḥ | su'jyotiṣaḥ | naḥ | śṛṇvantu | devāḥ | sa'joṣāsaḥ | adhvaram | vāvaśanāḥ || 1 ||
gāthī brūte |

1. la—saderaprateḥ | 2. ca—na—la—° māthānte | 3. kha—ga—ja—na—bha—vaiśvadevadevatāke ; ca—la—vaiśvadevatāke | 4. ta—vahniḥ : stutīnāṃ voḍhā ; la—vahniḥ : stutīnāṃ voḍhā hotā | 5. ta—vyucchiteṣu | 6. ta—devān nārita | 7. ta—sahapriyamāṇā parasparaṃmilitāḥ | 8. kha—ga—ca—na—bha—sa—vivāsane | 9. kha—ta—pha—° prakṛtisvaraḥ |
(ṛ. saṃ. 10. 89. 15) ityatrānta udāttatā | tasmādatra nāyaṃ samāsa: kintu tatpuruṣa eva | bhṛvantu | ‘bhru śravaṇe’ ityasmāt ‘śruva: śra ca’ iti bhru: tatsaniyogena dhāto: śra ityadeśa: | nighāta: | vāvaśānā: | ‘vaśa kāntau’ ityasya yaṅluki rūpam | abhyāsasya ‘dīrgho'kita:’ iti dīrgha: | vyatyayena śānac | cisasvara: ||
agne trī te vājinā trī ṣadhasthā tisrastai jihvā ṛtajāta pūrvī: | tisra u te tanvau devavātāstābhirna: pāhi giro aprayucchan || 2 || agne | trī | te | vājinā | trī | sudhasthā | tisra: | te | jihvā: | ṛta'jāta | pūrvī: | tisra: | ū~ iti | te | tanva: | deva'vātā: | tābhi: | na: | pāhi | gira: | apra'yucchan ||2||
he agne te tava trī vājinā ājyauṣadhisomātmakāni trīṇyannāni trī ṣadhasthā trīṇi āgnīdhrādīni dhiṣṇyāni sthānāni |

gāthī brūte |

yadoṣadhīṣvapsu iti |

kiṃca yena vāyurūpeṇa tejasā antarikṣam ākāśam uru ātatantha ātenitha vistīrṇaṃ kṛtavānasi |
tveṣaḥ dīptimān ata eva bhānuḥ bhāsamānaḥ nṛcakṣāḥ nṛṇāṃ sākṣitayā draṣṭā arṇavaḥ samudravanmahān |

ṛ. 2-15
‘ agne yatte divi varca ityādidyo vā asya’ divi varcaḥ pṛthivyāmityayamagniḥ pṛthivyāṃ’ yadoṣa- dhīṣvapsvā’ yajatreti ya evauṣadhiṣu cāpsu cāmistametadāha’ yenāntarikṣamuvātantayeti vāyuḥ sa tveṣaḥ sa bhānuraṇvo nṛcakṣā iti mahānma bhānuraṇvo nṛcakṣā ityetat ’ ( śa. brā. 7. 1. 23 ) iti ||
pṛthivyām |
‘ udāttayaṇe halpūrvān ’ iti vibhakterudāttatvam |
oṣadhīṣu |
‘ oṣadheśca vibhaktāva- prathamāyām ’ iti dīrghatvam |
apsu |
‘ sāvekācaḥ ’ iti vibhakterudāttatvam |
ātatantha |
‘ tanu vistāre ’ ityasya liṭi thali ‘ babhūvātantatha jagṛbhma vavardha ’ iti nipātanāt iḍḍhyābhyāsalopāḥ na bhavanti |
‘ liti ’ iti pratyayātpūrvasyodāttatvam |
yadvattayogādanighātaḥ |
arṇavaḥ |

‘ arṇasaḥ salopaśca ’ iti salopaḥ |
pratyayasvaraḥ |

kṛduttarapadaprakṛtisvaraḥ ||
agne divo arṇamacchā jigāsacchā devā~ ūciṣe dhiṣṇyā ye |
yā raucane purastātsūryasya yāśvāvastādupatiṣṭhanta āpaḥ ||
3 ||
agne | divaḥ | arṇam | accha | jigāsi | accha | devān | ūciṣe | dhiṣṇyāḥ | ye | yāḥ | rocane | purastāt | sūryasya | yāḥ | ca | avastāt | upatiṣṭhante | āpaḥ || 3 ||
he ‘agne tvaṃ ‘divo ‘arṇaṃ dyuloke vartamāno'mbhaḥ ‘accha abhilakṣya ‘jigāsi dhūmadvārā prāpnoṣi |
kiṃca ‘dhiṣṇyāḥ |
dhiyaṃ vṛddhayupahitaṃ dehamuṣṇyurvanti iti dhiṣṇyāḥ ‘prāṇābhimānino devāḥ |
tānprāṇākhyān ‘devān ‘accha anukrameṇa ‘ūciṣe samavetānkaroṣi |
samavetā bhavatatyuktavān apītyarthaḥ |
‘sūryasya’ sūryalokasya ‘purastāt upari ‘rocane |
rocano nāmāyaṃ loko yatrāgneyaṃ jyotiḥ tapati |
tasminbhāsamāne rocanākhye loke ‘yāḥ āpo vidyante ‘avastāt sūryalokāt ‘adhastāt antarikṣaloke ‘yāśca ‘āpaḥ ‘upatiṣṭhante vartante tā ubhayarapastvaṃ prerayasi |
uktārtho vājasaneyibhiḥ spaṣṭamabhitaḥ—‘āpo vā asya divo'rṇastā epa dhūmenācchetyacchā devā~ ūciṣe dhiṣṇyā ya iti prāṇā vai devā dhiṣṇyāste hi sarvā dhiya iṇvanti’ yā rocane purastātsūryasya yāśvāvastādupatiṣṭhanta āpa iti rocano ha nāmaipa loko yatraipa etattapati tadyāśraita pareṇāpo yāśvāvareṇa tā etadāha’ ( śa. brā. 7. 1. 24 ) iti |
tā ubhayīrapastvaṃ prerayasīti ||

‘nirmathita: sudhita: ’ iti pañcarcamekādaśaṃ sūktam |
atra anukramaṇikā—‘ nimathito devaśravā devavātaśca bhāratau tṛtīyā satobṛhatī ’ iti |

agnirdevatā |

nirmathita: mudhita ā madhasthe yuvā kavirdhvarasya praṇetā

agne vi paśya bṛhatābhi rāyeṣāṃ nau netā bhavatādanu yūna ||
2 ||
armanthiṣṭhām | bhāratā | revat | agnim | devaśravāḥ | devavātaḥ | sudakṣam | agne | vi | paśya | bṛhatā | abhi | rāyā | iṣām | naḥ | netā | bhavatāt | anu | yūna ||2||
he agne bhāratā bharatasya putrau devaśravāḥ devavātaḥ cetyubhau sudakṣaṃ śobhanasāmarthyo- petaṃ revat rayimantam agnim aṅganādiguṇopetaṃ tvām armanthiṣṭhām amiṭomādikarmagrāhyatharmalaluḍa- tām |
mathanena svāmutpāditavantāvithyarthaḥ |

enaṃ tvām ᐁajījanan udpīpadana | he ᐁdevaśravaḥ ᐁmātṛṣu mātṛbhūtārabhyoḥ ᐁsujātaṃ suṣṭutpannam ata eṣa ᐁpriyaṃ ᐁkamanīyaṃ ᐁdaivavātaṃ ᐁdevavātena3 mathitamenam ᐁagniṃ ᐁstuhi | ᐁyaḥ agniḥ ᐁjanānāṃ stotṛṇāṃ yajamānānāṃ ᐁvaśī vaśavartī ᐁkarmaṇāṃ svātantryeṇa pravartakaḥ ᐁasat bhavati taṃ stuhīti pūrveṇānvayaḥ || daśa | 'daṃśa daśane' | 'kanin' ityanuvṛttau 'nudaśoguṇaśca' iti kanin | nissvaraḥ | kṣipaḥ | 'kṣipa preraṇe' | auṇādikaḥ karmaṇi kvip | kṣipyante preryante karmasviti kṣipo'ṅgulayaḥ | dhātusvaraḥ | ajījanan | 'janī prādurbhāve' | hetumaṇṇyantasya luṅi caṅdvivacana- sanvadbhāvābhyāsetvadīrghāḥ | nighātaḥ | stuhi | 'ṣṭuñ stutau' ityasya loṭi rūpam | adāditvāt śapo luk | nighātaḥ | daivavātam | devavātena nirvṛttamityarthe 'tena nirvṛttam' (pā. sū. 4. 2. 68) ityaṇ | pratyayasvaraḥ | asat | 'asa bhuvi' | asya leṭyaḍāgamaḥ | yadvattayogādanighātaḥ ||

he ᐁagne ᐁiḻāyāḥ gorūpadhāriṇyāḥ ᐁpṛthivyāḥ bhūmeḥ ᐁvare variṣṭhaṃ ᐁpade uttaravedyām ᐁahnāṃ ᐁsudinatve yajanīyadivamānāṃ śobhanadinatvārtham |

yo'gni: pūrvamantre pratipāditaḥ vtaṃ vśubhraṃ dyotamānaṃ vvaiśvānaraṃ viśvānarasambandhinaṃ vmātariśvānam |

tejomātraṃ' tatrābhūta' | anantaram 'arūpastūpaḥ ārocamānatejaḥsaṃghopetaḥ 'iḻāyāstvaputraḥ uttaravedyāḥ' utpannaḥ' agniḥ 'vayune araṇyāṃ yonisthāne 'ajaniṣṭa ajani || jajāna | 'jana janane' | loḍarthe' liṭi 'bahulamantyatrāpi saṃjñāchandasoḥ' iti ṇilopaḥ | pratyayāntatvābhāvādādya bhavati | nighātaḥ | arūpastūpaḥ | na vidyate rūpaḥ krāṃdho yasyetyarūpaḥ | krodharāhityena recamāna ityarthaḥ | 'nañsubhyām' ityuttarapadāntodāttatvam | 'puñ stutau' | 'stauterdīrghaśca' (u. mū. 3. 305) iti papratyayaḥ | kicca dhātordīrghaśca | stūyate iti stūpastejaḥsaṃghaḥ | arūpaḥ ārocamānastejaḥsaṃgho yasyeti bahuvrīhau pūrvapadasvaraḥ' | ruśāna | 'ruśa hiṃsāyām' | śatari rūpam | vayune | 'aja gatikṣapaṇayoḥ' | 'ajayamiśīṅbhyaśca' ityunan visaṃjakaḥ | ajani vīyate vā vayunamaham | pratyayasya vyayayenāyudāttatvam | ajaniṣṭa | 'janī prādurbhāvi' | luṅi sici rūpam | nighātaḥ ||

iḻāyāḥ | tvā | pade | vayam | nābhā | pṛthivyāḥ | adhi | jāta'vedaḥ | ni | dhīmahi | agne | havyāya | voḻhave || 4 ||
'jātavedaḥ jātaprajña he 'agne adhvarvādyaḥ 'vayaṃ 'pṛthivyāḥ 'adhi bhūmerupari uttaravedyāṃ 'nābhā nābhau tasyā nābhisthāne madhyapradeśe |
tadeva sthānaṃ viśiṣyate |
'iḻāyāḥ 'pade mānavyā iḻāyā goḥ pade iḍāpadarūpe uttaravedyātmake sthāne 'tvā tvāṃ 'havyāya 'voḻhave puroḍāśādilakṣaṇaṃ havirvoḍhuṃ 'ni 'dhīmahi nidadhīmahi |
uktārthe brāhmaṇam—'iḻāyāstvā pade vayaṃ nābhā pṛthivyā adhīyatedvā iḻāyāspadaṃ yaduttaravedīnābhirjātavedo ni dhīmahīti nidhārayanto hyenaṃ bhavantyagne havyāya voḻhava iti havyaṃ hi vakṣyanbhavati' (ai. brā. 1. 28) iti ||

ṛgvedaḥ
naraḥ karmaṇāṃ netārī he adhvarvādyaḥ kavi kāntadarśanam advayantaṃ dvayamānācarantamataṃ kurvāṇaṃ manasā vācā ca ekavidhameva karma kurvāṇamata eva cetasaṃ prakṛṣṭajñānopetam amṛtaṃ maraṇadharmarahitaṃ supratīkaṃ śobhanairajvālābhiḥ sametaṃ tamimam agni manthata manthanena niṣpādayata |
kiṃca |
he naraḥ karmaṇāṃ netāraḥ adhvarvādyaḥ yyaṃ yajñasya jyotiśomādeḥ ketuṃ prajñāpakaṃ prathamaṃ mukhyāṅgabhūtam |
agnipūrvakatvāta sarveṣāmuttarakatnām |
suśevaṃ śobhanasukhopetaṃ tādṛśamagni purastāt karmaṇaḥ prāraṃbhe janayata |
tamimamagnimaṃtravidhaguṇayuktaṃ kuruteti bhāvaḥ |

^jātaḥ manthanānantaramutpannaḥ |
sa ca ^cekitānaḥ sarva ^jānānaḥ san ^vājī satatagamanasvabhāvaḥ ^vipraḥ karmābhijñaḥ ata eva ^kaviśastaḥ kavibhirmedhāvibhirhotrabhiḥ stutaḥ ^sudānuḥ suṣṭhu karmaphalasya dātā evaṃvidhaḥ ^agniḥ ^rocate dīpyate |
^devāsaḥ devāḥ indrādayaḥ ^īḍyaṃ hotrādibhiḥ ^mṛtyuṃ ^viśvavidaṃ sarvasya jagato veditāraṃ ^yam agnim ^adhvareṣu jyotiṣṭomādijñeṣu ^havyavāhaṃ havyānāṃ voḍhāram ^adadhuḥ vyadadhuḥ so'gnirdapyate iti pūrvaiṇānvayaḥ |

yajamāno'dhvaryādīnprati brūte | he 'sakhāyaḥ karmasahakāriṇaḥ' yūyaṃ 'vṛṣaṇaṃ kāmānāṃ varṣitāraṃ 'dhūmam | 'dhunoti kampayatīti dhramo'gniḥ | evaṃvidham' agniṃ 'kṛṇota kuruta | rathanena' utpādayata | yadya- nniṃ jāyate' tadānīmāha | 'asridhantaḥ akṣīṇā anuparatasāmarthyāḥ santo yūyaṃ 'vājaṃ mathanakṣaṇaṃ yuddham 'accha abhyābhimukhyena 'itana prāpnuta | 'suvīraḥ śobhanasāmarthyopetaḥ 'ayamagniḥ 'pṛtanāpāṭ pṛtanāpalakṣitaṃ yuddhaṃ sahamāno bhavati | 'yena yuddhaṃ mahamānenāgninā 'devāsaḥ devāḥ 'dasyūn upakṣapayitṛnasurān 'asaṃhanta abhyabhavan | tasmādakṣaṇabalā yūyaṃ mathanaṃ' kuruteti bhāvaḥ || kṛṇota | 'kṛvi hiṃsākaraṇayoḥ' | loṭi tapratyayasya 'tasanasanathanāśca' iti tavādeśaḥ | pitvānnuṇaḥ | pādātvādinighātaḥ | apratyayasvaraḥ | dhūmam | 'dhṛñ kampane' | 'ipidhudhṛndhi- dasi' ityādinā mak | kittvātnuṇābhāvaḥ | 'dhunoti kampayati drāānīti dhramo'gniḥ | pratyayasvaraḥ | itana | 'iṇa gatau' 'iṣyasya lāṃṭi tapratyayasya tanādeśaḥ | nighātaḥ | pṛtanāpāṭ | 'paha marṣaṇe' | 'chandasi sahaḥ' iti ṇviḥ | kittvātupadhāvṛddhiḥ | 'sahaḥ sāḍaḥ saḥ' iti pātvam | kṛduttarapadasvaraḥ | asaṃhanta | 'paha marṣaṇe' ityasya laṅi rūpam | yaṇogādanighātaḥ | dasyūn | 'dasu upakṣaye' | 'yajaminiśumbhradasijanibhyo yuṣ' iti yuṣ | dasyūntīti dasyūvaḥ śatravaḥ | 'vyayayenādyudāttatvam ||

vyadhā idānīṃ te tava vyā tāni vīryāṇi ka krāsate vada || suśipaḥ | ‘sūplṛ gatau’ | auṇā- diko ranpratyayaḥ | bāhulakāt sṛ ityasya śi ityādeśaḥ | bahuvrīhau ‘ādyudāttaṃ yacchandasi ’ ityuttarapadādyudāttatvam | taratraḥ | narateḥ ‘aśitrādibhya inñautrau ’ ityutrapratyayaḥ | vyatyayenādyu- dāttatvam | mahāvānataḥ | ‘ātmahataḥ ’ ityākāradeśo mahatastakāramya | nuvikramiḥ | karoterauṇādiko mipratyayaḥ | guṇe kṛte karoterakārasyatvaṃ chāndasam | ‘halica ’ iti dīrghaḥ | ṛdhāvāna | ṛn (nṛn) hantrādyuvāḥ | ‘anyebhyo'pi dṛśyante ’ iti vic | haśiprahaṇāllopo hakārasya ghatvam | tadasyāstīti matubarthe ‘chandasīvanipau ’ iti vanip | vyatyayenādyudāttaḥ | ka | kiṃśabdasya saptamyarthe ‘kimo'n ’ ityanapratyayaḥ | tasmin ‘kāni ’ iti kādeśaḥ | tisvvaraḥ ||
tvaṃ hi ṣmā cyāvayancyutānyekau vṛtrā carasi jighnamānaḥ |
tava dyāvāpṛthivī parvatāso'nu vratāya nirmitaiva tasthuḥ ||
4 ||
tvam | hi | sma | cyāvayan | acyutāni | ekaḥ | vṛtrā | carasi | jighnamānaḥ | tava | dyāvāpṛthivī iti | parvatāsaḥ | anu | vratāya | nirmitaiva | tasthuḥ || 4 ||
he indra tvam ekaḥ asahāya eṣa acyutāni cyutirahitāniva dṛḍhamullānyāpi tava pratipakṣa- bhūtāni rakṣāṃsi cyāvayan svasvasthānāt pātyayan tathā vṛtrā vṛtrāṇi pāpāni jighnamānaḥ hiṃman carasi hi sma vartase khalu |

he indra dhāyuḥ nirantaramaiśvaryaṃ dadhānastvaṃ yasmai matryāya manuṣyāya ardhāḥ yadīyaṃ dadhāsi maḥ matryaḥ abhaktaṃ cit pūrvamaprāptamapi gehaṃ gṛheṣu bhavaṃ kanakapaśvādi kaṃ bhajate prāpnoti |

saha vartata iti sahadānuḥ | taṃ 'kṣiyantaṃ bādhamānaṃ 'kuṇāhaṃ kkaṇaśīlaṃ kaṃcidasuram 'aharataṃ hastahīnaṃ kṛtvā 'saṃ 'piṇak samyak cūrṇīkuru | kiṃca he 'indra 'vardhamānaṃ sarvata iṣumātraṃ vardhamānam | ‘ sa iṣu- mātramiṣumātraṃ viśvaḍḍhavadhata ’ (tai.saṃ. 2.4. 12. 2) iti śruteḥ | 'piyāruṃ hiṃsaṃ 'vṛtraṃ vṛtrāsuram 'apādaṃ pādahīnaṃ kṛtvā 'tavasā balena 'abhi 'jaghantha hatavānasi | yadvā sahadānumudakadānopetaṃ kṣiyantamākāśe nivasantaṃ kuṇahaṃ garjantaṃ vṛtram | vṛṇotyācchādayati nabha iti vṛtro meghaḥ | tamimaṃ vardhamānaṃ meghaṃ hastapādaśūnyaṃ kṛtvā saṃpiṇhū saṃpiṇḍa | tathā ca yāskaḥ— ‘ ahastamindra kṛtvā saṃpiṇḍa parikraṇanaṃ megham ’ (niru. 6. 1) iti || sahadānum | ‘ do avakhaṇḍane ’ | ‘ dābhyāṃ nuḥ ’ iti nupratyayaḥ | catīti dānurdānam | bahuvrīhau pūrvapadaprakṛtisvaraḥ | kṣiyantam | ‘ kṣi nivāsagatyoḥ ’ | tudāditvācchaḥ | pratyayasvaraḥ | piṇak | ‘ piñ sañcūrṇe ’ | rudhāditvāt bham | helek | pakārasya kakāraśchāndasaḥ | kuṇaham | ‘ kuṇa śabde ’ | bāhulakādūpasiddhiḥ | ādyudāttaḥ | jaghantha | ‘ hana hiṃsāgatyoḥ ’ ityasya liṭi thali rūpam | ‘ abhyāsācca ’ iti dhātuhakārasya ghatvam | nighātaḥ | ‘ upadeśe'cvataḥ ’ itītpratiṣedhaḥ ||

arṣenu | 'ṛpī gatau' | vyatyayena śap-pratyayaḥ | guṇaḥ | pādāditvādanighātaḥ | āpaḥ | 'āplu vyāptau' | kip dhātorhrasvaśca | jasi 'apnunttuc' ityādinā dīrghaḥ | jasaḥ suhavādanudāttatve dhātusvaraḥ | prasūtāḥ | 'pṛ preraṇe' ityasya karmaṇi niṣṭhā | 'gatiranantaraḥ' iti gateḥ prakṛtisvaratvam ||

ha indra alātṛṇaḥ | bahudakatayā alamarthyamānucyate hiṃsyata ityalātṛṇaḥ | goḥ : mādhyamikāyāḥ vācaḥ vrajaḥ : goṣṭhabhūtaḥ valaḥ | vṛṇotyākāśamiti valo meghaḥ | puga hantoḥ tava vajraprahārāt prāk bhayamānaḥ bibhyan vyāra viṣṭo babhūva | anantaraṃ sa indraḥ gāḥ apaḥ niraje nirgamayituṃ sugān sugamān pathaḥ mārgān akṛṇot akarot | ataḥ vāṇīḥ vananīyāḥ dharmantīḥ śabdayamānāstā āpaḥ puruhūtaṃ bahubhirāhūtaṃ pārthivamudakaṃ prati prāvan abhyāgacchan | etāmṛcaṃ yāska evaṃ vyākhyat— 'alātṛṇo vala indra vrajo goḥ | alātṛṇo'lamātardnā megho valo vṛṇotervājāṃ vrajaḥ yantarikṣaṃ goratasyā mādhyamikāyā vācaḥ purā hanādvayamānāṃ vyāra suganpatho akṛṇonni raje gāḥ | sugamānpatho'karonnirgamanāya gavām | prāvanvāṇīḥ puruhūtaṃ dharmantīḥ | āpo vā vahanādvāco vā vadanādbahubhirgṛhyatamudakaṃ bhavati | dharmatirgatikarmā' (niru. 6. 2.) || alātṛṇaḥ | 'tṛhi hiṃsāyām' | alaṃpūrvāt tṛheḥ 'ghañarthe kavidhānam' iti karmaṇi kapratyayaḥ | alamo makārasyākāraḥ | samāsasvaraḥ | valaḥ | 'vala saṃvaraṇe' | vahana ācchādyane namo'neneti valo meghaḥ | 'puṃsi saṃjñāyām' iti ghaḥ | pratyayasvaraḥ | vrajaḥ | 'vraja gatau' | 'gocarasaṃcara' (pā0 sū. 3.3.119) ityādinā ghapratyayāntatvena nipātanāt kutvābhāvaḥ | vrajastvantarikṣa iti | pratyayasvaraḥ | bhayamānaḥ | 'tribhī bhaye' | vyatyayena śap | āra | 'ṛ gatau' ityasya liṭi gali rūpam | nighātaḥ | sugān | 'gamlū gatau' ityasmādadhikaraṇe 'suduro- radhikaraṇe' iti ḍapratyayaḥ | dvitvāṭṭilopaḥ | kṛtsvaraḥ | niraje | 'aja gatikṣepaṇayoḥ' ityasmān saṃpadādilakṣaṇāṃ bhāve kip | kṛduttarasvaraḥ | āvan | ava rakṣaṇagatyādiṣu ityasya laṅi rūpam | puruhūtam | hayaterniṣṭhāyāṃ yajāditvāt saṃprasāraṇam | 'halaḥ' iti dīrghaḥ | dhātādisvaraḥ | dharmantīḥ | 'dhamā śabde' | śapi 'pāghrā' ityādinā dhamādeśaḥ | 'vā chandasi' iti jamaḥ savarṇadīrghaḥ | śatu- rsārbadhātukasvare kṛte dhātusvaraḥ || || 2 ||

yo'yam indraḥ ekaḥ asahāya eva san pṛthivīm uta api ca dyāṃ dve ete dyāvāpṛthivyau samīcī parasparaṃ saṃgate vasumatī dhanayukte sadyau ā parprā samantāt pūrayāmāsa |
uta api ca he śūra sāmarthyaupaṃta tādṛśastvaṃ rathīḥ rathavān saḥ naḥ asmākaṃ samīkaṃ samīpe iṣaḥ syātumicchan sayujaḥ saṃgatān vājān vajanti gacchantīti vājā aśvāḥ |
tān śrān antarikṣāt antarikṣakāśāt abhi preraya |

‘viśva sarve janāḥ’ ‘vivasvatyāḥ vivasvataḥ nirgamanaparāyāḥ’ ‘aktoḥ rātreḥ saṃbandhanaḥ’ ‘upaso’ ‘yāman yāmani nirgamane sati’ ‘mahi mahat’ ‘citraṃ cāyanīyam’ ‘anīkaṃ sauraṃ tejaḥ’ ‘didṛkṣante draṣṭumicchanti |
‘yat yadā’ ‘āgāt upaḥkālaḥ’ ‘samantādāgataḥ’ ‘vyapaiti tadā tejo didṛkṣamāṇāḥ sarve janāḥ’ ‘mahinā mahanīyānmihotrādīni karmāṇi’ ‘jānanti kartavyatayāvabudhyante’ |
he indra ‘puruṇi bahūni’ ‘sukṛtā suṣṭhu kṛtāni’ ‘karma karmāṇi’ ‘indrasya tavaiva’ ||
didṛkṣante |

‘vakṣaṇāsu |

‘yat yasmādudakāt’ ‘svātraṃ svādutaraṃ’ ‘saṃbhṛtaṃ svena saṃpāditaṃ dadhighṛtakṣīrādikaṃ’ ‘sīm etat’ ‘viśvaṃ sarvam’ ‘ustriyāyāṃ gavi’ ‘bhojanāya’ ‘indraḥ’ ‘adadhāt |

‘vakṣa roṣe’ |
asmān ‘karaṇādhikaraṇayośca’ iti 1. kha-ga-ca-ja-ṇa-pa-pha-bha-la-sa-uṣaḥkāle samantādāgāt |
2. kha-ga-ca-na-bha-sa-kartavyatā ca budhyante |
3. la-etāni karmāṇi |
4. ca-pha-vivāsayateḥ |
karaṇe lyuṭ | lisrvaraḥ | pakram | pacaterniṣṭhākārasya 'paco vaḥ' iti vatvam | pratyayasvaraḥ | carati | caratigarthyarthaḥ | bibhratī | 'ḍubhṛñ dhāraṇapoṣaṇayoḥ' | juhotyādiḥ | śatarugisvāt 'ugitaśca' iti ṅīp | 'abhyastānāmādiḥ' ityādyudāttatvam | ustriyāyām | 'vasa nivāse' | 'sphāyitañca' ityādinādhikaraṇe rakpratyayaḥ | yajādivāt saṃprasāraṇam | 'śāsivasighasīnāṃ ca' iti patvābhāva- śchāndasaḥ | svārthiko ghapratyayaḥ | pratyayasvaraḥ ||
indra da‌rha yāmakośā abhūvanyajñāya śikṣa gṛṇate sakhibhyaḥ |

indra̭ | da‌rha | yāma̭'kośāḥ | a॒bhūvan | ya॒jñāya̭ | śi॒kṣa | gṛ॒ṇatḙ | sa॒khi'bhya̭ḥ | dṷḥ'ma॒yava̭ḥ | dṷḥ'e॒vāḥ | ma॒rtyāsa̭ḥ | ni॒ṣaṅgiṇa̭ḥ | ri॒pava̭ḥ | ha॒ntvāsa̭ḥ || 15 ||
he indra da‌rha dṛḍha bhava | yataḥ yāmakośāḥ | yāntyasminniti yāmo mārgaḥ | tasya kośāḥ kośa- vadācchādakāḥ mārgapratibandhakā rākṣasāḥ abhūvana | tvaṃ yajñāya yajñakartre gṛṇate stotraṃ kurvate yaja- mānāya sakhibhyaḥ putrādibhyaśca śikṣa apekṣitaṃ phalaṃ datsva | durmayavaḥ | duṣṭamāyudhāni minvanti prakṣipantīti durmayavaḥ | durevāḥ | duṣṭaṃ gacchantaḥ martyāmaḥ mārayitāraḥ niṣaṅgiṇaḥ | niṣaṅgaśabdena iṣudhirabhidhīyate | tadantaḥ | evaṃvidhasāmarthyopetāḥ ripavaḥ śatravo rākṣasāḥ hantvāsaḥ tvayā hananīyāḥ || da‌rha | 'raha daha vṛha vṛhi vṛddhau' | vyatyayena śyan | loṭi rūpam | nisvaraḥ | abhūvan | 'bhū sattāyām' | asya luḍi rūpam | 'gātisthā' iti sico luk | 'bhṛsuvostiṅi' iti guṇe pratiṣiddhe 'bhuvo vugluṅliṭoḥ' iti vugāgamaḥ | nighātaḥ | śikṣa | 'śikṣa vidyopādāne' | ayaṃ dhātutra dāne vartate | vyatyayena parasmaipadam | nighātaḥ | durmayavaḥ | minoteḥ 'kṛvāpājimi' ityādinā uṇpratyayaḥ | kṛduttarapadasvaraḥ | durevāḥ | 'dṛṅ gatau' | 'iṇsīdbhyāṃ van' iti kartari vanapratyayaḥ | ripavaḥ | 'rericopadhāyāḥ' ityupapratyayaḥ | upadhāyā hrasvam | pratyayasvaraḥ | hantvāsaḥ | 'hana hiṃsāgatyoḥ' | 'kṛtyārthe tavaikenyantyatvanaḥ' iti tvan | nivādādyudāttaḥ || || 3 ||
saṃ ghoṣaḥ śṛṇve'vamairamitrairjehī nyeṣvaśarni tarpiṣṭhām |
vṛśmedhastāddhi rujā sahasva jahi rakṣo maghavannrandhayasva ||

sam | ghoṣaḥ | śṛ॒ṇve | a॒vamai̭ḥ | a॒mitrai̭ḥ | ja॒hi | ni | e॒ṣu | a॒śarnim | ta॒rpiṣṭhām | vṛ॒śma | a॒dhastā̭t | vi | ru॒ja | sa॒hasva̭ | ja॒hi | rakṣa̭ḥ | ma॒ghavan | ra॒ndhayasva || 16 ||
he indra avamaiḥ samīpe vartamānaiḥ amitraiḥ śatrubhissurairhasṛṣṭo yo'śaniḥ ghoṣaḥ dhupyamāṇaḥ saṃ śṛṇve samyagasmābhiḥ śrūyate tapiṣṭhām atiśayena tāpikāṃ tām aśanim eṣu śatrudhveva
1. kha-ga-ca-ja-ṇa-pa-pha-bha-ācchādakāḥ | 2. kha-ga-ca-ja-ṇa-na-sa-putrādibhyastvam | 3. la- ityayam | 4. kha-ga-ca-ja-ṇa-pha-bha-ādāne | 5. kha-ga-ca-ja-ṇa-ta-na-pha-bha-sa-śatruṣu ava |
nidhāya 'jahi māraya | taiḥ prayuktāmaśāni' tāneva prāpayeti bhāvaḥ | 'īm enān śatrūn 'adhastāt mūlataḥ 'vṛśca' chinatthi | kiṃca 'vi 'ruja tān viśeṣeṇa bādhasva 'sahasva' abhibhāva ca | he 'maghavan indra 'rakṣaḥ karmavighnakārīṇi rakṣāṃsi 'jahi māraya | anantaramimaṃ yajñaṃ 'randhayasva pāraṃ naya' || śṛṇve | nighātaḥ | jahi | 'hana hiṃsāgatyoḥ' | asya loṭi rūpam | aśanim | 'aś vyāptau' | 'aśyavitṛbhyo 'niḥ' 'ityanipratyayaḥ | pratyayasvaraḥ | napiṣṭhām | namraśabdādiṣṭhaḥpratyayaḥ | nitsvaraḥ | vṛśca | 'ovaśca' 'chedane' | tudādiḥ | prahijyādinā saṃprasāraṇam | mahasva | vākyabhedādanighātaḥ | randhayasva | 'radha hiṃsā saṃrāddhayoḥ' 'ityasya pyannasya loṭi 'radhijabhorantra' iti numāgam | vākyabhedādanighātaḥ ||
urduha rakṣaḥ sahamūlamindra vṛśca madhyaṃ pratyagraṃ śṛṇīhi |
ā kīvataḥ salalakaṃ cakratha brahmadviṣe tarpaṣi hetimasya |

ut | vṛha | rakṣaḥ | sahamūlam | indra | vṛśca | madhyam | pratita | agram | bhūrṇāhi | ā | kīvataḥ | salalakam | cakratha | brahmadviṣe | tarpaṣi | hetim | asya || 17 ||
he 'indra 'rakṣaḥ karmavighnakāri rakṣaḥkulaṃ 'sahamūlaṃ mūlasahitaṃ yathā bhavati tathā 'urduha uddhara | kiṃca 'madhyaṃ tasya madhyabhāgaṃ 'vṛśca chinatthi | 'pratyagram agraṃ pratyagataṃ tata 'bhūrṇāhi jahi | tasya rakṣaḥkulasya madhye 'salalakaṃ saraṇaśīlaṃ rakṣaḥ 'ā 'kīvataḥ | 'āṅ maryādāyām' | kiyataḥ aniścīyamānaparimāṇāt atidūrādeśāt 'cakratha kuru | kṛtvā ca 'brahmadviṣe brāhmaṇa dveṣakāriṇe tasmai rakṣase 'tarpaṣi tāpakaṃ 'hetiṃ ghātakamāyudhaṃ 'asya kṣipa | uktamarthaṃ yāsko'bravāt---'uddhara rakṣaḥ sahamūlamindra | mūlaṃ mocanādvā mopaṇādvā mohanādvā | vṛśca madhyaṃ prati śṛṇātyagram | adrāgataṃ bhavati | ā kiyato deśātsalalakaṃ salalañca bhavati pāpakamiti nairuktāḥ | sarukkaṃ vā syātsarteravyaratāta | tarpipastapaterhinateḥ' (niru. 6. 3) iti || vṛha | 'vṛha udyamane' ityasya loṭi rūpam | śṛṇīhi | 'śṛ hiṃsāyām' | asya loṭi hau 'ī hatyaghoḥ' 'ityūha | nighātaḥ | kīvataḥ | kiṃśabdāt kiṃ parimāṇasyetyarthe 'kimidabhyāṃ vo ghaḥ' iti kiṃśabdāt catubaratīti jñāpitatvāt vatup | vādeśābhāvaśchāndasaḥ | 'idaṃ kimorīśkī' iti kī ityadeśaḥ | salalakam | 'sṛ gatau' | chāndasī rūpasiddhiḥ | cakratha | 'ḍukṛñ karaṇe' ityasya liṭi thali rūpam | krādiśvādisadbhāvaḥ | nighātaḥ | tarpipam | 'napa saṃtāpe' ityasmāt 'atiparvāpayajitanidhanitapivyo nit' ityusipratyayo nicca | akārasya ikāraśchāndasaḥ | nitsvaraḥ | hatim | 'hana hiṃsāgatyoḥ' | 'utiyutijūtisāti' ityādinā nipātādantodāttaḥ | asya | 'asu kṣepaṇe' | loṭi rūpam ||
1. ta-na-la-rā-prayuktāmaśānim | 2. gha-ja-ṇa-nityatha | 3. ta-sa-'sahasva' nāsti | 4. kha-ga- ca-ja-ṇa-ka-nayasva | 5. ma-na-vṛśca | 6. kha-ga-ca-ja-ṇa-ta-madhyaṃ bhāgam, na-madhyaṃ madhyam; sa- madhyam | 7. ha-apramagram 8. kha-ga-ca-ja-ṇa-ka-bha-abhijñāyamānam | 9. kha-ga-ja-ṇa-na-bha-sa-vṛha |

he indra naḥ asmadarthaṃ dyumantaṃ dyutiyuktaṃ bhagaṃ dhanam ā bhara ā samantāta saṃbhara | deṣṇasya dānaśīlasya te tava saṃbandhino dhanasya prareke prarecane dāne ni dhīmahi dhanadānamya vayaṃ pātrabhūtāḥ sma ityarthaḥ | kuta ityata āha | asme asmākaṃ kāmaḥ abhilāpaḥ ūrvaiva anavāsakāmo vaḍavānala iva prapathe prabhṛto vartate | vasūnāṃ vasupate dhanānāṃ madhye uttamadhanānāmadhipate he indra taṃ mahāntaṃ kāmam ā pṛṇa bahudhanapradānenāpūraya || bhara | 'bhṛñ bharaṇe' ityasya loṭi rūpam | deṣṇasya | 'ḍudāñ dāne' | 'gādābhyāmiṇṇuṇ' itīṇṇuṇpratyayaḥ | athyarthaṃ dadātīti deṣṇaḥ | cimasvaraḥ | dhīmahi | dadhāterliṅi 'bahula chandasi' iti śapo luk | 'chandasyubhayathā' iti mahiṅaḥ sārvadhātu- katvāt sīyuṭaḥ salopaḥ | ārdhadhātukatvāt āto lopaḥ | nighātaḥ | ūrvaiva | pūrvapadasvaraḥ | prapathe | 'pratha prakhyāne' ityasya liṭi rūpam | pṛṇa | 'pṛṇa prīṇane' ityasya loṭi serhiḥ | tudādiḥ | 'ato heḥ' iti herluk | vasūnām | nistvādādyudāttaḥ ||

imaṃ kāmaṃ mandyā gobhiraśvaiścandravatā rādhasā paprathaśca | svaryavo matibhistubhyaṃ viprā indrāya vāha: kuśikāso akran || 20 || imam | kāmam | mandya | gobhi: | aśvai: | candra'vatā | rādhasā | papratha: | ca | sva:'yava: | mati'bhi: | tubhyam | viprā: | indrāya | vāha: | kuśikāsa: | akran || 20 ||
he indra ˅imaṃ dhanādiviṣayaṃ ˅kāmam asmadīyabhilāṣaṃ ˅gobhi: agnihotrādikarmasādhana- bhūtairgāvādibhirvāhnasādhanabhūtai: ˅aśvai: ca ˅candravatā dīmiyuktena ˅rādhasā dhanena ˅ca ˅rūndaya |

ā nau gotrā dardahi gopate gā: samasmabhyaṃ sunayo yantu vājā: | divakṣā asi vṛṣabha satyaśuṣmo'smabhyaṃ su maghavanbodhi godā: || 21 || ā | na: | gotrā | dardahi | go'pate | gā: | sam | asmabhyam | sunaya: | yantu | vājā: | divakṣā: | asi | vṛṣabha | satya'śuṣma: | asmabhyam | su | maghavan | bodhi | godā: ||21||
˅gotrai' svargādhipate he indra ˅na: asmadartha ˅gotrā gotrāṇyatrāṇi ˅gā: apa: prati ˅ā ˅dardahi ādiyasva | atrāṇi vidīrya' jalāni prāpayetyartha | tata: ˅sanaya: saṃbhajanīyāni ˅vājā: annāni ˅asmabhyam asmadartha ˅saṃ ˅yantu samāgacchantu | ˅vṛṣabha kāmānāṃ varṣitahe' indra tvaṃ ˅divakṣā: ˅asi divaṃ vyāpya vartamānatvena mahānasi' | ata eva ˅satyaśuṣma: satyasāmarthya: ˅maghavan dhanavan he indra ˅asmabhyaṃ ˅godā: gavāṃ pradātā bhavāmīti ˅su ˅bodhi suṣṭhu budhdyasva || dardahi | ' dṛhū ādare ' | yaṅluki loṭi rūpam | ' ṛtaśca ' ( pā. sū. 7. 4. 92 ) ityabhyāsasya rugāgama: | nighāta: | gā: | ' auto'msāso: ' ( pā. sū. 6. 1. 93 ) ityokārasyākāra: | ekādeśasvara: | asmabhyam | ' bhyaso bhyam ' iti caturthībahuvacanasya bhyāmādeśa: | yantu | ' iṇ gatau ' | loṭi rūpam | divakṣā: | ' aś vyāptau ' | asun | divaṃ vyāpnotīti divakṣā: | kuduttara- padasvara: | godā: | ' hudāña dāne ' | asmān vic | kuduttarapadasvara: ' ||
1. kha-ga-ca-ta- ' ca ' nāsti | 2. ta-gopate gavāṃ pate | 3. kha-ga-ca-ja-ṇa-pa-pha-bha-la-sa- vidārya | 4. pha-varṣaka he | 5. ja-ṇa-bha-bhavasi | 6. la-ityasmāt | 7. ta-kuduttarapadaprakṛtisvara: |
śunaṃ huvema maghavānamindramasminbhare nṛtamaṃ vājasātau | śṛṇvantamugramūtaye sumatsu hantaṃ vṛtrāṇi saṃjitaṃ dhanānām || 22 || śunam | huvema | maghavānam | indram | asmin | bhare | nṛtamam | vāja'sātau | śṛṇvantam | ugram | ūtaye | sumat'su | hantam | vṛtrāṇi | sam'jitam | dhanānām || 22 ||
he indra 'vājasātau' |

yadvā 'śunamiti sukhanāma |
sukhakaraṃ 'maghavānaṃ dhanavantam ata eva 'indraṃ niratiśayaiśvaryasaṃpannaṃ 'nṛtamaṃ sarvasya jagato'tiśayena netāraṃ tvāṃ 'huvema kuśikā vayaṃ yajñārthamāhvayema |
tathā 'śṛṇvantam asmābhiḥ kriyamāṇāṃ stutiṃ śṛṇvantam 'ugraṃ śatrūṇāṃ bhayaṃkaraṃ 'samatsu saṃgrāmeṣu 'vṛtrāṇi vṛtropalakṣitāni sarvāṇi rakṣāṃsi 'hantaṃ hiṃsantaṃ 'dhanānāṃ śatrumandhināṃ 'saṃjinaṃ samyagjetāraṃ tvām 'ūtaye rakṣaṇāya vayamāhvayema ||

triṣṭupchandaḥ |
caturthābhiṣeke'hani mādhyaṃdine' savane' acchāvākasya ārambhaṇīyāyā ūrdhvamahīnasūkta- saṃjñakametasūktaṃ śaṃsanīyam |
sūtritaṃ ca —' asmā idu pranavase śāsaddihiratīrāvarhānasūkte' ( āśva. śrau. 7. 4 ) iti |
pṛṣṭhābhiplavapadahayoḥ |
prathame'hani mādhyaṃdinasavane'cchāvākasye'hīnasūktasya sthāne trīṇi saṃpātasūktāni |
tatredaṃ tṛtīyaṃ saṃpātasūktam |
'imāmū vicchanti tvā śāsaddihiriti saṃpātāḥ ' ( āśva. śrau. 7.5 ) iti ||
śāsaddirduhiturnaryaṃ gādidvāṃ ṛtasya dīdhitiṃ saparyana | pitā yatra duhituḥ sekamujjhantaṃ śagmyena manasā dadhanve || 1 || śāsat | vahneḥ | duhituḥ | naryam | gāt | vidvān | ṛtasya | dīdhitim | saparyana | pitā | yatra | duhituḥ | sekam | ṛjhan | sam | śagmyena | manasā | dadhanve || 1 ||
1. ca-pha-saṃbhajanam | 2. gha-ja-ṇa-asmin | 3. kha-ga-ja-ṇa-bha-'śunaṃ' nāsti | 4. kha-ga- ca-ja-ṇa-ta-na-bha-la-sa-'yadvā......sukhakaraṃ' nāsti | 5. la-ghatvam | 6. la-'tuk' nāsti | 7. kha-ga-ja-ṇa-ta-bha-sa-mādhyaṃdinasavane |
kuśikaḥ' prasaṅgāt kaṃcit śāstrārthaṃ brūte'-'aputrasya' pituḥ putrī dāyādā putrikā satī|
yataḥ saṃtānakṛttasyāḥ putra ityanayocyate' iti'|
aputro' yaḥ pitā kanyāmanyakulaṃ prāpayati sa 'vahviḥ ityucyate'|
sa pitā 'śāsat'|
'abhātṛkāṃ pradāsyāmi tubhyaṃ kanyāmalaṃkṛtām|
asyāṃ yo jāyate putraḥ sa me putro bhavediti' (vāsiṣṭha. 17. 17) smṛtyuktam' anuśāsanaṃ kurvan 'duhituḥ putrikāyāḥ 'naśya naptṛbhavaṃ piṇḍadānādikaṃ kartavyatayā' gān gacchati|
kiṃ kurvan|
'vidvān aryāṃ duhitari jātaḥ putro mama svadhākaro bhaviṣyatīti jānan 'ṛtasya' satyasya putrotpādanasamarthasya' retasaḥ 'dīdhiti dhatīraṃ jāmātaraṃ tatpatiṃ' 'saparyan vastrādyalaṃkārādinā pūjayan 'pitā naptṛbhavaṃ' gacchati|
'yatra yasyāḥ 'duhituḥ pitā pālakaḥ patirjāmātā 'sekaṃ tayāṃ reta:sekam 'tra ṛn prasā
dadātītyabhiprāyaḥ |
'asaṃskṛtāstu saṃskāryā bhrātṛbhiḥ pūrvasaṃskṛtaiḥ |
bhaginyaśca nijādaṃśātsvāṃśaṃ tu turīya kam' (yājña. 2. 124) iti yājñavalkyasmaraṇāt |
strīpuṃsalakṣaṇasyāpatyasyakopādha- tvāviśeṣānpumapatyasya ko viśeṣa ityāśaṅkya viśeṣaṃ darśayati |
'yadi yadyapi 'mātaraḥ mātāpitarau 'vahvim |
avahniḥ strī vahviḥ pumān svabhāryāyā voḍhṛtvāt' |
avahniśca vahviśca' |
tādṛśaṃ strīpuṃsalakṣaṇa- mapatyaṃ 'janayanta |
tathāpi tayormadhye 'anyaḥ puṃlakṣaṇaḥ 'sukṛtoḥ śobhanasya piṇḍadānādeḥ karmaṇaḥ 'kartā bhavati |
'anyaḥ strīlakṣaṇaḥ 'ṛndhan vastrāṃlakārādinā ṛdhyamāna eva bhavati |
piṇḍadānādi- kartṛtvātputro dāyādaḥ duhitustathātvaṃ 'neti' na dāyārhā sā tu kevalaṃ parasmai dīyate |
tathā ca niruktaṃ- 'na jāmaye bhaginyai |
jāmiranye'syāṃ janayanti jāmapatyaṃ jamatervā syādvitakarmaṇo nirgamanaprayā bhavati |
tānva ātmajaḥ putro rikthaṃ prāricatrapādāccakāraināṃ garbhinadhānīṃ sanirtuhastagrāhasya yadiha mātaro'janayanta vahni putramavahviṃca striyamanyataraḥ saṃtānakartā bhavati pumānadāyādo'nyataro'dhayitvā jāmiḥ pradīyate parasmai' (niru. 3. 6) iti ||

agniḥ | jajñe | juhvā | rejamānaḥ | mahaḥ | putrān | arupasya | prayakṣai | mahān | garbhaḥ | mahi | ā | jñātam | eṣām | mahī | pravat | hari | aścasya | yajñaiḥ || 3 ||
he indra 'arupasya rocamānasya' tava 'prayakṣai yajñārthaṃ 'juhvā jvālābhiḥ 'rejamānaḥ kampamānaḥ 'agniḥ 'mahaḥ prabhūtān 'putrān raśmīna 'jajñe utpādayāmāsa |
'eṣāṃ raśmīnāṃ 'garbheḥ jalāramanā' kroḍīkāraḥ 'mahān āsīt |
kiṃca eṣāṃ raśmīnāṃ melanam 'ā 'jātaṃ sarvata oṣadhyātmanā jātaṃ 'mahi mahadabhūt |
kiṃca eṣāṃ raśmīnāṃ 'pravat anena prakāreṇa pravṛttiḥ 'haryaśvasya indrasya 'yajñaiḥ yajanāhaiḥ ' somājyāhutibhiḥ 'mahī mahatī bhavati ||

1. na-pha-udayatvāt | 2. kha-ga-ca-pha- 'vahisva' nāsti; la-rā-vahniḥ | 3. ta-na-duhitustathātve- 'pi | 4. kha-ga-ja-ṇa-ta-na-bha-sa- 'tānvaḥ' nāsti | 5. kha-ga-ja-ṇa-ta-na-la-sa-kupratyayaḥ | 6. la-ārocamānasya | 7. ca-pha-balātmanā | 8. kha-ga-pha-yajanāhaiḥ; ta-rā-yajñairjamānāhaiḥ | 9. sa-somājyādibhiḥ prabhṛtibhiḥ |
abhi jaitrīrasacanta spṛdhaṃ mahi jyotistamaso nirajānan |
taṃ jānatīḥ pratyadāyanupāsaḥ patirgavāmabhavadeka indraḥ ||

abhi | jaitrīḥ | asacanta | spṛdham | mahi | jyotiḥ | tamasaḥ | niḥ | ajānan | tam | jānatīḥ | prātaiḥ | ut | āyan | uṣasaḥ | patiḥ | gavām | abhavat | ekaḥ | indraḥ ||4||
'jaitrīḥ jitvaryo viśo marutaḥ 'spṛdhānaṃ vṛtreṇa saha' spardhamānamindram 'abhi 'asacanta sadhaitaḥ samagacchan |
te ca marutaḥ 'mahi mahat 'jyotiḥ sūyāryaṃ tejaḥ 'tamasaḥ tamorūpāt vṛtrāt 'nirajānan nirgatamajñāsipuḥ |

tathā cāsyā ṛco'yamarthaḥ |
'dhīrāḥ dhīmantaḥ 'sapta 'viprāḥ medhāvinaḥ sapta- saṃkhyākā aṅgirasaḥ 'vīḻau dṛḍhe parvate 'satīḥ nigṛḍhāḥ 'satīrgāḥ 'abhi abhilakṣya 'atandan upekṣāṃ kurvan |
tataste'ṅgirasaḥ parvatabile gāvaḥ santīti 'manasā niścitya 'prācā yena mārgeṇa praviṣṭāstenaiva prācīnena mārgeṇa tā gāḥ 'ahinvan nirgamayan |

svaṃ svakīyaṃ godhanam abhi abhilakṣya saṃpaśyamānāḥ madīyā etā gāva ityātmīyatayā jānānāḥ te'ṅgirasaḥ pratnasya purā jātasya retasaḥ nadupalakṣitasya putrasya dhāraṇārthaṃ payaḥ duhānāḥ tā gāḥ payo duhantaḥ santaḥ amadan hṛṣṭā abhūvan |

he indra tvaṃ devīḥ devanśīlāḥ apaḥ pari vavrivāṃsaṃ parita āvṛtya vartamānamata eva adevaṃ bahudakatāyasvacchatvenāpratibhāsamānaṃ śayānam ekatra niścalatayā vartamānaṃ vṛtraṃ meghaṃ yat yadā caratā tamuddiśya vartamānena vadhena jaghanvān hatavānasi tadā tvam ājī yuddhe sartavai ativegagamanāya atyāniva yathāśvān prerayasi tathā tvamapaḥ udakānyantarikṣādākāśān prāsṛjaḥ ha prakarṣeṇa meghabhedanaṃ kṛtvā sṛjaḥ khalu |
taṃ yajāma ityuttareṇānvayaḥ ||

'hana hiṃsāgatyoḥ' |

1. na-la-sa- 'haritavarṇāśvopeta' nāsti | 2. kha-ga-ja-ṇa-bha-sa-prāpaya sa | 3. tra-pa-pha-bha- yathā tvamabhān | 4. ca-pha-śavikaraṇaḥ |
bhājo | ‘aja gatikṣepaṇayoḥ’ | ‘ajyātibhyāṃ ca’ (u.sū. 4.570) iti iṇpratyayaḥ | ṇittvādupadhāvṛddhiḥ | ajanti gacchantyasminnidhyājiyurddham | pratyayasvaraḥ | sartavājāvityatra saṃhitāyām āyādeśe kṛte tasya ‘lopaḥ śākalyasya’ iti yakāralopaḥ | sarvavidhīnāṃ chandasi vikalpitatvāt savarṇa- dīrghatve kartavye yalopo nāsiddho bhavati | savarṇadīrghaḥ | śayānam | ‘śīṅ svapne’ | śānac | adāditvācchapo luk | ḍittvālasārvadhātukasvareṇa śānaco'nudāttatve dhātusvaraḥ | vadhena | hanterbhāve ‘hanaśca vadhaḥ’ ityaṇpratyayo vadhādeśaśca dhātorantodāttaśca | vavrivāṃsam | ‘vṛñ varaṇe’ ityasya kvasau iḍāgamaśchāndasaḥ | pratyayasvaraḥ ||

7 ||
yajāmaḥ | it | namasā | vṛddham | indram | bṛhantam | ṛṣvam | ajaram | yuvānam | yasya | priye iti | mamatuḥ | yajñiyasya | na | rodasī iti | mahimānam | mamāte iti || 7 ||
˅namasā havirlakṣaṇenānnena ˅vṛddhaṃ pravṛddham ata eva ˅bṛhantaṃ mahāntam ˅ṛṣvam |

indrasya | karma | sukṛtā | puruṇi | vratāni | devāḥ | na | minanti | viśve | dādhāra | yaḥ | pṛthivīm | dyām | uta | imām | jajāna | sūryam | uṣasam | sudaṃsāḥ ||8||
˅viśve sarve ˅devāḥ ˅indrasya tava ˅karma karmāṇi ˅sukṛtā sukṛtāni suṣṭhu nirmitāni pṛthivyādīni ˅purūṇi bahūni ˅vratāni yajñādīni ca ˅na ˅minanti |
etāni sarvāṇi tava karmāṇi na hiṃsanti |
kimvādriyante |

1. la-pratyayasvaraḥ | yajñiyasya | yajñaśabdādahetyarthe ‘yajñartibhyāṃ ghakhamo’ iti ghapratyayaḥ | ghasya iyādeśaḥ | pratyayasvaraḥ | 2. kha-ga-gha-ca-ja-ṇa-na-bha-sarvāṇi |
'imām imamantarikṣalokaṃ ca etāṃstrīn lokān 'dhādhāra |
'sudaṃsāḥ sukarmā śobhanakarmā sañjhindraḥ 'sūrya lokaprakāśakamādityam 'upasam agnihotrādikarmanirvāhakamuṣaḥkālaṃ 'jajāna utpādayāmāsa |
'indrasya karma sukṛtā puruṇi ' ityetatpadakadambakam 'eko dve vasumatī ' ityasmin varge paṭhitam (ṛ. saṃ. 3.30.13) |
atrāpi tadeva' paṭhyate 'kimiti' padakāle notsṛjyata iti cenna |
vākyabhedāt |
tatra 'indrasya karma sukṛtā puruṇi ' ityekaṃ vākyam |
atra tu ' karma sukṛtā ' ityekaṃ vākyaṃ ' puruṇi vratāni ' ityaparam |
kiṃca tatratyaṃ padakadambakaṃ prathamāntam atratyaṃ dvitīyāntam |
ato vākyabhedāt vibhaktibhedācca' padakāle notsṛjyate |

adraugha | satyam | tav | tat | mahi'tvam | sadhaḥ | yat | jātaḥ | arpibaḥ | ha | somam | na | dyāvaḥ | indra | tavasaḥ | te | ojaḥ | na | ahā | na | māsāḥ | śaradaḥ | varanta ||9||
'adraugha drohavarjita he indra 'tava' 'tanmahitvaṃ tādṛśaṃ māhātmyaṃ 'sadhyaṃ yathārtham |

'ha prasiddhau |
kiṃca 'tavasaḥ balavataḥ 'te tava 'ojaḥ sāmarthyaṃ 'dyāvaḥ divaḥ 'divopalakṣitāḥ sarvai lokāḥ 'na 'varanta na vārayanti |
kiṃca 'ahā pratipadādīnyahāni na vārayanti |
tathā 'māsāḥ caitrādyāḥ 'śaradaḥ saṃvatsarāśca tava sāmarthyaṃ na vārayanti |
kiṃtu ' tava sāmarthyamanusmṛtya sarve vartanta iti bhāvaḥ |

1. kha-ga-ja-ṇa-bha-tadevamucyate; ca-na-pha-sa-tadeva ucyate | 2. kha-ga-ca-ja-ṇa-pha-sa-'kimiti' nāsti | 3. kha-ca-pha-bha-'vibhaktibhedācca' nāsti | 4. ta-abhyāsadīrghaḥ | 5. kha-ga-ta-na-la- sa-te tava | 6. gha-na-la-sa-'divaḥ' nāsti | 7. ta-nivārayanti | 8. ta-'kiṃtu' nāsti | 9. kha-ga-ja-ṇa-na-bha-sa-gulmavanaspatīnām |

tvam | sadyaḥ | apibaḥ | jñātaḥ | indra | madāya | somam | parame | vi'omain | yat | ha | dyāvāpṛthivī iti | ā | āvirveśīḥ | atha | abhavaḥ | pūryaḥ | kārudhāyāḥ || 10 ||
he indra sadyaḥ tadānīmeva jñātaḥ parame vyoman uttame sthāne sthitaḥ tvaṃ madāya harṣāya somam apibaḥ |

ojāyamānam| ojaḥśabdādācārārthe 'kartuḥ kyaṅ salopaśca' iti kyaṅpratyayaḥ| salopaśca| ṅittvādadātmanepadam| pratyayasvaraḥ| tadyān | tu iti sautro dhātuḥ| asmāt tṛjantāt 'tucchandasi' itīyasun| 'turiṣṭhemeyaḥsu' iti tuco lopaḥ| bhasaṃjñāyāmorguṇaḥ| ikāralopaśchāndasaḥ| niruccaraḥ| bhūt| bhavaterluṅi rūpam| sphigyā| 'yaci bham' iti bhasaṃjñāyāḥ bādhitatvāt 'svādiṣva- sarvanāmasthāne' iti padasaṃjñāyāṃ jaśtvam| tṛtīyāyāḥ pūrvasavarṇaḥ| kṣām| 'kṣi nivāsagatyoḥ'| 'anyebhyo'pi dṛśyate' iti nirupapadādapi ḍapratyayaḥ| tataṣṭāp| kṣiyanti nivasantyasyāṃ prāṇina iti śrā bhūmiḥ| udāttanivṛttisvareṇāntodāttaḥ| avasthāḥ| 'vama ācchādane' dṛśyasya laṅi anudāttatvāna ātmanepadam| thāmi adāditvācchapo luk| yathogādanighātaḥ| bhaḍāgamamvaraḥ||

he indra ammadīyo'yaṃ yajñaḥ te tava vardhanaḥ haviṣpradānena vardhayitā bhūt hi bhavati khalu| uta api ca sutasomaḥ abhiputasomavān miyedhaḥ mārabhūto'yaṃ yajñaḥ tava priyaḥ| sa tvaṃ yajñiyaḥ yajñārhaḥ man yajñaṃ yajñasya kartāraṃ yajamānaṃ yajñena anuṣṭhīyamānenānena ava phalapradānena rakṣa| kiṃcāyaṃ yajñaḥ te tava vajrmahitye vṛtrahanane karmaṇi āvan rakṣatu| vardhanaḥ| 'vṛdhu vardhane' dṛśyasya ṇyantasya nancāditvāt hyupratyayaḥ| litsvaraḥ| bhūt| hiyogādanighātaḥ| ava| avaterloṭi rūpam| nighātaḥ| san| aseḥ śatṛ rūpam| pratyayasvaraḥ| ahihatye| 'hana hiṃsāgatyoḥ' dṛśyasya bhāve 'hanasta ca' iti kyap nakāraśchāntādeśaḥ| kudunarapadasvaraḥ| āvana| avateśchandase laṅi rūpam| nighātaḥ||
yajñenendramavasā cakre arvāgainaṃ sumnāya narvyase vavṛtyām | yaḥ stomairbhā'vṛdhe pūrvyairbhyo madyumebhiruta nūtanebhiḥ || 13 || yajñena | indram | avasā | ā | cakre | arvāk | ā | enam | sumnāya | narvyase | vavṛtyām | yaḥ | stomaibhiḥ | vavṛdhe | pūrvyaibhiḥ | yaḥ | madyumebhiḥ | uta | nūtanebhiḥ || 13 ||
he indra sarvo yajamānaḥ avasā svarakṣaṇena yajñena indraṃ tvām arvāk ā cakre haviṣpradānenābhimukhaṃ karoti|

1. la-bhasaṃjñāṃ bādhitvā| 2. kha-ga-ca-ja-ṇa-ta-na-bha-sa-nivāse| 3. kha-ja-ṇa-pa-bha-te tava| 4. kha-ga-ca-ṇa-bha-'pyantasya nāsti| 5. ta-asteḥ| 6. ta-kuryāt|

‘ gāṣṭa pratiṣṭhālipsayorgranthe ca ’ | asmān īracpratyayaḥ | ’ | dhakārasya bhakāraḥ ākārasya hrasvaśca ‘ gambhīragambhīrī ’ ( u. sū. 4. 475 ) ityanena nipātyate | ciśvaraḥ | sindhuḥ | ‘ syanda prastravaṇe ’ | ‘ syandeḥ saṃprasāraṇaṃ caśca ’ ityupratyayaḥ : saṃprasāraṇaṃ dukārasya bhakāraḥ | ‘ nit ’ ityanuvṛtterādyudāttaḥ | paripantaḥ | mantaḥ | agneḥ śatari rūpam | ‘ upasargaprādurbhyāṃmannircaparaḥ ’ iti padavam | kecit pare : ‘ kriyānvayam ’ icchanti | tadā padamanupapannam | pari | nipātasvaraḥ | santa : | pratyayasvaraḥ | varanta | ‘ vṛñ ’ varaṇe | bhauvādikaḥ | laṅi rūpam | nidhāna : | aruja : | ‘ rajo bhaṅge ’ ityasya laṅi rūpam | gavyam | gorvikāra ityarthe ‘ gopayamoryana ’ iti yat | ‘ yano'nāva ’ ityādyudāttatvam ||
śunaṃ huvema
parvatānāṃ girīṇāṃ śailānām upasthāt ujjhannirgamya uśatī samudragamanaṃ kāmayamāne| gamane dṛṣṭāntaḥ| aśveva yathā vipite mandirāto vimukte hāsamāne anyonyajavena spardhamāne| yadvā hyanyathāśve iva vaḍave iva svarayā gacchantyau parasparaṃ hyanyanyau| tathā gāveva śubhre| yathā dvau gāvau śobhamānau vartete tadvacchubhre śobhamāne| kiṃca mātarā| yathā mātarau dhenū rihāṇe| antarṇītamanyartho lihiḥ| vasmaṃ jihvayā leḍhumicchantyau śīghraṃ gacchantastadat samudraṃ gantaṃ javāta gacchantyau payasā saṃyukte vipāṭ| kūlavipāṭanāt vipāśanādvā vimocanādvā vipāṭ| sutudrī| su kṣipraṃ tu tnā tunneva dravati gacchatīti sutudrī| etannāmake nadyau javete samudraṃ prati śīghraṃ gacchataḥ| atra nirūktaṃ—parvatānā- mupasthādupasthānādṛśyau kāmayamāne aśva iva vimukte iti vā vipaṇe iti vā hāsamāne hyanyataḥ spardhāyo haryamāṇe vā gāvāviva śubhre śobhane mātare rihāṇe vipāṭchutūdrī payasā prajavete (niru. 9. 39) iti || uśatī| vaśa kāntau| abhya śaturdvitvāna mahiñyāvavi ityādinā prasāraṇam| vipite| piñ bandhane ityasya karmaṇi niṣṭhā| saṃhitāyāṃ parinivibhyaḥ sevasitamaya- siyusaha ityādinā patvam| gatiranantaraḥ iti gateḥ prakṛtisvaraḥ| hāsamāne| hāmatiḥ spardhārkarmā hase hasane vā| śānac| tasya lasārvadhātukasvare kṛtaṃ dhātusvaraḥ| rihāṇe| liha āsvādane| svaritatvādubhayapadī| śānac| adāditvācchapo luk| lakārasya rephaśchāndasaḥ| rephavalamasya ṇatvam| citvādantodāttaḥ| vipāṭ| paṭa gatau| paśa bādhanasparśanayoḥ iti vā ṇyantāvenau viprau| śakārasya vaśrādinā patvam| sutudrī| chāndasī rūpasiddhiḥ| javete| jṛ gatau| auvādiḥ| ḍittvādātmanepadam| āto ḍitaḥ iti iyādeśaḥ| nighātaḥ ||
indraiṣite prasravaṃ bhikṣamāṇe acchā samudraṃ rathyeva yāthaḥ| samārāṇe ūrmibhiḥ pinvamāne anyā vāmanyāmpaiti śubhre || 2 || indraiṣite itīndra'iṣite| pra'sravam| bhikṣamāṇe iti| acchā| samudram| rathye'iva| yāthaḥ| samārāṇe iti sam'aārāṇe| ūrmibhiḥ| pinvamāne iti| anyā| vām| anyām| arpi| eti| śubhre iti || 2 ||
he nadyau indraiṣite indreṇa preṣite prasravaṃ tanmendrasyānujñāṃ bhikṣamāṇe prārthayamāne yuvāṃ samudram acchā ābhimukhyena yāthaḥ gacchathaḥ|
tatra dṛṣṭāntaḥ|
rathyeva iti|
yathā rathinau lakṣyaṃ deśamabhigacchatastadvat|
kiṃ kurvatyau|
samārāṇe parasparaṃ saṃgacchantyau ūrmibhiḥ taraṅgaiḥ pinvamāne parisarapradeśaṃ saṃtarpayantyau śubhre śobhamāne|
yuvāṃ samudraṃ gacchatha iti pūrvaṇānvayaḥ|
tathā vāṃ yuvayormadhye anyā ekā anyām aparām nadīm apyeti apigacchati|
parasparamaikyamāpadyata ityarthaḥ ||

1. ta-la-girīṇāṃ nāsti| 2. kha-ga-ca-pa-pha-bha-uśatī vipāṭchutūdrī; dha-ja-ṇa-vipāṭchutūdrī uśatī| 3. ta-anyonyaṃ javena| 4. ja-ṇa-hyanyanyau nāsti| 5. kha-ga-pra-ca-ja-ṇa-pa-pha-bha-gantuṃ prajavete| 6. kha-ga-ca-ja-ṇa-bha-śu; ta-tujnā| 7. ta-etannāmike| 8. ja-ṇa-samudraprāntam| 9. la-ityasya| 10. ta-svaratvam| 11. kha-ga-ja-ṇa-spaśa| 12. kha-ga-ca-ja-ṇa-bha-iti nāsti| 13. ta-niṣṭhāyām|
‘tṛtīyā karmaṇi’ iti pūrvapadasvaraḥ | prasavam | ‘pū preraṇe’ ityasya ap | thāthādisavaraḥ | bhikṣamāṇe | ‘bhikṣa yācayāyām’ | ātmanepadī | śānaco lasārvadhātukasvareṇānudāttatve dhātusvaraḥ | ryaiva | rathyyamau | tasyedam ityarthe ‘rathādyat’ iti yatpratyayaḥ | tiścaritaḥ | ivena vibhaktyalopaḥ ityādi | yādhaḥ | yāterleṭi rūpam | samārāne | ‘ṛ gatau’ ityasya liṭ | saṃpūrvasyāteḥ ‘samo gamiḥ’ ityādinā ātmanepadatvāt tasya kānajādeśaḥ | ‘ṛcchahṛtām’ iti guṇaḥ | pivvamāne | ‘pitra mecane’ | bhūvādiḥ | lasārvadhātukasvareṇa śānaco'nudāttatve dhātusvaraḥ 3 ||

dyorbahuvacanaṃ pūjārtham | tāsāmasmākaṃ sargatakaḥ sarge gamane pravṛtta: prasavaḥ udyoga: na vartave nivartanāya' na bhavati | kiṃyu: kimicchan asau vipra: brāhmaṇa: nadya: nadī: asmān johavīti bhṛśamāhvayati || enā | etacchabdasya tṛtīyāyā enādeśa: | supāṃ suluk ' iti tṛtīyāyā ājādeśa: | aḍidam ' iti vibhakterudāttatvam | pivamānā: | piva secane ' | devakṛtam | tṛtīyā karmaṇi ' iti pūrvapadaprakṛtisvara: | vartave | vṛta vartane ' | tumarthe tavenpratyaya: | nisvara: | sargataka: | ke ca ' iti pūrvapadaprakṛtisvara: | kiṃyu: | kimicchan | kvaci māntāvyayapratiṣedha: ' (pā. sū. 3. 1. 8. 1) iti chāndasāvādana pratiṣedho na bhavatīti kvaca | kyācchandasi ' ityapratyaya: | pratyayasvara: | nadya: | chāndaso yaṇādeśa: | johavīti | heja: yaṅluki abhyastasya ca ' iti saṃprasāraṇe kṛte guṇo yaṅluko: ' ityabhyāsasya guṇa: | yaṅo vā ' iti iḍāgama: | guṇa: | nicāta: ||

nadyaḥ pratyūcuḥ | he viśvāmitra vajrabāhuḥ | vajrayukto bāhuryasyāsau vajrabāhuḥ | tādṛśo balavān indraḥ nadyaḥ asmān aradat | radatiḥ khanatikarmā | avanat | kathamakhanat | ucyate | nadīnāṃ śabdakāriṇāmapāṃ paridhiṃ parito nihitamudakamantaḥ kṛtvā vartamānamityarthaḥ | tādṛśaṃ vṛtram | vṛṇotyākāśamiti vṛtro meghaḥ | taṃ megham apāhan jaghāna | tasmin hate āpaḥ patitāḥ | nābhiragacchantībhirvyaṃ khātaḥ | evaṃ meghahanadvāreṇāvanat | na kevalamakhanat kiṃ tarhi savitā sarvasya jagataḥ prerakaḥ | supāṇiḥ śobhanahastaḥ utpattisthitikartṛtvāttādṛśaḥ | devaḥ dyotamānaḥ indraḥ asmān anayat | meghabhedanaṃ kṛtvā udakapreraṇena samudramapūrayat | tasya tādṛśasāmarthyenasya indrasya pramave abhyanujñāyāṃ vartamānāḥ urvīḥ udakaiḥ prabhṛtāḥ vayaṃ yāmaḥ gacchāmaḥ | na tatra vacanādaparamāmaṃha | uktārthaṃ yāsko bravīti—' indro asmānaradadvajrabāhū radatiḥ khanatikarmāpāhantya paridhi nadīnāmiti vyākhyānam | devo'nayatsavitā supāṇiḥ kalyāṇapāṇiḥ | pāṇiḥ daṇāyateḥ pūjākarmaṇaḥ pragṛhya pāṇi devān pūjayanti | tasya vayaṃ prasave yāma urvīrhyaḥ ' ( niru. 5. 26 ) iti || aradat | radaterlaṅi rūpam | vajrabāhuḥ | bahuvrīhau pūrvapadasvaraḥ | ahan | hanterlaṅi rūpam | nighātaḥ | paridhim | ' ḍudhāñ dhāraṇapoṣaṇayoḥ ' dṛśyasmāt karmaṇyupasarge ' ghoḥ kiḥ ' iti vipratyayaḥ | āno lopaḥ | kruduttarapadasvaraḥ | anayana | nayaterlaṅi rūpam | supāṇiḥ | ' daṇa vyavahāre ranutau ca ' | ' aśipaṇākhyo ruḍāyalukī ca ' ( u. sū. 4. 572 ) inīṇ | āyaluka | bahuvrīhau ' nasubhyām ' iti svaraḥ | prasave | ' pū preraṇe ' | ' ṛdorap ' iti bhāve'ppratyayaḥ | ' thāthaghantā ' ityuttara- padāntodāttatvam | yāmaḥ | ' yā prāpaṇe ' dṛśyasya laṭi rūpam | nighātaḥ | urvīḥ | uruśabdāt ' voto guṇavacanāt ' iti ṅīp | ' vā chandasi ' iti savarṇadīrghaḥ | pratyayasvaraḥ ||

o pu sva̭māraḥ kāra॒ve śṛṇota yayau vo̭ durāda̭nasā rathḙna | ni ṣū na̭madhvaṃ bha̭vatā supā॒rā a̭dhoakṣāḥ sindhavaḥ srotayābhiḥ || 9 || o iti | su | sva̭sāraḥ | kāra॒ve | śṛṇota | yayau | vaḥ | durāt | anasā | rathḙna | ni | su | na̭madhvam | bha̭vata | su'pā॒rāḥ | adhaḥ'akṣāḥ | sindhavaḥ | srotayābhiḥ || 9 ||
viśvāmitro nadīḥ pratyuvāca |
'svasāraḥ bhaginyaḥ 'sindhavaḥ he nadyaḥ 'kārave stotraṃ kurvāṇasya mama vacanaṃ 'su' suṣṭhu 'o' 'śṛṇota śṛṇuteva' |
'anasā śakaṭena 'rathena ca saha 'dūrāt viprakṛṣṭādvāt 'vaḥ yuṣmān 'yayau prāpto'smi |
yūyaṃ 'su suṣṭhu 'ni 'namadhvam ātmanā svayaṃ prahāḥ 'bhavata |
tathā 'supārāḥ |
rathādīnāṃ tīrāt sukhenārohaṇārohaṇe yathā syātāṃ tathā śobhanarodhasaśca bhavata |
kiṃca yūyaṃ 'srotayābhiḥ sravaṇaśīlābhirvāḥ 'adhoakṣāḥ rathā'syākṣasyādhastādbhavanti tadā rathādīni netuṃ śakyante |
tasmāt tatparimāṇodakāḥ 'bhavateti arthābhiprāyaḥ '||
o iti pragṛhyasaṃjñā |
śṛṇota |
'śru śravaṇe ' ityasya loṭi tapratyayasya 'tasanasanathanāśca ' iti tatrādeśaḥ |
pittvādguṇaḥ |
nighātaḥ |

ekādeśasvaraḥ |

pratyayasvaraḥ ||
ā tai kāre śṛṇavāmā vacāṃsi yayārtha durāda̭nasā rathḙna | ni tai na̭sai pīpyāneva yoṣā maryoyeva kanyā śaśvacai tai || 10 || ā | te | kāre iti | śṛṇavāma | vacāṃsi | yayārtha | durāt | anasā | rathḙna | ni | te | na̭sai | pīpyānā'iva | yoṣā | maryo'iva | kanyā | śaśvacai | tu iti te || 10 ||
nadyaḥ pūrvaṃ viśvāmitraṃ pratyākhyāyānayāṃ tasya vākyamāśṛṇuvuḥ |
'kāro stotraṃ kurvāṇaṃ 'viśvāmitra 'te tava 'vacāṃsi imāni vākyāni 'ā 'śṛṇavāma śṛṇumaḥ |
'tava samīhitaṃ prayojanaṃ kurma ityarthaḥ |
'anamā śakaṭena 'rathena ca saha 'yayātha |
yato 'dūrāt āgato'si |
vayaṃ ca 'te 'yavartha 'ni 'naṃsai nīcainemāma |
pratyekavivakṣayā 'atraikavacanam |
rathena gantuṃ gādhodakā bhavāmetyarthaḥ |
1. kha-ga-ca-ja-ṇa-na-pha-bha-sa-śṛṇota su suṣṭhu ośṛṇotaiva | 2. ja-ṇa-prahībhavata | 3. kha-ga-ca-ja-ṇa-na-la- parimāṇodakāḥ | 4. gha-la-abhiprāyaḥ | 5. ta-mu | 6. la-sa-viśvāmitravākyam | 7. kha-ga-ca-ja-ṇa-bha-la- 'tava ' nāsti | 8. kha-ga-ca-bha-āśṛṇumaḥ; ja-ṇa-āśṛṇuma | 9. ja-ṇa-la-pratyekaṃ vivakṣayā |
tatra dṛṣṭāntaḥ |
pīpyāneva yoṣā |
pīpyānā putraṃ stanaṃ pāyayantī yoṣā mātā yathā prahībhavati |
dṛṣṭāntāntaram |
yathā kanyā yuvatiḥ maryāyeva manudhyāya pitre ātre vā śaśvacca pariṣvajanāya nannībhavati tadvat te tvadarthaṃ prahībhāvam |
te iti punaruktirādarartham |
etāmṛcayāska evaṃ vyācaṣṭe- ‘āśruvāma te kāro vacanāni yāhi durādanasā ca rathena ca ninamāma te pāyayamāneva yoṣā putraṃ maryāyeva kanyā pariṣvajanāya ninamā iti vā’ (niru. 2.27) iti kāro |
‘saṃbuddhau śākalya- syeto’ (pā. sū. 1. 1. 16) iti pragrahasaṃjñā |

yat | aṅga | tvā | bharatāḥ | sa॒m'tareyuḥ | gavyam | grāmaḥ | iṣitaḥ | indra̭'jūtaḥ | a॒rṣāt | aham | pra॒'savaḥ | sarga̭'taktaḥ | ā | vaḥ | vṛṇe | su॒'matim | ya॒jñiyā̭nām ||11||
viśvāmitro nadīḥ pratyuvāca |
‘aṅga ityāmantraṇe |
he nadyaḥ ‘yat yasmāt yuṣmābhiruttīryoḥ mamottaraṇamabhyanujñātaṃ tasmāt bharatāḥ : bharatakulajā madīyāḥ sarve tvā parasparamekatāmāpannā nadīṃ tvāṃ saṃtareyuḥ : samyagguttīrṇā bhaveyuḥ |
tadeva viśinaṣṭi |
‘gavyam gā udakāni tarītumicchan iṣitaḥ : tvāmabhyanujñātaḥ indrajūtaḥ : yuṣmākaṃ pravartakendreṇa ca preritaḥ grāmaḥ : bharatānāṃ saṃghaḥ arṣāt maṃtarena |
yataḥ sargataktaḥ : gamanāya pravṛttaḥ prasavaḥ : tepāmudyogaḥ ahaṃ pūrvaṃ yuṣmābhiranujñātaḥ |
ahaṃ tu yajñiyānāṃ yajñārhāṇāṃ vaḥ : yuṣmākaṃ sumatiṃ śobhanāṃ stutim ā vṛṇe sarvataḥ saṃbhaje ||

atāriṣurbharatā gavyavaḥ samabhakta vipraḥ sumatiṃ nadīnām | pra pinvadhvamiṣayantīḥ surādhā ā vakṣaṇāḥ pṛṇadhvaṃ yāta śīrdham || 12 || atāriṣuḥ | bharatāḥ | gavyavaḥ | sam | abhakta | vipraḥ | sumatim | nadīnām | pra | pinvadhvam | iṣayantīḥ | surādhāḥ | ā | vakṣaṇāḥ | pṛṇadhvam | yāta | śīrdham ||12||
^gavyavaḥ gā ātmana icchantaḥ ^bharatāḥ bharatakulajāḥ sarve ^atāriṣuḥ tāṃ nadīṃ samataran | ^vipraḥ medhāvī viśvāmitraḥ ^nadīnāṃ ^sumatiṃ śobhanāṃ stutiṃ ^samabhakta samabhajata | yūyaṃ tu yathā- pūrvam ^iṣayantīḥ kulyādvārā atraṃ kurvāṇā ata eva ^surādhāḥ śobhanadhanopetā yūyaṃ ^vakṣaṇāḥ kṛtrimasaritaḥ kulyāḥ ^pra ^pinvadhvaṃ prakarṣeṇa tarpayata | ^ā ^pṛṇadhvaṃ tāḥ sarvataḥ pūrayata ca | ^śīrdhaṃ śīghraṃ ^yāta gacchata ca || atāriṣuḥ | ‘tṛ plavanataraṇayoḥ’ ityasya luṅi ‘sici vṛddhiḥ parasmaipadeṣu’ iti vṛddhiḥ | aḍāgamasvaraḥ | gavyavaḥ | supaḥ kyac | ‘kyācchandasi’ ityupratyayaḥ | tasya svaraḥ | abhakta | ‘bhaja sevāyām’ ityasya luṅi sivaḥ ^ ‘alo'li’ iti lopaḥ ^ | pādāditvādanudāttaḥ | pinvadhvam | ‘pivi secane’ ityasya loṭi rūpam | nighātaḥ | iṣayantīḥ | iṣaṃ kurvantyaḥ | ‘tatkaroti’ iti ṇic | ‘ṇāviṣṭhavatprātipadikasya’ iti iṣṭhavadbhāvāt ‘ṭeḥ’ iti ṭilopaḥ | ‘vā chandasi’ iti satvagaṃdīrghaḥ | pratyayasvaraḥ | pṛṇadhvam | ‘pṛṇa prīṇane’ | loṭi rūpam | vyatyayenātmanepadam | vākyabhedādanudāttaḥ | yāta | ‘yā prāpaṇe’ ityasya loṭi rūpam | atrāpi na nighātaḥ | śīrdham | ‘śībhra kathane’ | śrādhayate'nena tadvāniti karaṇe ghañ | jinsvaraḥ ||
ud ūrmiḥ śamyā hantvāpo yoktrāṇi muñcata | mārduṣkṛtau vyenasādhyau śūnamāratām || 13 || ud | vaḥ | ūrmiḥ | śamyāḥ | hantu | āpaḥ | yoktrāṇi | muñcata | mā | arduḥ'kṛtau | vi'enasā | adyau | śūnam | ā | artam || 13 ||

gavyoḥ ' ityasya loṭi rūpam | nighātaḥ | yoktrāṇi | ' yujira yoge ' | karaṇe ' dāgnīśasayuyuja ' ityādinā chapratyayaḥ | nisvaraḥ | sunna | ' succa mokṣaṇe ' | nighātaḥ | adupakṛttau | ' isusoḥ sāmarthye ' iti visarjanīyasya patvam | vyenasā | bahuvrīhau pūrvapadasvaraḥ | supo ḍādeśaḥ | adyau | ' hana hiṃsāgatyoḥ ' ityasya nappūrvasya ' adyādayaśca ' iti nipātanāt yak | kittvā- dupadhālopaḥ | ' ho hanteḥ ' iti ghanvam | sarvavidhīnāṃ chandasi vikalpitatvādna auhaḥ | śībhāvā- bhāvaḥ | ekādeśasvaraḥ | śūnyam | dhayateḥ ' napuṃsake bhāve ktaḥ ' iti ktaḥ | yajāditān saṃprasāraṇam | ' halaḥ ' iti dīrghatvam | ' oditaśca ' iti niṣṭhātvam | ' niṣṭhā ca dvayajanāta ' ityādyudāttaḥ | aratām | ' ṛ gatau ' ityasya luṅi caleḥ ' matiśāstyatibhyaśca ' ityaḍādeśaḥ | ' ṛdaśo'ṅi guṇaḥ ' | ' na māṅayoge ' ityaḍabhāvaḥ | nighātaḥ | || 14 ||
' indraḥ pūrbhin ' ityekādaśarcaṃ pañcamaṃ sūktaṃ vaiśāmitraṃ ṛṣamaindram |
atrānukramaṇikā - ' indraḥ pūrbhidekādaśa ' iti |
agniṣṭome mādhyandinasavane brāhmaṇācchaṃsiśastre ' indraḥ pūrbhin ' iti sūktam |
' hotrakāṇām ' iti khaṇḍe sūtritam - ' indraḥ pūrbhidudu brahmāṇi ' ( āśva. śrau. 5. 16 ) iti |
pṛṣṭhābhipavapaḍahayoḥ prathame'hani mādhyandinasavane brāhmaṇācchaṃsiśastre cāturviṃśikātidiṣṭamya ahīnasūktasya sthāne trīṇi saṃpātasūktāni |
tatredaṃ prathamam |

udvidalabhidorniṣkevalye etatsūktaṃ nividdhānīyam |
sūtritaṃ ca - ' indra somamindraḥ pūrbhiditi madhyaṃdinaḥ ' ( āśva. śrau. 9. 8 ) iti ||
indraḥ pūrbhidātirdāsamkairvidvadmurdayamāno vi śatrūn |
