he indra bhūpan svāmalaṃkurvannaha maghavasya mahanīyasya stutyasya taviṣasya balavataḥ te tava jūtiṃ manasā preritāṃ vācaṃ stutilakṣaṇāṃ amṛtāya annāya pra iyarmi prakarṣeṇoccārayāmi | mānuṣīṇāṃ kṣitīnāṃ manojātānāṃ manuṣyāṇām uta api ca daivīnāṃ devasambandhinīnāṃ viśām prajānāṃ pūrvayāvā agrato gantā tvam asi || maghavasya | ‘maha pūjāyām’ | ‘naherhalopaśca’ (u. sū. 5. 709) iti ‘halopaḥ’ ityanuvṛttau ‘maheśva’ iti kvapratyayo halopaśca | mahate pūjyate savairiti makhaḥ | svāntamyāsādeḥ’ ityantodāttatvam | yadyapi makārādirbhavati tathāpi vacanamāmarthyādbhavati | taviṣasya | tavateḥ ṭipcapratyayaḥ | citsvaraḥ | jūtim | ‘ūtiñūti’ ityādinā cinaḥ udāttatvam | iyarmi | anirjuhātyādiḥ | ‘atipivatyośca’ ityabhyāsasyetvam | ‘abhyāsasya’ itīyaḍādeśaḥ | pādādisvānna nighātaḥ | abhyastasvaraḥ | bhūpan | ‘bhūpa alaṃkāre’ ityamya śatar rūpam | śata- lamārvadhātukasvare’ dhātusvaraḥ | asi | asterlaṭri rūpam | nighātaḥ | mānuṣīṇām | ‘manorjātau’ ityañ | pugāgamaḥ | ‘ṭiḍḍhāṇañ’ iti ṅīp | jhisvaraḥ | daivīnām | ‘devādyañau’ ityañ | atrāpi pūrvavat ṅīp | jhisvaraḥ | pūrvayāvā | ‘yā prāpaṇe’ ityasmāt ‘āto manina kanivani- paśca’ iti vanip | kuduttarapadasvaraḥ ||

śardhanītiḥ | nayanaṃ nītiḥ karma | śardha pravṛddhaṃ nītiḥ karma yasya sa tathoktaḥ | saḥ indraḥ vṛtram asuram avṛṇot balenāruṇat | tathā varṣaṇītiḥ yuddhe paraprahārāṇāṃ nivārakakarmā indraḥ māyinām | dvitīyārthe ṣaṣṭhī | māyino'surān pra amināt prakarṣeṇāvadhīt | uśadhak śatruvadhaṃ kāmayamānaḥ indraḥ vaneṣu gūḍhacāriṇamasuraṃ dhyasaṃ vigatāṃsaṃ yathā bhavatā tathā ahan jaghāna | atha tasmin rātricare gavāmapahartari hate sati rāmyāṇām | ramaṇaṃ strībhiḥ saha krīḍā rāmaḥ | tamarhantīti rāmyāḥ rātrayaḥ | tāsāṃ rātrīṇāmantaḥsthitāḥ dhenāḥ gāḥ āviḥ akṛṇot prakaṭā akarot | rātrīṇām asurākrāntatve taittirīyakama—‘ahardevānāmāsīdrātrirasurāṇāṃ te'surā yadevānāṃ vittaṃ vedyamāmīttena saha rātri prāviśanniti ye divā stotāro rāmyāsteṣāṃ vāco dhenāḥ’ iti || akṛṇota | ‘vṛja varaṇe’ ityasya laṅi rūpam | nighātaḥ | śardhanītiḥ | śardhatirasāhārthaḥ | asmāt karmaṇi ghañ | bahuvrīhau pūrvapadasvaraḥ | amināt | ‘miñ hiṃsāyām’ ityasya laṅi ‘mānāternigame’ iti hrasvaḥ | nighātaḥ | ahan | hanterlaṅi rūpam | uśadhak | ‘vaśa kāntau’ | guṇodaditvāta rūpa- siddhiḥ | āviḥ | svarādiṣvantodāttatvena paṭhitatvādantodāttaḥ | dhenāḥ | ‘dhe pāne’ | ‘gheṭa iñ’ ( u. sū. 3. 291 ) iti namratyaya īkārāntādeśaḥ | guṇaḥ | dhayanti tāmiti dhenā vāk | dhenā jigāti’ (ṛ. saṃ. 1. 2. 3) ityatra pratyayasvareṇodāttaḥ | atra vyatyayenādyudāttatvam | akṛṇota | ‘kṛvi hiṃsākaraṇayoḥ’ | nighātaḥ | rāmyāṇām | ‘ramu krīḍāyām’ | rāmamahantītyarthe ‘chandasi ca’ iti yapratyayaḥ | tasya svaraḥ | yadvā rameḥ antaṇītaṇyarthāna ‘adhyādayaśca’ iti yapratyayaḥ | ‘pramayati bhūtāni naktaṃcārīṇyuparamayatītarāṇi’ ( niru. 2. 18 ) iti yāskaḥ | varṇyatyayaḥ ||

svarṣāḥ svargapradaḥ |

indraḥ | oṣadhīḥ | asinot | ahāni | vanaspatīn | asinot | antārekṣam | bibheda | valam | nunude | vi'vācaḥ | atha | abhavat | damitā | abhikratūnām || 10 ||
tādṛśaḥ indraḥ oṣadhīḥ pakphalasaṃyuktāstṛṇagulmādikāḥ asinot prāṇināmupabhogārthe prādāt |

1 ||
tiṣṭha | harī iti | rathe | ā | yujyamānā | yāhi | vāyuḥ | na | ni'yutaḥ | naḥ | accha | pibāsi | andhaḥ | abhi'spṛṣṭaḥ | asme iti | indra | svāhā | rarimā | te | madāya || 1 ||
viśvāmitraḥ stauti |
he indra rathe syandane yujyamānau harī aśvau abhilakṣya kaṃcitkālam ā tiṣṭha |
tatra dṛṣṭāntaḥ vāyurna iti |
yathā vāyu rathe niyujyamānāḥ niyutaḥ vaḍavā abhilakṣya kaṃcitkālaṃ tiṣṭhati tadvat |
atha rāruḍhastvaṃ naḥ asmān accha abhilakṣya ā yāhi śīghramāgaccha |
āgamya ca asme asmābhiḥ abhispṛṣṭaḥ anujñātastvam andhaḥ somalakṣaṇamannaṃ pibāsi piba |
svāhā |
dattaśrāyaṃ somaḥ |
vayaṃ te tava madāya harṣāya svāhākṛtamimaṃ somaṃ rarima dadāma |
atra 'yāhi vāyurna niyuto no accha
brahmaṇā taṃ brahmayujāṃ yunajmi harī sakhāyā sadhamādaṃ āśu |
sthiraṃ rathaṃ sukhamindrādhitiṣṭhanprajānanvidvā~ upa yāhi somam ||
4 ||
brahmaṇā | te | brahmayujā | yunajmi | harī iti | sakhāyā | sadhamāde | āśu iti | sthiram | ratham | su'dham | indra | adhi'tiṣṭhan | pra'jānan | vidvān | upa | yāhi | somam || 4 ||
ha 'indra 'brahmayujā brahmaṇā mantreṇa yoktavyau 'sadhamāde |

sahamādaḥ sadhamādaḥ |
tasmin yuddhe 'sakhāyā samānaprasiddhā ata eva 'āśū kṣipragantavau 'te tava 'harī aśvau 'brahmaṇā stotreṇa 'yunajmi rathe saṃyojayāmi |
'sthiraṃ sudṛḍhaṃ 'sudhaṃ suṣṭhudāraṃ tādṛśaṃ 'ratham 'adhitiṣṭhan ārūḍhastvaṃ somapānārthamāyāhi |
'prajānan ihyāgamanam ||
brahmayujā |
yujerṇādikaḥ karmaṇi kvip |
tavāyaṃ somastvamevāvāś śaśvattamaṃ sumanā asya pāhi | asmin‌yajñe barhiṣyā niṣadyā dadhiṣvameṃ jaṭhara indumindra || 6 || tava | ayam‌ | somaḥ | tvam‌ | ā | ihi | arvāṅ‌ | śaśvattamam‌ | sumanāḥ | asya | pāhi | asmin‌ | yajñe | barhiṣi | ā | niṣadya | dadhiṣva | imam‌ | jaṭhare | indum‌ | indra || 6 ||
he indra ayaṃ somaḥ tava tvadarthamabhiputaḥ | tvam arvāṅ‌ somābhimukhyena ā ihi āyāhi | āgatya ca sumanāḥ śobhanamanasko bhūtvā asya imaṃ somaṃ śaśvattamaṃ bahutamaṃ pāhi piba | barhiṣi pravṛddhe asmin yajñe niṣadya upaviśya induṃ kledanaśīlam imaṃ somaṃ jaṭhare ā dadhiṣva dhāraya | yathā nābherdhastānna gacchati tathā dhāraya | ‘aṅ‌gobhirāgahi yajñebhiḥ’ (ṛ. saṃ. 10. 14. 5) ityatra na yajñaviśeṣaṇaṃ barhiḥ bhinnarūpaṃ vā tat ityatāṃ notsahyate | ihi | ‘iṇ gatau’ | asya loṭi rūpam | nighātaḥ | śaśvattamam | ucchādivādantodāttatvam | pāhi | ‘pā pāne’ ityasya loṭi rūpam | ‘bahula chandasi’ iti śapo luka | nighātaḥ | niṣadya | saderlyapi ‘madirprateḥ’ iti patvam | limsvaraḥ | dadhiṣva | dadhāterloṭi ‘chandasyubhayathā’ iti bhāsa ārdhadhātu katvādiḍāgamaḥ | pratyayasvaraḥ | indum | ‘undī kledane’ | ‘undīccādeḥ’ ityupratyayaḥ | dhātorgadaḥ ikārādeśaḥ | ‘nit’ ityanuvṛtterādyudāttaḥ ||
stīrṇaṃ tai barhiḥ suta indra somaḥ kṛtā dhānā attave te haribhyām‌ | tadaukase puruśākāya vṛṣṇe marutvate tubhyaṃ rātā havīṃṣi || 7 || stīrṇam‌ | te | barhiḥ | sutaḥ | indra | somaḥ | kṛtāḥ | dhānāḥ | attave | te | hari'bhyām‌ | tataḥ'okase | puru'śākāya | vṛṣṇe | marutvate | tubhyam‌ | rātā | havīṃṣi || 7 ||
he indra te tavopaveśanārthaṃ barhiḥ stīrṇaṃ vistṛtam |
tathā somaḥ tvadarthaṃ sutaḥ abhiputaḥ |
te tava haribhyām attave bhakṣaṇārthaṃ dhānāḥ bhṛṣṭayavāḥ kṛtāḥ saṃpāditāḥ |
tadaukase tata barhiḥ oko nilayo yasya tasmai puruśākāya bahubhiḥ stutyāya yadvā bahubhakṣāya vṛṣṇe kāmānāṃ varṣitre marutvate maruto devaviśaḥ |
tadvate |
evaṃ bhūtāya tubhyaṃ havīṃṣi somādyādīni rātā asmābhi rdattāni |
tāni sevasveti bhāvaḥ ||

1. kha-sa somaḥ | 2. kha-ga-ca-ja-ṇa-pha-tu | 3. sva-ga-ca-ja-ṇa-na-bha-sa-rūpam | 4. na-bha-la- geḥprateḥ | 5. kha-ga-ca-ja-ṇa-pa-bha- ‘kāmanāṃ’ nāsti | 6. la--ityasya nighātam |
imaṃ naraḥ parvatāstubhyamāpaḥ samindra gobhirmadhumantamakran | tasyāgatyā sumanasā ṛṣva pāhi prajānanvidvānpathyā i anu svāḥ || 8 || imam | naraḥ | parvatāḥ | tubhyam | āpaḥ | sam | indra | gobhiḥ | madhu'mantam | akran | tasya | ā'gatya | su'manāḥ | ṛṣva | pāhi | pra'jānan | vidvān | pathyāḥ | anu | svāḥ ||8||
he indra naraḥ karmaṇāṃ netāro'dhvarasya parvatāḥ grāvāṇaḥ āpaḥ cete sarve saṃbhūya tubhyaṃ tvadartham imaṃ somaṃ gobhiḥ payobhiḥ madhumantaṃ mādhuryopetaṃ sam akran samyakārpuḥ |
ṛṣva darśanīya he indra sumanasā śobhanamanaskaḥ vidvān karmābhijñaḥ svāḥ svakīyāḥ pathyāḥ vaidike pathi karmaṇi sādhubhūtāḥ stutīḥ anu prajānan tvam āgatya tasya pāhi imamabhiputaṃ somaṃ piba ||

he indra yān marutaḥ some somapānaviṣaye ābhajaḥ samabhāvayaḥ ye ca marutaḥ tvām avardhan yuddhe protsāhapādanenāvardhayan ye ca marutaḥ te tava gaṇaḥ abhavan sahāyā abhavannityarthaḥ |
he indra evaṃvidhaiḥ tebhiḥ tairmarudbhiḥ sajoṣāḥ saṃgataḥ san etam abhiputaṃ somaṃ vāvaśānaḥ kāmayamānastvam agreḥ āhutyadhikaraṇabhūtasyāhavanīyasya jvālārūpayā jihvayā imaṃ piba ||

śunaṃ huvema maghavānmindramasminbhare nṛtamaṃ vājasātau | śṛṇvantamugramūtaye samatsu ghanantaṃ vṛtrāṇi saṃjitaṃ dhanānām || 11 || śunam | huvema | maghavān | indram | asmin | bhare | nṛtamam | vāja'sātau | śṛṇvantam | ugram | ūtaye | samatsu | ghanantam | vṛtrāṇi | sam'jitam | dhanānām || 11 || pūrvaṃ vyākhyātā^3 || || 18 ||
'imām pu' ityekādaśacaṃ saptamaṃ sūktaṃ traiṣṭubhamaindram |
atrānukramaṇikā— 'imām papmānyāṃ ghoro'paśyan mā nirdahecchamyamāneti śrūyate' iti |
daśamyā āṅgiraso ghora ṛṣiḥ |
śiṣṭānāṃ vidhā- mitraḥ |
agniṣṭome mādhyandinasavane'cchāvākaśastre daśamīvarjitam 'imām pu' iti sūktam |
sūtritaṃ ca^— 'imām pivyupottamāmuddharetsarvatra' ( āśva. śrau. 5. 16 ) iti |
pṛṣṭhābhiṣavapaḍahayoḥ mādhyandinasavane' acchāvākaśastre cāturviṃśikātidiṣṭasyāhīnasūktasya sthāne trīṇi saṃpātasūktāni |
tatropattamāvarjametataprathamaṃ sūktam |

imām ṣu prabhṛti sātayai dhāḥ śaśvacchaśvadatibhiryārdamānaḥ | sutesute vāvṛdhe vardhanebhiḥ karmabhirmahadbhiḥ suśruto bhūta || 1 || imām | ū~ iti | su | ṣaḍabhṛtim | sātayai | dhāḥ | śaśvat'śaśvat | ūti'bhiḥ | yārdamānaḥ | sute'sute | vavṛdhe | vardhanebhiḥ | yaḥ | karmabhiḥ | mahat'bhiḥ | su'śrutaḥ | bhūta || 1 ||

prabhṛtiḥ somaḥ mnṛtirvā | 'imām imaṃ somam imāṃ' stutiṃ vā' 'sātaye asmākaṃ dhanalābhārthaṃ 'su 'dhāḥ suṣu saṃpādaya | tathā sa indraḥ 'sutesute some abhipute sati 'vardhanebhiḥ vardhanakāribhiḥ somāvyādibhiḥ havirbhiḥ 'vāvṛdhe' vardhate | 'yaḥ indraḥ 'mahadbhiḥ 'karmabhiḥ asuravadhameghabhedanādilakṣaṇairvyāpāraiḥ 'suśrutaḥ loke prasiddhaḥ 'bhūta | sa indra iti pūrveṇānvayaḥ' || prabhṛtim | 'tādau ca niti kyatau ' iti gateḥ prakṛtisvaratvam | dhāḥ | dadhāteśchāndase luṅi rūpam | nighātaḥ | śaśvacchaśvat | 'nityavīpsayoḥ ' iti dvivacanam | yādamānaḥ | 'duyācṛ yācañāyām' | śānaci rūpam | cakārasya dakāraśchāndasaḥ | vāvṛdhe | 'vṛdha vardhane ' ityasmān liṭi rūpam | tujāditvābhyāsasya dīrghaḥ saṃhitāyām | nighātaḥ | vardhanebhiḥ | vardhanerdhantamasya nandyādisyān lyuṭ | litsvaraḥ | suśrutaḥ | 'śru śravaṇe ' ityasmān karmaṇi ktaḥ | 'gatiranantaraḥ ' iti gatisvaraḥ | bhūta | bhavaterluṅi rūpam ||

yo'yamindraḥ mahān atiśayitasāmarthyavān vṛjane balapalabdhite yuddhe amatraḥ śatrūṇāmabhi- bhavitrā ata eva virapśī virapaṇaśīlo bhujāsphālanena yuddhārthaṃ śatrūṇāmāhnānakārī | yadvā vividhaṃ rapaṇaṃ stutilakṣaṇaṃ yasyeti | savaiḥ stutya ityarthaḥ | tādṛśasyendrasya ugram udvarṇaṃ śavaḥ balaṃ dhṛṣṇu dharṣaṇaśīlam ojaḥ parākramalakṣaṇaṃ tejaḥ pasyate sarvatra prasarati | enam etādṛśamahimopetamindraṃ pṛthivī vistīrṇā bhūmiḥ na vivyāca na vyāpnoti | ahaśabdaḥ evārthe | cana ityapyarthe | dyaurapi na vyāpnotītyarthaḥ | yat yadā somāsaḥ svāhākṛtāḥ somāḥ haryaśvam indram amandan amādayan | tadā tamenaṃ dyāvāpṛthivyau vyāptuṃ na śakuta ityarthaḥ || amatraḥ | ama gatyādiṣu | ‘aminakṣiyaji’ ityādinā atranpratyayaḥ | nitsvaraḥ | virapśī | rapa lapa vyaktāyāṃ vāci | auṇādikaḥ śapratyayaḥ | vividhaṃ rapantīti virapsāḥ stotāraḥ | te'sya santītyata iniḥ | yadvā vividhaṃ rapaṇaṃ virapśam | tadasyāstīti | prāyasvaraḥ | patyate | vyatyayena kartari yak | nighātaḥ | dhṛṣṇu | ‘dhṛṣvā prāgalbhye’ | trasigṛdhidhṛṣipeḥ kupratyayaḥ | ‘rabhyām’ iti ṇatvam | pratyayasvaraḥ | vivyāca | vyacerliṭi ṇali | ‘liṭyabhyāsasyobhayeṣām’ ityabhyāsasya saṃprasāraṇam | nighātaḥ | caneti nipātasamudāyaścārthe vartate | evamādivādantodāttaḥ | amandan | madi stutyādiṣu ityasya laṅi rūpam | yadyogādanighātaḥ ||

2. kha-ga-ca-ja-ṇa-ta-ma-bha-sa-somāḥ sutāsaḥ; gha-somāsaḥ sutāsaḥ gha; la-somāsaḥ sutāsaḥ |

^mahān yuddhakarmaṇi balavān ^ugraḥ śatrūṇāṃ bhayaṃkaraḥ ^indraḥ ^vīryāya vīryakaraṇāya ^vāvṛdhe vardhate | ^vṛṣabhaḥ phalasyābhivarṣakaḥ ^bhagaḥ sarvairbhajanīyaḥ saḥ ^indraḥ ^kāvyena kavayaḥ stotāraḥ | tatkarma stotram | tena stotreṇa ^samāca̭ke saṃgacchate | tādṛśasya ^asya^ indrasya ^gāvaḥ ^vājadāḥ kṣīrādilakṣaṇānnapradāḥ ^pra ^jāyante | tathā ^asya ^pūrvīḥ bahvastā gāvaḥ ^dakṣiṇāḥ prajāyante || ^vāvṛdhe | vardhateriti rūpam | nighātaḥ | vīryāya | vīre bhavaṃ vīryam | ' bhave chandasi ' iti yat | ' vīravīryāṃ ca ' ityatra ' yato'nāvaḥ ' ityasyānityatvasya jñāpitatvāt ttisvaritaḥ | samāca̭ke | karoterliṭ rūpam | ' gatirgatau ' iti pūrvapadasya^ gaternighātaḥ | kāvyena | kaveḥ karma kāvyam | brāhmaṇā- ditvāt vyaṣ | | jiratvā dupadhāvṛddhiḥ | jirasvaraḥ | vājadāḥ | dadāteḥ ' āto'nupasarge kaḥ ' | jāyante | ' janī prādurbhāve ' ityasya laṭi rūpam | dakṣiṇāḥ | ' dakṣa vṛddhau ' ityasmāt ' dudakṣabhyāminan ' itīnpratyayaḥ | niccādādyudāttaḥ || || 19 ||

he indrāvaruṇau ᵛyuvaṃ yuvāṃ ᵛpratnasya stotuḥ ᵛmahaḥ tādṛśaṃ śreyaḥ ᵛsādhathaḥ sādhayathaḥ |

atha caturthe'nuvāke pañcadaśa sūktāni|
tatra ‘ indraṃ matiḥ ’ iti navarcaṃ prathamaṃ sūktaṃ vaiśvāmitraṃ traiṣṭubhamaindram |
‘ indraṃ matirnava ’ ityanukrāntam |
sūktaviniyogo liṅgādavagantavyaḥ ||
indraṃ matirhṛda ā vacyamānācchā patiṃ stomataṣṭā jigāti | yā jāgṛvirvidathe śasyamānendra yatte jāyate viddhi tasya || 1 || indram | matiḥ | hṛdaḥ | ā | vacyamānā | accha | patim | stoma'taṣṭā | jigāti | yā | jāgṛviḥ | vidathe | śasyamānā | indra | yat | te | jāyate | viddhi | tasya || 1 ||
viśvāmitraḥ stauti |
he indra ᵥhṛdaḥ hṛdayāt ᵥvacyamānā ᵥstomataṣṭā stomakāribhiḥ kṛtā ᵥmatiḥ stutiḥ ᵥpatiṃ sarvasya jagataḥ svāminaṃ svām ᵥaccha abhilakṣya ᵥā ᵥjigāti samantāt gacchatu |
ᵥyā madīyā stutiḥ ᵥjāgṛviḥ stutyasya tava jāgaraṇa kurvāṇā ᵥvidathe yajñe ᵥśasyamānā bhavati |
he ᵥindra ᵥte tvadarthaṃ ᵥyat stotraṃ mattaḥ ᵥjāyate tat ᵥviddhi jānīhi |
ᵥtasya iti dvitīyārthe ṣaṣṭhī |
matiḥ |
‘ mantre vṛṣa ’ ityādinā ktinudāttaḥ |

divāścidā puṭhyā jāyamānā vi jāgṛvirvidathe śasyamānā | bhadrā vastrāṇyarjunā vasānā seyamusme saṃnajā pityā dhīḥ || 2 || divaḥ | cit | ā | puṭhyā | jāyamānā | vi | jāgṛviḥ | vidathe | śasyamānā | bhadrā | vastrāṇi | arjunā | vasānā | sā | iyam | asme iti | sam'jā | pityā | dhīḥ ||2||
1. ja-ṇa-pūrvavat vyākhyātā; na-vyākhyātā |
2. kha-ga-gha-ca-ja-ṇa-pa-pha-bha-tasya patim |
3. ca-pha-gacchati |
he indra 'divaśrit dyotamānāt sūryādapi 'pūrvyā pūrve bhavā upaḥkālasaṃbandhinī 'ā 'jāyamānā yā stutilakṣaṇā vāk vibhavati 'seyaṃ vāk 'vidathe yajñe 'śayamānā 'jāgṛtiḥ stutyasya tava jāgaraṇa kurvāṇā 'bhadrā kalyāṇāni 'arjunā śuddhavarṇāni 'caruāṇi yogyatayā tejāṃsi 'vasānā 'asme asmākaṃ 'pityā pitṛkramāgata|
ata eva 'sanajā purātanī satī 'dhīḥ tava stutirbhavati ||
divaḥ |
diveḥ kip |
'udidam ' iti vibhakteradāttatvam |

yamā | cit | atra | yama'sūḥ | asūta | jihvāyāḥ | agram | patat | ā | hi | asthāt | vapūṃṣi | jātā | mithunā | sacete iti | tamaḥ'hanā | tarpuṣaḥ | budhne | ā'itā || 3 ||
prasaṃgādupaḥkālaṃ praśaṃsati | 'yamasūḥ | yamau yamalau sūta iti yamasūrūpobhimāninī devatā | sā 'yamā yamalāśvinau 'atra upaḥkāle 'asūta | aśvinoratra śayamānatvāttāvaśvinau stotuṃ mama 'jihvāyāḥ 'agraṃ 'patat cañcalam 'ā 'asthāt 'hi tiṣṭhati khalu | 'tamohanā 'tamaso hantārau 'tapuṣaḥ | tapyasmin sūrye iti tapuḥ divasaḥ | tasya 'budhne mūle 'etā etāvāgatau 'mithunā parasparaṃ saṃgatau 'tāvaśvinau 'jātā utpannau 'vapūṃṣi | upyante nidhīyante iti vapūṃṣi upaḥkāle kriyamāṇāni śastrā- dīni karmāṇi | 'sacete saṃgacchataḥ || yamasūḥ | 'ṣaḍ prāṇigarbhavimocane ' ityasya kip | kṛduttarapadasvaraḥ | asūta | tasyaiva dhātorlaṅi adādivācchapo luk | nighātaḥ | patat | ' patalū gatau ' | śatṛsārva- dhātukasvare dhātusvaraḥ | asthāt | ' ṣṭhā gatinivṛttau ' | luṅi rūpam | hiyogādanighātaḥ | vapūṃṣi | ' ḍuvap bījasaṃtāne ' | ' artipavapiyajitanidhanitapibhyo nit ' iti usipratyayaḥ | ' nicca ' ityuktvādādyudāttaḥ | sacete | ' paca samavāye ' ityasya laṭi rūpam | tapuṣaḥ | ' tapa saṃtāpe ' | ' atipvapi ' ityādinā usiḥ | budhne | ' bandha bandhane ' | ' bandherdhividhudhī ca ' (u. sū. 3. 285 ) iti napratyayaḥ | dhividhudhī etāvādekau dhātoḥ | pratyayasvaraḥ | etā | ' iṇ gatau ' ityasya karmaṇi niṣṭhā | ' gatiranantaraḥ ' iti gateḥ svaraḥ ||
nakireṣāṃ ninditā martyeṣu ye asmākaṃ pitaro goṣu yodhāḥ |

nakiḥ | eṣām | ninditā | martyeṣu | ye | asmākam | pitaraḥ | goṣu | yodhāḥ | indraḥ | eṣām | duhitā | māhinā'vān | ut | gotrāṇi | sasṛje | daṃsanā'vān || 4 ||
1. kha-ga-ca-ja-ṇa-na-pha-bha-la-kalyāṇā | 2. gha-ja-ṇa-pitṛkramādagatā | 3. la-tamohanā nāma | 4. ta-deśaḥ | 5. na-la-parasparasaṃgatā | 6. la-ityasmāt |
he indra satre pravṛttānām ᵥasmākaṃ ᵥye ᵥpitaraḥ aṅgirasaḥ ᵥgoṣu paṇibhirapahṛteṣu tannimittaṃ ᵥyodhāḥ yoddhāro vartante teṣām ᵥeṣāṃ ᵥmartyeṣu ᵥninditā dūṣakaḥ ᵥnakiḥ na kaścidasti |
kuta ityata āha |
ᵥmāhināvān mahimopetaḥ ᵥdaṃsanāvān vṛtrahananādikarmavān ᵥindraḥ ᵥeṣām aṅgirasāṃ ᵥhaṃhitā haṃhitāni samṛddhāni ᵥgotrāṇi gavāṃ vṛndāni ᵥut ᵥsasṛje tebhyo'ṅgirobhyo dadau||

ᵥsakhibhiḥ samānasthānaiḥ ᵥsatvabhiḥ indramabhitaḥ sīdadbhiḥ ᵥnavagvaiḥ |
medhātithiprabhṛtayo'ṅgirasaḥ kecicca māsān satramanuṣṭhāya phalaṃ lebhire keciddaśa māsānanuṣṭhāyeti |
tatra ye nava māsān satra- manuṣṭhāya labdhaphalā udatiṣṭhan te navagvāḥ |
taiḥ satvabhiḥ aṅgirobhiḥ ᵥsakhā samānasthānaḥ ᵥindraḥ ᵥyatra yasmin bile paṇibhiniruddhāḥ ᵥgāḥ abhilakṣya ᵥabhijñu abhigatajānukaṃ yathā bhavati tathā ᵥajugman anugacchan ᵥindraḥ ᵥtat tatra bile ᵥdaśagvaiḥ |
ye daśa māsān satramanuṣṭhāya labdhaphalā udatiṣṭhan te daśagvāḥ |
ᵥdaśabhiḥ daśasaṃkhyākairṛṣibhiḥ sahitaḥ san ᵥtamasi bilavartinyandhakāre ᵥkṣiyantaṃ nivasantaṃ ᵥsatyaṃ yathārthaprakāśaṃ ᵥsūrya ᵥviveda lebhe |

imā | brahma̭ | brahma'vāhaḥ | kriyantai | ā | bahi̭ḥ | sīda | vīhi | śūra | puroḻāśam ||3||
٧brahmavāhaḥ |
٧brahmāṇi ٧stotrāṇi stutyatayā vahatīti brahmavāhā brahmaṇā stotreṇa prāpyate iti vā |
he stutya ٧indra tubhyam ٧imā imāni ٧brahma brahmāṇi stotrāṇi asmābhiḥ ٧kriyante |
ataḥ ٧bahi̭ḥ ٧ā ٧sīda stutyatayā bahi̭ṣi yajñe upaviśa |
he ٧śūra samarthendra asmābhīyamānamimaṃ ٧puroḻāśaṃ puroḻāśākhyaṃ haviḥ ٧vīhi bhakṣaya ||
brahmavāhaḥ |
‘ vaha prāpaṇe ’ ityasmāt ‘ vahidhāñyacchandasi ’ ityasun |
‘ ṇit ’ ityanuvṛtterupadhāvṛddhiḥ |
āmantritatvātnighātaḥ |
kriyante |
karoteḥ karmaṇi yak |
‘ riṅ śayagliṅkṣu ’ iti riḍādeśaḥ |
pratyayasvaraḥ |
pādāditvādanighātaḥ |
bahiḥ |
kālādhvanoratyanta- saṃyoge dvitīyā |
vīhi |
vī kāntyādiṣu |
loṭi rūpam |
herṅittvādudāttaḥ |
puroḻāśam |
‘ dāśva dāne ’ ityasmāt puraḥpūrvāt ‘ mantre śvetavokthaśapuroḻāśo ṇvin ’ ( pā. sū. 3. 2. 71 ) |
puro dāśanta enamiti |
nipātādakārasya lakāraḥ |
kṛduttarapadasvaraḥ ||
1. ga-paraspare saṃbaddham; ja-ṇa-parasparaṃ saṃbaddham |
2. kha-ga-ca-ja-ṇa-bha-sateṣu stīrṇam |
3. ja-ṇa-viśaraṇe |
4. na-la-sa-brahmāṇi; pha-bha-brahmavāhā brahmāṇi |
5. kha-ga-ca-na-pha-bha-stutya; la-brahmavāha indra !
rarandhi sarvaneṣu ṇa eṣu stomeṣu vṛtrahan | uktheṣvindra girvaṇaḥ || 4 || rarandhi | sarvaneṣu | naḥ | eṣu | stomeṣu | vṛtra'han | uktheṣu | indra | girvaṇaḥ || 4 ||
he girvaṇaḥ gīrbhiḥ stutibhirvandanīya vṛtrahan vṛtrasya hanter indra naḥ asmākaṃ sambandhiṣu triṣu savaneṣu eṣu kriyamāṇeṣu stomeṣu stotreṣu uktheṣu śastreṣu ca rarandhi ramasva ||
rarandhi |
ramaterloṭi 'bahulaṃ chandasi' iti śapaḥ śluḥ |
'chandasyubhayathā' iti herdhadhātutvenāṅiṅatvāt |
'aditaśca' iti herdhiḥ |
savaneṣu |
sūyate somo yeṣviti adhikaraṇe sunoteryuṭ |
litsvaraḥ |
ṇaḥ |
naso nakārasya 'naśca dhātusthorupabhyaḥ' iti saṃhitāyāṃ ṇatvam ||
matayaḥ somapāmuruṃ rihanti śarvasaspatim | indraṃ vansaṃ na mātaraḥ || 5 || matayaḥ | soma'pām | urum | rihanti | śarvasaḥ | patim | indram | vatsam | na | mātaraḥ ||5||
he indra matayaḥ asmābhiḥ kriyamāṇāḥ stutayaḥ uruṃ mahāntaṃ somapāṃ somasya pātāraṃ śavasaḥ balasya patim indraṃ tvāṃ rihanti lihanti |
tatra dṛṣṭāntaḥ |
vatsaṃ na |
yathā vatsaṃ mātaraḥ dhenavo lihanti tadvat ||
matayaḥ |
'mana jñāne' ityasmāt karmaṇi 'mantra vṛpa' ityādinā ktin udāttaḥ |
urum |
'ūrṇuñ ācchādane' ityasmānmahyarthe gamyamāne 'mahati hmaśca' (u. sū. 1. 32) iti kupratyayo dhātorhrasvaḥ |
pratyayasvaraḥ |
rihanti |
'liha āsvādane' ityasya laṭi rūpam |
ralayorabhedaḥ |
pratyayasvaraḥ |
śarvasaspatimiti saṃhitāyāṃ 'ṣaṣṭhyāḥ patiputrapṛṣṭha' ityādinā 'visarjanīyasya satvam |
vatsam |
'vada vyaktāyāṃ vāci' |
asmāt 'vṛtṛdahina' ityādinā sapratyayaḥ |
vadatyabhīkṣṇaṃ mātaramiti vatso gobālaḥ |
pratyayasvaraḥ ||
3 ||
sa mandasvā hyandhaso rādhase tanvā mahe | na stotāraṃ nide karaḥ || 6 || saḥ | mandasva | hi | andhasaḥ | rādhase | tanvā | mahe | na | stotāram | nide | karaḥ ||6||
he indra saḥ tathāvidhastvaṃ mahe mahate rādhase dhanāya andhasaḥ asmābhirdīyamānasya somasya pānena tanvā śarīreṇa saha mandasva hṛṣṭo bhava |
hi atra samuccayārthaḥ |
kiṃca tvaṃ stotāraṃ māṃ nide nindāyai viṣayabhūtaṃ na karaḥ mā kārṣīḥ ||
mandasva |
madi stutyādiṣu ityasya loṭi rūpam |
'hi ca' iti nighātapratiṣedhe bhavitavyamiti cenna |
tatra 'atrātilomye' iti vartate |
atra heḥ samuccayārthatvātrighātaḥ |
tanvā |
'udāttasvaritayoḥ' iti svaritatvam |
mahe |
takāralopaśchāndasaḥ |
'brahmahatopasaṃsyānam' iti vibhakterudāttatvam |
nide |
'ṇidi kutsāyām' ityasmāt saṃpadādilakṣaṇe bhāve kip |
āgamānuśāsanasyānityatvātnumabhāvaḥ |
'sāvekācaḥ' iti vibhakterudāttatvam |
karaḥ |
karoterluṅi caleḥ 'kamṛdaruhibhyaśchandasi' ityaḍādeśaḥ |
nighātaḥ ||
1. kha-ga-ca-na-bha-sa- 'triṣu' nāsti | 2. kha-ga-ca-na-bha-sa- 'śastreṣu' nāsti | 3. kha-ga-ca-ta-bha-sa-rarandhi | 4. kha-ga-ja-ṇa-ta-na-cet | kra. 2-50
va॒yāmi̭ndra tvā॒yavaṷ ha॒viṣma̭nto jarāmahe | u॒ta tva̭ma॒smayṷrva॒so || 7 || va॒yam | i॒ndra | tvā॒'yava̭: | ha॒viṣma̭ntaḥ | ja॒rā॒ma॒he | u॒ta | tvam | a॒smayṷ: | va॒so iti̭ ||7||
he indra tvāyavaḥ yajñārthaṃ tvāmātmānaṃ icchantaḥ tvākāmāḥ santaḥ haviṣmantaḥ saṃbhṛta- haviṣkāḥ vayaṃ jarāmahe tvāṃ stumaḥ |
he vaso rurvasya vāsaka indra uta tvam api asmayuḥ haviḥsvīkaraṇārtham asmānātmānaṃ icchan bhava ||
tvāyavaḥ |
tvāmicchantītyarthe ' rūpa ātmanaḥ kyac ' |
' pratyayottarapadayośca ' iti maparyantasya tvādeśaḥ |
' vayācchandasi ' ityupapratyayaḥ |
tvayava iti prāptau ' yuṣmadasmadoranādeśe ' ityavibhaktyapi halādau vyatyayenātvam |
pratyayasvaraḥ |
haviṣmantaḥ |
' tasau matvarthe ' iti bhasaṃjñāyāṃ sakārasya ratvābhāvaḥ |
' svaravidhau vyañjanama- vidyamānavat ' iti paribhāṣānāśrayaṇāt matupa udāttatābhāvaḥ |
isipratyayasvaraḥ |
jarāmahe |
' jaratiḥ stutikarmā ' iti yāskaḥ |
asmayuḥ |
asmānicchantītyarthe rūpaḥ kyac |
tatrāha- pratyayaḥ |
pūrvapadāntalopaścāndasaḥ |
pratyayasvaraḥ ||
māre a॒smadvi mṷmu॒co ha̭ripriyā॒rvāṅ yā̭hi | i॒ndra sva॒dhāvo̭ ma॒tsvaiha || 8 || mā | ā॒re | a॒smat | vi | mu॒mu॒caḥ | ha॒ri'pri॒ya | a॒rvāṅ | yā॒hi | i॒ndra | sva॒dhāvaḥ | ma॒tsva | i॒ha || 8 ||
he haripriya harī aśvau priyau yasya tādṛśa he indra asmat arusaḥ āre dūre sthe ye ji- tāvaśvau mā vi mumucaḥ mā vimocaya |
tārthādupeta eva arvāṅ asmadābhimukhyena yāhi āgaccha |
he svadhāvaḥ somalakṣaṇasvavan indra iha asmin devayajane makhaṃ somaṃ pītvā hṛṣṭo bhava ||
asmat |
asmacchabdasya pañcamībahuvacanasya ' pañcamyā ata ' ( pā. sū. 7. 1. 31 ) ityādeśaḥ |
mumucaḥ |
' muc mokṣaṇe ' ityasya pyantasya chāndase luṅi vahi rūpam |
abhyāsasya dīrghābhāvaśchāndasaḥ |
' na mā'yoge ' ityadabhāvaḥ |
nighātaḥ |
haripriya |
āmantritasya pādā- ditvāt pākṣikamādyudāttatvam |
masva |

āmantritasyāvidyamānatvena pādāditvādanighātaḥ ||
a॒rvāñca̭ tvā su॒khe rathḙ va॒hatā̭mindra ke॒śinā̭ | ghṛ॒tasnū ba॒rhirā॒sadḙ || 9 || a॒rvāñca̭m | tvā | su॒khe | rathḙ | va॒hatā̭m | i॒ndra | ke॒śinā̭ | ghṛ॒tasnū iti̭ ghṛ॒ta'snū | ba॒rhiḥ | ā॒sadḙ || 9 ||
1. ca-pha-āgaccha |
āgatya iha asmin yajñe svadhāvaḥ he svadhāvan indra matsva mandasva |
. ja-savanīyapuroḍāśādirūpamannaṃ tadan |
he ᵥindra ᵥghṛtasnū śramajanitajalaprasravaṇayuktau ᵥkeśinā keśinau skandhapradeśe lasamānakeśa- yuktau harī ᵥāsade āsadanīyaṃ ᵥbarhiḥ abhilakṣya ᵥsukhe sukhakare ᵥrathe ᵥarvāñcam asmadabhimukhaṃ ᵥtvā tvāṃ ᵥvahatām |

4 ||
‘upa naḥ sutam’ iti navacaṃ caturthaṃ sūktaṃ vaiśāmitraṃ gāyatramaindram |
‘upa naḥ’ ityanu kramaṇikā |
pūrvasūktena sahokto viniyogaḥ |
upa naḥ sutamā gahi somamindra gavāśiram |
haribhyāṃ yastai asmayuḥ ||
1||
upa | naḥ | sutam | ā | gahi | somam | indra | go'āśiram | hari'bhyām | yaḥ | te | asmayuḥ || 1 ||
he ᵥindra ᵥnaḥ asmadīyaṃ ᵥsutam abhiputaṃ ᵥgavāśiraṃ payasā miśritaṃ ᵥsomaṃ prati ᵥupa ᵥā ᵥgahi samīpa āgaccha |
yataḥ ᵥharibhyām aśvābhyāṃ yuktaḥ ᵥte tava ᵥrathaḥ ᵥasmayuḥ asmān kāmayamāno vartate |
gavāśiram |
āṅpūrvasya śrīṇāteḥ kipi ‘apaspṛdhamānācca’ (pā. sū. 6. 1. 36) ityādinā śira ityādeśaḥ |
bahuvrīhau pūrvapadasvaraḥ |
tamindra madamā gahi barhiḥṣṭhāṃ grāvabhiḥ sutam |
kuvittvasya tṛṣṇavaḥ ||
2||
tam | indra | madam | ā | gahi | barhiḥ'sthām | grāvabhiḥ | sutam | kuvit | nu | asya | tṛṣṇavaḥ || 2 ||
he ᵥindra ᵥgrāvabhiḥ ᵥsutam abhiputaṃ ᵥbarhiḥṣṭhāṃ barhiṣi sthitaṃ ᵥmadam | mādyate aneneti madaḥ somaḥ | ᵥtam imaṃ ᵥsomam ᵥā ᵥgahi āgaccha | ᵥkuvit prabhūtaṃ yathā bhavati tathā ᵥasya somasya pānena ᵥnu kṣipraṃ ᵥtṛṣṇavaḥ tṛpto bhava || madam | ‘madī harṣe’ | ‘mado'nupasarge’ ityappratyayaḥ | tasya pittvādanudāttatve dhātusvaraḥ | barhiḥṣṭhām | ‘ṣṭhā gatinivṛttau’ | kvip | saṃhitāyāṃ pūrvapada- sakārasya ‘ādeśapratyayayoḥ’ iti ṣatvam | ṣutvam | kṛduttarapadasvaraḥ | | tṛṣṇavaḥ | ‘tṛpa prīṇane’ ityasya levyaḍāgamaḥ | kuvityogādanighātaḥ | kṣubhādiṣu ‘nṛmana tṛṇoti’ iti paṭhitam | tathāpi sarvavidhīnāṃ chandasi vikalpitatvādatra ṇatvapratiṣedho na bhavati | pratyayasvaraḥ ||
indramitthā giro mamācchāguriṣitā itaḥ |
āvṛte somapītaye ||
3||
indram | itthā | giraḥ | mama | accha | aguriṣitāḥ | itaḥ | ā'vṛte | soma'pītaye || 3 ||
1. la-āsādanīyam | 2. na-la-sa-tava; pa-yaḥ te tava | 3. na-la-sa-madyate | 4. kha-ga-ja-ṇa-bha- somamimam; gha-taṃ madaṃ somam; la-sa-somamimaṃ somam | 5. na-sa-pāne | 6. ca-yadyapi kṣubhādiṣu |
he indra ᐞipitāḥ ᐞtvadarthaṃ preritāḥ ᐞitthā itthamena prakāreṇa ucyamānāḥ ᐞmama ᐞgiraḥ stuti- lakṣaṇā vācaḥ ᐞindraṃ tvām ᐞitaḥ asmādevajanadeśāt ᐞacchāguḥ ābhimukhyena gacchantu |
kimartham |

ᐞstomaḥ trivṛtpañcadaśādistomasādhyaiḥ stotraiḥ ᐞuvathebhiḥ uvathaiḥ ihaśca ᐞiha karmaṇi ᐞsomasya ᐞpītaye somapānārtham ᐞindraṃ ᐞhavāmahe vayamāhvayāmaḥ |

āgaman |
gamelaṃṭyaḍāgamaḥ |

kuvidyogādanighātaḥ |
indra somāḥ sutā ime tāndadhiṣva śatakrato | jaṭharai vājinīvaso || 5 || indra | somāḥ | sutāḥ | ime | tān | dadhiṣva | śatakrato iti śata'krato | jaṭharai | vājinīvaso iti vājinī'vaso || 5 ||
he ᐞśatakrato ᐞindra ᐞime ᐞsomāḥ ᐞsutāḥ prāvabhisrvadarthamabhiputāḥ | he ᐞvājinīvaso annadhanaᐞ | yadvā | vājo'nnamāsu phalatvenāstīti vājinyaḥ kriyāḥ | tāsāṃ2 vāsaka he indra ᐞtān somasān ᐞjaṭhare ᐞdadhiṣva dhāraya || vājinīvaso | vājaśabdāt matvarthe ‘ata iniṭhanau’ iti inipratyayaḥ | ‘ṛṣebhyo dīp’ iti dīp | tāsāṃ vaso | ‘saṃbuddhau ca’ iti guṇaḥ | āmantritatvādādya- mikamanudāttatvam || || 5 ||
vigrā hi tvā dhanaṃjayaṃ vājeṣu dadhṛṣaṃ kave | ardhā te subhramīmahe || 6 || vigrā | hi | tvā | dhana'jayam | vājeṣu | dadhṛṣam | kave | ardhā | te | subhram | īmahe ||6||

dhanaṃ jayatīti dhanaṃjayaḥ |

imam | indra | go'āśiram | yava'āśiram | ca | naḥ | piba | ā'gatya | vṛṣabhaḥ | sutam || 7 ||
he indra gavāśiraṃ gavyamiśraṇopetaṃ tathā yavāśiraṃ yavamiśraṇopetaṃ vṛṣabhaḥ prāvabhiḥ sutam abhiputaṃ naḥ asmadīyam imaṃ somaṃ devayajanam āgatya piba ||
gavāśiraṃ yavāśiramityatra āṅpūrvasya śrīṇāteḥ kipi 'apaspṛdheyāmānucuḥ' iyādināśira ityādeśaḥ |

8 ||
tubhya | it | indra | sve | okye | somam | codāmi | pītayai | eṣaḥ | rarantu | te | hṛdi || 8 ||
he indra tubhyaṃ tubhyameva pītaye pānārthaṃ sve svakīye okye sthāne jaṭhare'bhiputamamimaṃ somaṃ codāmi prerayāmi | eṣaḥ pītaḥ somaḥ te tava hṛdi hṛdaye rarantu asyarthaṃ ramatām || tubhya | yuṣmacchabdasya caturthyekavacane 'tubhyamahau' iti tumyādeśaḥ | 'supāṃ suluk' iti supo luk | 'ṅici ca' ityādyudāttaḥ | okye | vasvāditvāt svādhiko yatpratyayaḥ | nisvaritaḥ | codāmi | 'cuda preraṇe' | pyantasya laṭi rūpam | 'bahulamanyatrāpi' iti ṇerluk | nighātaḥ | rarantu | 'ramu krīḍāyām' ityasya yaṅluki loṭi sarvavidhīnāṃ chandasi vikalpitatvāt atrābhyāsasya numabhāvaḥ | saṃhitāyām 'anyepāmāpi' ityabhyāsasya dīrghaḥ | nighātaḥ | hṛdi | hṛdayaśabdasya 'padañjomāstan' ityādinā hvādeśaḥ | 'ūḍidam' iti vibhakterudāttatvam ||
tvāṃ sutasya pītayai pratnamindra havāmahe |
kuśikāso avasyavaḥ ||

tvām | sutasya | pītayai | pratnam | indra | havāmahe | kuśikāsaḥ | avasyavaḥ || 9 ||
he indra avasyavaḥ tvatto rakṣaṇamicchantaḥ kuśikāsaḥ kuśikagotrotpannā vayaṃ sutasya prāvabhirabhiputasya somasya pītaye pānārthaṃ pratnaṃ purātanaṃ tvāṃ havāmahe stutilakṣaṇābhirgīrbhiḥ ||
1. gha-la-gavyamiśraṇopetaṃ somam | 2. kha-ga-ca-ja-ṇa-na-bha-sa- 'yavāśiraṃ ' nāsti | 3. ja-ṇa- rarantu | 4. ja-ṇa-rarantu |
āhvayāmaḥ || pīnaye | 'sthāgāpāpaco bhāve' iti kin | 'hiṣṭvatsaratiśathāntānām' (ki. sū. 7) ityantodāttatvam | hvāmahe | hvayaterleṭi 'bahula̭ chandasi' iti saṃprasāraṇe rūpam | avasyavaḥ | ava icchantītyarthe supaḥ kyac | 'kyācchandasi' ityupratyayaḥ | kuśikāso avasyavaḥ ityatra saṃhitāyām 'adhyādavacān' (pā. sū. 6. 1. 116) ityādinā eḍaḥ prakṛtibhāvaḥ || 6 ||

3. na-sahaprīta: |
he indra ᵥvṛpaṇā secanasamarthā ᵥsudhurā śobhanayā dhurānvitau ᵥsvaśvā śobhanāvayavopetau ᵥsakhāyā śobhanasyānau ᵥetā etau ᵥharī ᵥtvām ᵥā ᵥvahātaḥ? yajñaṃ pratyāgamanaya rathe svāmāvahataḥ|
atha ᵥdhānāvata bhṛtyavopetaṃ ᵥsavanaṃ ᵥjupāṇaḥ sevamānaḥ ᵥsakhā sakhibhūtaḥ saḥ ᵥindraḥ ᵥsabhyuḥ stoturmama ᵥvandanāni stotrāṇi ᵥśrṛṇvan ᵥca ᵥśrṛṇuyāt ||
vahātaḥ|
vaherleṭyāḍāgame rūpam|
‘cavāyoge prathamā’ iti na nighātaḥ|
śrṛṇvan|

nighātaḥ||
kuvinmāṃ gopāṃ karase janasya kuvidrājānaṃ maghavanṛjīṣin | kuvinm ṛṣīṃ papivāṃsaṃ sutasya kuvinme vastrau amṛtasya śikṣāḥ || 5 || kuvit | mā | gopām | karase | janasya | kuvit | rājānam | maghavan | ṛjīṣin | kuvit | ṛṣīm | papivāṃsam | sutasya | kuvit | me | vasvaḥ | amṛtasya | śikṣāḥ ||5||
he indra ᵥkuvit api ca ᵥmā māṃ ᵥjanasya ᵥgopāṃ goptāraṃ ᵥkarase kuru|

he ᵥindra ᵥtvā tvāṃ ᵥbṛhantaḥ mahāntaḥ ᵥyujānāḥ rathe yujyamānāḥ ᵥharayaḥ aśvāḥ ᵥsadhamādaḥ saha mādayantaḥ santaḥ ᵥarvāk asmadabhimukham ᵥā vahantu |
ᵥvṛṣabhasya kāmānāṃ varṣiturindrasya ᵥmūrāḥ śatrūṇāṃ mārakāḥ ᵥsusaṃsṛṣṭāsaḥ indrasya hastābhyāṃ pṛṣṭhabhāge suṣṭhu saṃspṛṣṭāḥ ᵥdivaḥ nabhasaḥ sakāśādāgacchantaḥ ᵥye aśvāḥ ᵥātāḥ |

cisvādantodāttaḥ | sadhamādaḥ | ‘madī harṣe’ ityasmādaṇyantāt kvip | kṛduttarapadasvaraḥ | dvitā | dviśabdāt ‘saṃkhyāyā vidhārthe dhā’ iti dhāpratyayaḥ | dhakārasya takāraśchāndasaḥ | pratyayasvaraḥ | divaḥ | ‘uḍidam’ iti vibhakterudāttatvam | ātāḥ | ‘ata sātatyagamane’ ityasmāt karmaṇi ghañ | vyatyayenādyudāttaḥ ||
indra̭ piba॒ vṛṣa̭dhūtasya॒ vṛṣṇa̭ ā॒ yaṃ tḙ śyena॒ uśa̭te jabhāra̭ | yasya̭ ma॒de॒ cyā̭vayasi॒ pra kṛ॒ṣṭīryasya̭ ma॒de॒ arpa̭ go॒trā va̭vardha || 7 || indra̭ | piba̭ | vṛṣa̭dhūtasya | vṛṣṇa̭: | ā | yam | tḙ | śyena̭: | uśa̭te | jabhāra̭ | yasya̭ | ma॒de॒ | vya̭vayasi | pra | kṛ॒ṣṭīḥ | yasya̭ | ma॒de॒ | arpa̭ | go॒trā | va̭vardha || 7 ||
he indra vṛṣadhūtasya prāvabhirabhiputaṃ vṛṣṇaḥ abhimataphalasecakaṃ somaṃ piba | śyenaḥ śaṃsanīyagatiḥ chandorūpaḥ suparṇaḥ uśate somaṃ kāmayamānāya te tubhyaṃ yaṃ somam ā jabhāra ājahāra | yasya somasya made harṣe saṃjāte sati kṛṣṭīḥ ākarṣanti vaśīkurvanti karmaṇā sarvān iti kṛṣṭayo manuṣyāḥ | tān sapatnabhūtān manuṣyān pra cyāvayasi prakarṣeṇa pātayasi | yasya somasya made saṃjāte sati gotrā | gāmudakaṃ raśmibhirā vṛtaṃ varṣavatsu trāyante pālayantīti gotrā meghāḥ | tān apa vavardha apāvṛṇopi || vṛṣadhūtasya | ‘dhṛñ kampane’ ityasya niṣṭhāyāṃ ‘yasya vibhāṣā’ itīṭ-pratiṣedhaḥ | ‘tṛtīyā karmaṇi’ iti pūrvapadasvaraḥ | śyenaḥ | ‘syaiṅ gatau’ | ‘śāstyāhapravibhya imac’ (u. sū. 2. 204) itīnpratyayaḥ | śyāyate iti śyenaḥ | citvādantodāttaḥ | jabhāra | haraterliṭi ṇali rūpam | ‘hagrahorbhaśchandasi’ iti bhakāraḥ | yadvattayogādanighātaḥ | lisvaraḥ | made | ‘madī harṣe’ | ‘maḍo'nupasarge’ iśyapratyayaḥ | tasya piśrādanudāttatvaṃ dhātusvaraḥ | gotrā | ‘gai pālane’ ityasya ‘āto'nupasarge kaḥ’ iti kaḥ | supoḍaḍādeśaḥ | ekādeśasvaraḥ | vavardha | ‘vṛñ varaṇe’ ityasya liṭi thali ‘ṛto bhāradvājasya’ itīṭpratiṣedhaḥ | guṇaḥ | lisvaraḥ ||
śuna̭ṃ huvema ma॒dhavā̭namindra̭ma॒sminbha̭re nṛ॒tama̭ṃ vā॒rjasā̭tau | śṛ॒ṇvanta̭mu॒gramū॒tayḙ sa॒matsṷ ghna॒ntaṃ vṛ॒trāṇi̭ sa॒ñjita̭ṃ dha॒nānā̭m || 8 || śunam | huvema | maghavānam | indram | asmin | bhare | nṛtamam | vājasātau | śṛṇvantam | ugram | ūtaye | samatsu | ghnantam | vṛtrāṇi | sañjitam | dhanānām || 8 ||

‘ayaṃ te astu’ iti pañcamaṃ ṣaṣṭhaṃ sūktaṃ vaiśvāmitraṃ bārhatamaindram |
atrānukramaṇikā— ‘ayaṃ te pañca bārhataṃ tu’ iti |
mahāvrate bārhatyāṃ tṛcāśītāvidaṃ sūktam (ai. ā. 5. 2. 4) |
ṣoḍaśiśastre
1. kha-ga-ca-ṇa-bha-śaṃsanīyaṃ gacchatīti | 2. kha-ga-ca-bha-saṃjñāte | 3. kha-ga-ca-ja-ṇa-karmāṇi sarvāṇi | 4. ta-na-sa-saṃpannabhūtān | 5. kha-ga-ca-ja-ṇa-na-pha-bha-varṣeṣu; ta-varṣāvyatirikte'tṛptu | 6. ja-ṇa-iti pūrvavata; sa-iti gatam | ṛ 2-51
‘ ayaṃ te astu haryataḥ ’ haryācastṛco viniyuktaḥ |
sūtritaṃ ca—‘ ayaṃ te astu haryata ityauṣṇiha- bārhato tṛcau ’ ( āśva. śrau. 6. 2 ) iti |
ayaṃ te astu haryataḥ soma ā haribhiḥ sutaḥ | juṣāṇa indra haribhir ā guhā tiṣṭha haritaṃ ratham || 1 || ayam | te | astu | haryataḥ | somaḥ | ā | hari'bhiḥ | sutaḥ | juṣāṇaḥ | indra | hari'bhiḥ | naḥ | ā | guha | tiṣṭha | haritam | ratham || 1 ||
he ‘indra ‘haribhiḥ’ |
haranti somarasānebhiḥ iti harayo grāvāṇaḥ |
taiḥ ‘ā ‘sutaḥ’ sarvato'bhidutaḥ ‘haryataḥ kamanīyaḥ ‘juṣāṇaḥ prītiviṣayaḥ ‘ayaṃ ‘somaḥ ‘te tubhyam ‘astu |
tvaṃ ‘haribhiḥ aśvairyuktaṃ ‘haritaṃ haridvarṇaṃ ‘ratham ‘ā tiṣṭha adhitiṣṭha |
tataḥ ‘naḥ asmānabhilakṣya ‘ā gahi āgaccha |
haryataḥ |
‘ harya gatikāntyoḥ ’ ityas
so'yam ᵥindro ᵥhariyāyasam | harito haritavarṇā dhāyaso dhārakā raśmayā yasyāḥ sā | tāṃ ᵥdhām ᵥadhārayat | tathā ᵥharivarpasam oṣadhibhirharitavarṇāṃ ᵥpṛthivīm adhārayat | ᵥharitaḥ haridvarṇayoḥ | ᵥyayoḥ ᵥdyāvāpṛthivyormadhye svakīyayoraśvayoḥ ᵥbhūriprabhūtaṃ ᵥbhojanam | bhujyata iti bhojanaṃ yavasādi | labhyate | yayośca ᵥdyāvāpṛthivyoḥ ᵥantaḥ madhye ᵥhariḥ indraḥ ᵥcarat carati | te dyāvā- pṛthivyāvindraḥ adhārayat' adhārayat | || hariyāyasam | 'ḍudhāñ dhāraṇapoṣaṇayoḥ' | ityasmāt 'vahilā- dhābhyaścandasi' dṛśyasun | 'jin' ityanuvṛtteḥ āto yuk | bahuvrīhau pūrvapadasvaraḥ | pṛthivīm | 'pratheḥ pivan saṃprasāraṇaṃ ca' iti pivanpratyayaḥ | 'pitrādibhyaśca' iti ṅīp | pratyayasvareṇāntodāttaḥ | harivarpasam | 'vṛṅ saṃbhaktau' | 'vṛṅśībhyāṃ rūpasañjñayoḥ' | ityasunpratyayaḥ pugāgamaśca | saṃbhajyate taditi ᵥvapo rūpam | bahuvrīhau pūrvapadasvaraḥ | haritaḥ | 'hañ haraṇe' ityasmāt 'hyuruhiyuṣibhya itiḥ' (u. sū. 1. 97) iti itipratyayaḥ | pratyayasvaraḥ | bhojanam | 'bhuja pālanābhya- vahārayoḥ' ityasmāt 'kṛtyalyuṭo bahulam' iti karmaṇi lyuṭ | litsvaraḥ | antaḥ | svarādiṣvanto- dāttatvena paṭhitatvādantodāttaḥ | carat | caratirgatyarthaḥ | leṭi rūpam | yattayogādanighātaḥ ||

jajñānaḥ | haritaḥ | vṛṣā | viśvam | ā | bhāti | rocanam | hari'aśvaḥ | haritam | dhatte | āyudham | ā | vajram | bāhvoḥ | harim || 4 ||
ᵥvṛṣā kāmānāṃ varṣakaḥ ᵥharitaḥ haridvarṇopetaḥ ᵥjajñānaḥ jāta indraḥ ᵥviśvaṃ sarvaṃ ᵥrocanaṃ dīpyamānaṃ lokam ᵥā ᵥbhāti sarvataḥ prakāśayati |
tathā ᵥharyaśvaḥ indraḥ ᵥharitaṃ haridvarṇopetam ᵥāyudhaṃ ᵥdhatte bāhvordhatte |
tathā ᵥhariṃ śatrūṇāṃ prāṇāpahārakaṃ ᵥvajraṃ ᵥbāhvoḥ ᵥā dhatte ||

varṣaka he indra ᵥadhvaryavaḥ adhvaraṃ yajamānāyecchantaḥ ᵥte svadatha ᵥtaṃ somaṃ ᵥhinvanti prerayanti | kiṃca ᵥte tava ᵥpātavai pānāya ᵥtaṃ somaṃ ᵥmṛjanti śodhayanti | ᵥu prasiddhau | pṛthivīdyāvā | ‘ divo dyāvā ’ iti dyāvādeśaḥ | āyudāttaḥ | ‘ nottarapade'nudāttādāvapṛthivīrudrapūṣamanthiṣu ’ ityatra paryudāsāt ‘ devatādvandve ca ’ ityubhayapadaprakṛtisvaraḥ | kṛtasamāsasya viparyāschāndasaḥ | | bibhrataḥ | | ‘ dubhran dhāraṇapoṣaṇayoḥ ’ ityasmālluṭi ‘ bhṛjāmin ’ ityabhyāsasyetvam | yadvattayogādanighātaḥ | ajādipratyayaparatvābhāvātra ‘ abhyastānāmādiḥ ’ iti svaro na bhavati | pratyayasvaraḥ | | tvāyā | tvāmicchatītyarthe ‘ supa ātmanaḥ kyac ’ | pūrvapadadakārasyākāraśchāndasaḥ | | a pratyayāt ’ iti bhāve'pratyayaḥ | | ‘ supāṃ suluk ’ iti tṛtīyāyāḥ savarṇadīrghaḥ | ekādeśasvaraḥ | | adhvaryavaḥ | adhvara- micchantaḥ | supaḥ kyac | | kyadhvarapṛtanasyarci lopaḥ ’ ityakāralopaḥ | | kyācchandasi ’ ityupapratyayaḥ | pratyayasvaraḥ | | pātavai | | pā pāne ’ | tumarthe ’ tavaipratyayaḥ | | antaśca tavai yugapat ’ iti dhātvantasya pratyayasya codāttatvam | | ujaḥ ’ iti pragṛhyasaṃjñā || || 10 ||
‘ marutvān ’ iti pañcamaṃ navamaṃ sūktaṃ vaiśvāmitraṃ traiṣṭubhamaindram |
‘ marutvān ’ ityanukrama- ṇikā |
daśarātre caturthe'hani marutvatīyaśastre etat sūktaṃ ‘ nividdhānīyam ’ |
sūtritaṃ ca—‘ śrudhī havamindra marutvān ’ indrati marutvatīyam ’ ( āśva. śrau. 7. 11 ) iti |
ṛṣabhanāmyaikāhe'pi marutvatīyaśastre etanni vidvānam |

maruvā~ indra vṛṣabho raṇāya pibā somaṃmanuṣvādhaṃ madāya |
ā siñcasva jaṭhare madhva ūrmiṃ tvaṃ rājāsi pradivaḥ sutānām ||
1 ||
marutvān | indra | vṛṣabhaḥ | raṇāya | piva | somam | anu'svadham | madāya | ā | siñcasva | jaṭhare | madhvaḥ | ūrmim | tvam | rājā | asi | pradivaḥ | sutānām || 1 ||
he ᵥindra yaḥ ᵥvṛṣabhaḥ apāṃ varṣitā ᵥmarutvān |
maruto devaviśaḥ tadvān |
sa tvam ᵥanuṣvādhaṃ svadhayā savanīyapuroḍāśādirūpeṇa ᵥagnenānugataṃ ᵥsomaṃ ᵥraṇāya ramaṇīyasaṃgrāmārthaṃ ᵥmadāya taṃ somaṃ ᵥpiba |
kiṃca ᵥmadhvaḥ madakarasya somasya ᵥūrmiṃ saṃghātaṃ bahurasaṃ ᵥjaṭhare |

śaṃsā mahāmindraṃ yasminviśvā ā kṛṣṭayaḥ somapāḥ kāmamavyan | yaṃ sukratuṃ dhiṣaṇe vivvataṣṭaṃ ghnaṃ vṛtrāṇī janayanta devāḥ || 1 || śaṃsa | mahām | indram | yasmin | viśvāḥ | ā | kṛṣṭayaḥ | soma'pāḥ | kāmam | avyan | yam | su'kratum | dhiṣaṇe iti | vi'vva'taṣṭam | ghnam | vṛtrāṇām | janayanta | devāḥ ||1||
he hota: 'mahāṃ mahāntam 'indraṃ 'śaṃsa śastraiḥ stuhi | 'yasmin indre rakṣitari sati 'viśvāḥ 'kṛṣṭayaḥ sarve manuṣyāḥ 'somapāḥ yajñamanuṣṭhāya somapāḥ santaḥ 'kāmam | kāmyate iti kāmaḥ svargādiḥ | tam 'ā 'avyan sarvataḥ prāpnuvanti | 'dhiṣaṇe devamanuṣyādīnāṃ dhārayitryau | yadvā | pragalbhe samarthe svāśritān rakṣitumiti dhiṣaṇe dyāvāpṛthivyau | 'devāḥ ca 'sukratum | kratuḥ' karme jagatpālanādi | śobhanaḥ kraturyasyāsau sukratuḥ | tādṛśaṃ 'vivvataṣṭaṃ vibhunā brahmaṇā taṣṭaṃ jagadādhipatye sthāpitaṃ mahāntaṃ 'vṛtrāṇāṃ pāparūpāṇāṃ 'ghnaṃ hantāraṃ 'yam indraṃ 'janayanta ajanayan || śaṃsa | 'śansu stutau' ityasya loṭi rūpam | pādāditvādanudāttaḥ | 'aco'tastiṅaḥ ' iti saṃhitāyāṃ dīrghaḥ | avyan | vī' kāntigatyādiṣu' | laṅi rūpam | yadvṛttayogādanighātaḥ | adasvaraḥ | sukratum | 'katvādyaśca' ityuttarapādādyudāttaḥ | dhiṣaṇe | 'dhiṣaṇā prāgalbhye' ityasmāt 'dhredharṣa ca saṃjñāyām' iti kyupratyayaḥ | dhiṣa ityadeśaḥ | pratyayasvaraḥ | ghnam | hanteḥ 'puṃsi saṃjñāyām' iti ghaḥ | 'ho hanteḥ' iti kutvam | pratyayasvaraḥ | janayanta | janayerdhantasya laṅi rūpam | yadvṛttayogādanighātaḥ ||
yaṃ nu nākiḥ pṛtanāsu svarājaṃ dvitā taraṃti nṛtamaṃ hariṣṭhām | inatamaḥ satvabhiryo ha bhūṣaiḥ pṛthujryā aminādāyurdasyoḥ || 2 || yam | nu | nākiḥ | pṛtanāsu | sva'rājam | dvitā | tarati | nṛtamam | hari'sthām | ina'tamaḥ | satva'bhiḥ | yaḥ | ha | śubhaiḥ | pṛthu'jryāḥ | amināt | āyuḥ | dasyoḥ ||2||
'pṛtanāsu |
vyāpriyante'tra yoddhāra iti pṛtanāḥ saṃgrāmāḥ |
teṣu 'svarājaṃ 'hariṣṭhāṃ haristhāṃ hariyukte' rathe' vartamānaṃ 'nṛtamaṃ balasya yuddhe netṛtvaṃ 'dvitā yuddhamāsādya senāṃ dvidhā kṛtvā vartamānaṃ 'yam indraṃ 'nākiḥ na kaścidapi 'tarati uttīrya gacchati |
na ko'pyavyasti' ityarthaḥ |
'nu prasiddhau |
sa indraḥ 'inatamaḥ atiśayena senāsvāmī 'satvabhiḥ paritaḥ sīdadbhirudraiḥ saha 'yaḥ |
yān gacchan 'śubhaiḥ |
śoṣayanti parabalābhīti śṛpāṇi balāni |
taiḥ 'pṛthujryāḥ |
yuddhaṃ prati tīvravegaḥ san 'dasyoḥ śatroḥ 'āyuḥ annaṃ prāṇaṃ vā 'amināt saṃgrāmeṣu mināti hinasti' |
'ha prasiddhau |
atra niruktaṃ--
1. kha-ga-gha-ca-ja-ṇa-pha-bha-śobhanakratum |
2. na-sa- 'taṣṭaṃ' nāsti |
3. kha-ga-ca-ja-ṇa-na-bha- sa- 'vī kāntigatyādiṣu' nāsti |
4. kha-ga-ca-ja-ṇa-hariyukterathe |
5. kha-ga-ca-ja-ṇa-ta-na-pha-bha-la- sa-ko'pyasti; gha-pa-ko'pi |
6. kha-ga-gha-ca-ja-ṇa-na-bha-la-sa- 'saha' nāsti |
7. kha-ga-na-bha- sa-mināti hinasti; gha-amināt saṃgrāmeṣu hinasti; ca-la-amināt hinasti |
' pṛthujyāḥ pṛthujavaḥ | pṛthujyā aminādāyurdesyoḥ | prāmāpayadāyurdesyoḥ ' ( niru. 5. 9 ) iti || tarati | ' tṛ plavanataraṇayoḥ ' ityasya laṭi rūpam | yadvṛttayogānnighātaḥ | jhapaḥ pittvādanudāttatve dhātusvaraḥ | yaḥ | ' yā prāpaṇe ' | auṇādikaḥ kapratyayaḥ | āto lopaḥ | pratyayasvaraḥ ||
mahāvā̭ pṛ॒tsu ta॒ra॒ṇirvā̭ vyā॒na॒śī roda̭sī me॒hanā̭vān |
bha॒go na kā॒re ha॒vyo ma̭tī॒nāṃ pi॒teva̭ cā॒ruḥ su॒harvo̭ va॒yodhā̭ḥ ||
3 ||
suharvā | pṛtsu | taraṇiḥ | na | arvā | viḍaānaśiḥ | rodasī iti | mehanā'vān | bhagaḥ | na | kāre | havyaḥ | matīnām | pitā'iva | cāruḥ | su'harvaḥ | vayaḥ'dhāḥ || 3 ||
ya indraḥ 'sahāvā' balavān 'pṛtsu saṃgrāmeṣu 'taraṇiḥ
^niyuvān sahāyavān |
yadvā netyayamuttareṇāpi saṃbadhyate |
yathā vāyurniyuvān niyuktāmakavaḍvābhiḥ tadvān evamindro vasubhirmarudbhiḥ sahāyavān |
tathā ^kṣapā ^varatā rātrimācchādayan ^sūryasya jagatprakāśakasyādityasya ^janitā janayitā ^bhāgaṃ bhajanīyaṃ ^vājaṃ karmaphalarūpamanna ^vibhaktā sādhu vibhāgakartā bhavati |

5 ||
śunam | huvema | maghavānam | indram | asmin | bhare | nṛtamam | vāja'sātau | bhṛvantam | ugram | ūtaye | samat'su | ghnatam | vṛtrāṇi | sam'jitam | dhanānām ||5|| ‘ śunaṃ huvema ’ iti pūrvavat || || 13 ||
‘ indraḥ svāhā ’ iti pañcacaṃ dvādaśaṃ sūktaṃ vaiśvāmitraṃ traiṣṭubhamaindram |
daśarātre navame'hani maruvatīyaśastre idaṃ sūktam |
sūtritaṃ ca- ‘ tṛtīyayyendraḥ svāhā gāyatrasāma ’ (āśva. śrau. 8. 7) iti ||
indraḥ svāhāṃ pibatu yasya somaṃ āgatyā tugro vṛṣabho marutvān |
uruvyacāḥ |
pṛṇatāmebhirairāsya havistanvaḥ |
kāmaṃmṛdhyāḥ ||
1 ||
indraḥ | svāhā | pibatu | yasya | somaḥ | ā'gatya | tugraḥ | vṛṣabhaḥ | marutvān | ā | uru'vyacāḥ | pṛṇatām | ebhiḥ | annaiḥ | ā | asya | haviḥ | tanvaḥ | kāmam | ṛdhyāḥ ||1||
ayam ^indraḥ ^svāhā svāhākṛtamimaṃ somaṃ ^pibatu |
^yasya indrasyāyaṃ ^somaḥ sa indro yajñaṃ prati ^āgatya ^tugraḥ karmavighnakāriṇāṃ hiṃsakaḥ ^vṛṣabhaḥ yaṣṭaṇāmabhimataphalavarṣakaḥ ^marutvān marunmisradvān ^uruvyacāḥ prabhūtavyāptiḥ san ^ebhiḥ asmābhirdattaiḥ ^annaiḥ somādilakṣaṇairhavibhiḥ ^ā ^pṛṇatāṃ samantāt parayatu |
tataḥ ^asya indrasya saṃbandhana ^tanvaḥ śarīrasya ^kāmaṃ pṛṣṭhādi- lakṣaṇaviṣayamabhilāṣaṃ ^haviḥ asmābhirdattam ^ā ^ṛdhyāḥ sarvataḥ pūrayatu ||

tumirāha- nanāthaiḥ |

ṛdhyateḥ āśīrliṅi rūpam ||
1. kha-evaṃ pūrvaṃ vyākhyātā; sa-siddham |
ṛ. 2-5, 3
ā teṃ saparyū juvase yunajmi yayoranuṃ pradivaḥ śruṣṭimārvaḥ |
iha tvā dheyurharayaḥ suśiprā pibā tvasya suputasya cāroḥ ||
2 ||
ā | te | saparyū iti | juvase | yunajmi | yayoḥ | anu | pradivaḥ | śruṣṭim | āvaḥ |
iha | tvā | dheyuḥ | harayaḥ | suśiprā | piba | tu | asya | suputasya | cāroḥ || 2 ||
he indra 'te tava 'javase yajña pratyāgamanāya 'saparyū paricārakau 'harī 'ā 'yunajmi rathe yojayāmi |
'pradivaḥ purātanasthaṃ 'yayoḥ aśvayoḥ 'śruṣṭiṃ vegam |
'śruṣṭīti kṣipranāma ' iti yāskaḥ |
tam 'anu 'āvaḥ anugacchasi |
he 'suśiprā śobhanahano indra 'harayaḥ rathe yojitāste'śvāḥ 'iha karmaṇi 'tvā tvāṃ 'dheyuḥ yajñaṃ prati dhārayantu |
tata āgatasthaṃ 'suputasya suṣṭhubhiputaṃ 'cāroḥ kamanīyam 'asya imaṃ somaṃ 'tu kṣipraṃ 'piba |
saparyūśabdaḥ kaṇvādiḥ |

3 ||

'gṛṇānāḥ stotraṃ kurvāṇā ṛtvijaḥ 'mimiksuṃ stotṝṇāmabhimaṃtaphalaṃ varṣitumicchantaṃ 'supāraṃ śobhanapāram |

he 'ṛjīṣin somavannindra sa tvaṃ 'mandānaḥ modamānaḥ 'somaṃ 'papidvān somasya pātā san stotṛbhyaḥ 'asmabhyaṃ 'purudhā bahudhā 'gāḥ agnihotrādikarmasiddhayarthaṃ dhenūḥ 'sam 'iṣaṇya samyak preraya ||

'varṣaṇīṣṭatam' iti dvādarcaṃ trayodaśaṃ sūktaṃ vaiśvāmitramaindram |
atrānukramaṇikā—'varṣaṇīṣṭataṃ dvādaśādhānyau tucau jāgatagāyatrau' iti |
ādyastṛco jāgato'nyastṛco gāyatraḥ |
śiṣṭā ṛcastriṣṭubhaḥ |
sūktaviniyogo laikṣikaḥ |
ukte kratau maitrāvaruṇaśastre'nupapannānantaramādyastṛcaḥ śaṃsanīyaḥ |
sūtritaṃ ca—'varṣaṇīṣṭatamastakjhāyamasura iti tucau' (āśva.śrau. 6. 1) iti |
vipuvati nikevalye 'nṛṇāmu tvā' iti tṛcaḥ |
'viṣuvān' iti khaṇḍe sūtritaṃ—'nṛṇāmu tvā nṛtamaṃ gīrbhirṛthairiti tisraḥ' (āśva. śrau. 8. 6) iti |
bṛhaspatisave apyayaṃ tṛco nikevalye nividdhānīyaḥ |
sūtritaṃ ca—'nṛṇāmu tvā' iti madhyandinaḥ' (āśva. śrau. 9. 5) iti |
mahāvrate'pi nikevalye'yaṃ tṛcaḥ |
daśarātre caturthe'hani marutvatīye 'indra marutvaḥ' iti tisraḥ |
ṣaṣṭhe'hani 'paṣṭhasya' iti khaṇḍe sūtritam—'indra marutva iti tisra iti marutvatīyam' (āśva.śrau. 8. 1) iti |
bṛhaspatisave'pi marutvatīyaśastre'yaṃ tṛco nivi- ddhānīyaḥ |
sūtritaṃ ca—'indra marutva iha nṛṇāmu tveti madhyandinaḥ' (āśva. śrau. 9. 5) iti |
ādyā marutvatīyagrahasyānuvākyā |
sūtritaṃ ca—'marutvatīyena praheṇa carantīndra marutva iha pāhi somam' (āśva. śrau. 5. 14) iti |
tṛtīye paryāye hotṛḥ śastre 'idaṃ hyanvojasā' iti stotriyastṛcaḥ |
sūtritaṃ ca—'idaṃ hyanvojasā mahāṃ indro ya ojasā' (āśva. śrau. 6. 4) iti ||
1. kha-ga-ja-ta-na-ma-la-sa-iṣu | 2. kha-ga-ja-evāyam; ca-cāyam | 3. kha-ga-ca-ja-ṇa-na- bha-sa-mādhyandinaḥ | 4. ga-ca-ja-ṇa-marutvatīyagṛhasya śastre | 5. kha-ga-ca-ja-na-bha-sa-mādhyandinaḥ |
karṣaṇīdhṛtaṃ maghavānamukthyamindraṃ girau bṛhatīrabhyanūṣata |
vāvṛdhānaṃ puruhūtaṃ suvṛktibhiramatyaṃ jaramāṇaṃ divedive ||
1 ||
karṣaṇī'dhṛtam | magha'vānam | ukthyam | indram | giraḥ | bṛhatīḥ | abhi | anūṣata | vavṛdhānam | puru'hutam | suvṛkti'bhiḥ | amartyam | jaramāṇam | dive'dive || 1 ||
vbṛhatīḥ prabhṛtāḥ vgiraḥ asmadīyāḥ stutilakṣaṇā vācaḥ vkarṣaṇīdhṛtaṃ karṣaṇīnāṃ manuṣyāṇāmabhi- mataphalapradānena dhārakaṃ poṣakam |
yadvā |
ākṛṣyanyanena sarvamiti karṣaṇi balaṃ taddhārakam |

kurmaḥ| tathā ca mantravarṇaḥ- 'grasetāmakṣā vi muceha śoṇā divedive sadṛśīrdhi dhānāḥ' (ṛ.saṃ. 3.35.3) iti | marudbhiḥ sagaṇaḥ tvam appaṃ puroḍāśam adbhi bhakṣaya | he śūra asahāya he indra vṛtrahā vṛtrasya hantā vidvān sarvaṃ jānānastvaṃ somaṃ piba || pūṇavate | pūpaśabdasya matup | 'ano nud' iti juḍāgamaḥ | 'rapābhyām' iti ṇatvam | cakram | karoteśchandase vartamāne liṭi krādiśvāt iḍabhāvaḥ | nighātaḥ | harivate | 'chandasīraḥ' iti matupo vatvam ||
prati dhānā bharata tūryamasmai puroḻāśaṃ vīratamāya nṛṇām | divedive sadṛśīrindra tubhyaṃ vardhantu tvā somapeyāya dhṛṣṇo || 8 || prati | dhānāḥ | bharata | tūryam | asmai | puroḻāśam | vīratamāya | nṛṇām | divedive | sadṛśīḥ | indra | tubhyam | vardhantu | tvā | somapeyāya | dhṛṣṇo iti || 8 ||
yajamāno'dhvarvādīn prati brūte |

atha pratyakṣakṛtaḥ |

'indrāparvatā' iti caturviṃśatyṛcaṃ pañcadaśaṃ sūktam |
atreyamantramaṇikā- 'indrāparvatā caturviṃśatirādhendrāpārvatī pañcadaśyādidve vāce sasarpyaṃ catasro rathāṅgatuto'nyā abhiśāpāstā vasiṣṭhe'piṇyo na vasiṣṭhāḥ śṛṇvanti |
daśamīpoḍaśyau jagatyau trayodaśī gāyatrī dvādaśīviṃśīdrā- viṃśayo'nuṣṭubho'ṣṭādaśī bṛhatī' iti |
viśvāmitra ṛṣiḥ |
daśamī poḍaśī ca dve jagatyau trayodaśī gāyatrī dvādaśīviṃśīdrāviṃśayastisro'nuṣṭubho'ṣṭādaśī bṛhatī śiṣṭāstriṣṭubhaḥ |
āghyendrāparvatadevatākā pañcadaśīpoḍaśyau vāgdevatye taduttarāśatamau rathāṅgedevatākāḥ śiṣṭā aindrayaḥ ||
indrāparvatā bṛhatā rathena vāmīriṣa ā vahataṃ suvīrāḥ | vītaṃ havyānyadhvareṣu devā vardhethāṃ gīrbhirīḻayā madantā || 1 || indrāparvatā | bṛhatā | rathena | vāmīḥ | iṣaḥ | ā | vahatam | suvīrāḥ | vītam | havyāni | adhvareṣu | devā | vardhethām | gīrbhiḥ | īḻayā | madantā || 1 ||
indrāparvatā indraśca parvataśca |
he indrāparvatau bṛhatā rathena āgatya vāmīḥ vananīyāḥ suvīrāḥ śobhanaputropetāḥ iṣaḥ annāni ā vahatam asmadarthaṃ dhārayatam |
prayacchatamityarthaḥ |
1. ca-ja-pūpaśabdāt | 2. ja-ṇa-krādiniyamāt | 3. kha-ga-caṃ-ja-ṇa-na-bha-sa- 'prati' nāsti | 4. kha-ga-na-bha-sa-tvām; ca-la-tvā |

1. kha-ga-ca-pha-ityākārakaḥ | 2. kha-ga-ca-ja-ṇa-vadasva | 3. kha-ga-na-bha-sa-'karavāva' nāsti | 4. kha-ga-ja-ṇa-bha-sa-'śastaṃ' nāsti | 5. kha-ga-ca-ja-ṇa-na-bha-sa-maghavan dhanavan | 6. la-eva yatra te jāyā tiṣṭhati |
he maghavan dhanavan indra tvaṃ parā yāhi asmādevajanāt parāṅmukho gṛhaṃ prati gaccha | athavā devayajanaṃ prati ā yāhi | he bhrātaḥ poṣaka ubhayatra api te tava artham | ayaṃte prāpta ityarthaḥ | prayojanam | asti tatra jāyā anna somaḥ | prayojanāntaramāha | yatra gṛhaṃ prati gamane bṛhataḥ mahataḥ rathasya upari nidhānam avasthānaṃ prayojanam | atra sthitau rāsabhasya haṣāravaṃ kurvataḥ vājinaḥ aśvasya rathāt vimocanaṃ prayojanam || artham | ṛ gatau | upikupitārthasya iti than | nisvaraḥ | nidhānam | dadhāterbhāve hyuṭ | liti iti pratyayātpūrvasyodāttatvam | rāsabhasya | rāsu śabde | rāsivalbhyāṃ ca (u. sū. 3. 405) iti kartari abhacapratyayaḥ | prāmādivādādyudāttaḥ || || 19 ||

he indra somam apāḥ | atraiva sthitvā somaṃ piba | athavā astaṃ gṛhaṃ pra yāhi prakarṣeṇa gaccha | te tava gṛhe kalyāṇīḥ maṅgalakāriṇī jāyā śacī vidyate | suraṇaṃ śobhanagītādi- dhvaniścāsti | yadvā | suraṇaṃ suramaṇīyaṃ yathā bhavati tathā gṛhe jāyā tiṣṭhati | yatra ityyādi gatam | dakṣiṇāvat | atra sthitau satyāṃ rathe yojitānāṃ vājināṃ vimocanaṃ khādanapānādinā dakṣiṇāvat prayo- janavadbhavati | vimuktebhyo vājibhyaḥ khādanaṃ pānaṃ copaharanti || apāḥ | pā pāne | luṅi gātisthā iti sico luk | aḍāgamasvaraḥ | kalyāṇīḥ | kalyāṇamasyāstīti matvarthe chandasīvannipau iti īpratyayaḥ | pratyayasvaraḥ | suraṇam | raṇa śabdārthaḥ | asmādakṛcchrārthe khal | litsvaraḥ | pratyayasya viśvādanu- dāttatve dhātusvaraḥ | samāse kṛduttarapadasvaraḥ ||

1 ||
pra | me | vivikrān | avidat | manīṣām | dhenum | carantīm | pra'yutām | agopām | madyaḥ | cit | yā | duduhe | bhūri | dhāseḥ | indraḥ | tat | agniḥ | panitāraḥ | asyāḥ || 1 ||

ᵛmitra: bhagavānāditya: ᵛdeveṣu dyotamānādiguṇayukteṣu ᵛāyuṣu manuṣyeṣu madhye yo jano vṛktabarhi: |
